local guiEffects = {
	NoAnimation = getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true,
	TweenService = TweenService,
	CleanupElapsed = 0,
	MatrixNames = {
		CMatrix = true,
		LogoMatrix = true,
		MatrixBackground = true,
		MatrixColumn = true,
		MatrixDigit = true,
		MatrixStream = true,
		ButtonMatrixStream = true,
	},
	SuppressedMatrixObjects = {},
}

function guiEffects.HideMatrix()
	for _, instance in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
		if guiEffects.MatrixNames[instance.Name] and instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
			if guiEffects.SuppressedMatrixObjects[instance] == nil then
				guiEffects.SuppressedMatrixObjects[instance] = instance.Visible
			end
			instance.Visible = false
		end
	end
end

function guiEffects.RestoreMatrix()
	for instance, wasVisible in pairs(guiEffects.SuppressedMatrixObjects) do
		if instance.Parent then
			instance.Visible = wasVisible
		end
	end
	table.clear(guiEffects.SuppressedMatrixObjects)
end

function guiEffects.CreateTween(instance, tweenInfo, properties)
	if getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true then
		tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0, tweenInfo.EasingStyle, tweenInfo.EasingDirection)
	end
	return guiEffects.TweenService:Create(instance, tweenInfo, properties)
end

local function track(connection)
	table.insert(connections, connection)
	return connection
end

local mainFrameScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
mainFrameScale.Name = "ViewportScale"
mainFrameScale.Parent = mainFrame

local function updateMainFrameScale()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
    local scaleX = math.max(0.1, (viewport.X - 16) / 440)
    local scaleY = math.max(0.1, (viewport.Y - 16) / 390)
    mainFrameScale.Scale = math.min(1, scaleX, scaleY)
    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
end

updateMainFrameScale()
local function bindViewportScale(camera)
    if camera then
        track(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateMainFrameScale))
    end
end
bindViewportScale(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
track(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    updateMainFrameScale()
    bindViewportScale(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
end))

local function createHorizontalOpenController(frame)
	local expandedSize = frame.Size
	local collapsedSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, expandedSize.Y.Scale, expandedSize.Y.Offset)
	local state = {
		Open = false,
		Tween = nil,
	}
	frame.Size = collapsedSize

	local function setOpen(open)
		if state.Open == open then return end
		state.Open = open
		if state.Tween then
			state.Tween:Cancel()
		end
		if open then
			frame.Visible = true
		end

		local tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
			frame,
			TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, open and Enum.EasingDirection.Out or Enum.EasingDirection.In),
			{ Size = open and expandedSize or collapsedSize }
		)
		state.Tween = tween
		tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
			if state.Tween ~= tween or state.Open ~= open or playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
				return
			end
			state.Tween = nil
			if not open then
				frame.Visible = false
			end
		end)
		tween:Play()
	end

	return setOpen, state
end

local setMainFrameOpen, mainFrameAnimation = createHorizontalOpenController(mainFrame)

local function round(instance, radius)
	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
	corner.Parent = instance
	return corner
end

local function applyGlass(instance, transparency)
	instance.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
	instance.BorderSizePixel = 0

	local stroke = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	if stroke then stroke:Destroy() end
end

	local function showInfo()
		if not infoHoverEnabled then
			info.Visible = true
			info.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(149, 56)
			return
		end
		info.Visible = true
		info.Position = infoHoverPosition
		if infoTween then infoTween:Cancel() end
		infoTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(info, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(149, 56),
		})
		infoTween:Play()
	end

	local hideInfo
	local function setInfoHoverEnabled(enabled)
		infoHoverEnabled = enabled
		if infoTween then infoTween:Cancel() end
		if enabled then
			info.Position = infoHoverPosition
			hideInfo()
		else
			info.Visible = true
			infoTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
				info,
				TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{
					Position = infoHoverPosition,
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(149, 56),
				}
			)
			infoTween:Play()
		end
	end

	local function updateInfoMatrix(deltaTime)
		if not info.Visible then return end
		local width = info.AbsoluteSize.X
		local height = info.AbsoluteSize.Y
		local cornerRadius = 5
		for _, stream in ipairs(infoMatrixStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)

			for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
				local x = stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset + digit.Position.X.Offset + digit.AbsoluteSize.X * 0.5
				local y = stream.Y + digit.Position.Y.Offset + digit.AbsoluteSize.Y * 0.5
				local inside = x >= 0 and x <= width and y >= 0 and y <= height
				if inside and x < cornerRadius and y < cornerRadius then
					inside = (x - cornerRadius) ^ 2 + (y - cornerRadius) ^ 2 <= cornerRadius ^ 2
				elseif inside and x > width - cornerRadius and y < cornerRadius then
					inside = (x - (width - cornerRadius)) ^ 2 + (y - cornerRadius) ^ 2 <= cornerRadius ^ 2
				elseif inside and x < cornerRadius and y > height - cornerRadius then
					inside = (x - cornerRadius) ^ 2 + (y - (height - cornerRadius)) ^ 2 <= cornerRadius ^ 2
				elseif inside and x > width - cornerRadius and y > height - cornerRadius then
					inside = (x - (width - cornerRadius)) ^ 2 + (y - (height - cornerRadius)) ^ 2 <= cornerRadius ^ 2
				end
				digit.Visible = inside
			end

			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.2 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
					infoMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > height then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
				stream.Speed = infoMatrixRandom:NextNumber(12, 22)
				for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
					digit.Text = infoMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				end
			end
		end
	end

	hideInfo = function()
		if not infoHoverEnabled then return end
		task.delay(0.08, function()
			if not infoHoverEnabled then return end
			local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			local function contains(guiObject)
				local position = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
				local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize
				return mouse.X >= position.X and mouse.X <= position.X + size.X
					and mouse.Y >= position.Y and mouse.Y <= position.Y + size.Y
			end
			if contains(button) or (info.Visible and contains(info)) then return end
			if infoTween then infoTween:Cancel() end
			infoTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(info, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(149, 0),
			})
			local closingTween = infoTween
			closingTween.Completed:Connect(function()
				if infoTween == closingTween then
					info.Visible = false
				end
			end)
			closingTween:Play()
		end)
	end

	track(button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setMainFrameOpen(not mainFrameAnimation.Open)
	end))
	track(button.MouseEnter:Connect(showInfo))
	track(button.MouseLeave:Connect(hideInfo))
	track(info.MouseEnter:Connect(showInfo))
	track(info.MouseLeave:Connect(hideInfo))
	track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
		buttonRing.Rotation = (buttonRing.Rotation + deltaTime * 36) % 360
		updateInfoMatrix(deltaTime)
		for _, stream in ipairs(logoMatrixStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			local maskWidth = logoMatrix.AbsoluteSize.X
			local maskHeight = logoMatrix.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local centerX = maskWidth * 0.5
			local centerY = maskHeight * 0.5
			local safeRadius = math.min(maskWidth, maskHeight) * 0.5 - 5
			for digitIndex, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
				local digitCenterX = stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset + digit.Position.X.Offset + digit.AbsoluteSize.X * 0.5
				local digitCenterY = stream.Y + digit.Position.Y.Offset + digit.AbsoluteSize.Y * 0.5
				local offsetX = digitCenterX - centerX
				local offsetY = digitCenterY - centerY
				digit.Visible = safeRadius > 0
					and (offsetX * offsetX + offsetY * offsetY) <= safeRadius * safeRadius
			end
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.16 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = logoMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > logoMatrix.AbsoluteSize.Y then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
				stream.Speed = logoMatrixRandom:NextNumber(24, 42)
				for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
					digit.Text = logoMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		track(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
			if introCompleted then
				button.Position = getBottomLeftPosition()
			end
		end))
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local viewport = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
		button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(viewport.X * 0.5 - 38, viewport.Y * 0.5 - 36)
		introTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 72),
		})
		button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
		introTween:Play()
		introTween.Completed:Wait()
		if not launcherGui.Parent then return end
		task.wait(0.35)
		if not launcherGui.Parent then return end
		currentPosition = getBottomLeftPosition()
		introTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = currentPosition,
		})
		introTween:Play()
		introTween.Completed:Wait()
		if launcherGui.Parent then
			introCompleted = true
		end
	end)

	return button, {
		CancelIntro = function()
			if introTween then introTween:Cancel() end
		end,
		SetInfoHoverEnabled = setInfoHoverEnabled,
	}
end)()

local matrixRandom = Random.new()

local function randomMatrixDigit()
	return matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
end

for columnIndex = 1, 19 do

	column.Name = "MatrixStream"

		digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, matrixLineHeight)

	end

	table.insert(matrixColumns, {
		Frame = column,
		Digits = digits,
		Y = matrixRandom:NextNumber(-matrixCharacterCount * matrixLineHeight, 390),
		Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(38, 82),
		RefreshElapsed = 0,
		RefreshIndex = matrixRandom:NextInteger(1, matrixCharacterCount),
	})
end

-- Matrix plus seasonal/live particle backgrounds.
local visualThemeMode = getgenv().AaVisualTheme or "Matrix"
local seasonalLayer = Instance.new("Frame")

seasonalLayer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
seasonalLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
seasonalLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0

seasonalLayer.ZIndex = 1
seasonalLayer.Parent = mainFrame

local seasonalRandom = Random.new()
local seasonalParticles = {}
for index = 1, 28 do
    local particle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    particle.Name = "SeasonalParticle"
    particle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    particle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    particle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
    particle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    particle.TextSize = seasonalRandom:NextInteger(9, 15)
    particle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 225, 235)
    particle.TextTransparency = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(0.2, 0.65)
    particle.ZIndex = 1
    particle.Parent = seasonalLayer
    table.insert(seasonalParticles, {
        Object = particle,
        X = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(0, 440),
        Y = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(0, 390),
        Speed = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(10, 30),
        Drift = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(-7, 7),
    })
end

local function resolveVisualTheme(mode)
    if mode ~= "Live" then return mode end
    local month = os.date("*t").month
    if month == 12 or month <= 2 then return "Snow" end
    if month >= 6 and month <= 8 then return "Stars" end
    return "Particles"
end

local function setVisualTheme(mode)
    local allowed = { Matrix = true, Snow = true, Stars = true, Particles = true, Live = true }
    if not allowed[mode] then return end
    visualThemeMode = mode
    getgenv().AaVisualTheme = mode
    local activeMode = resolveVisualTheme(mode)
    seasonalLayer.Visible = activeMode ~= "Matrix" and not guiEffects.NoAnimation
    for _, stream in ipairs(matrixColumns) do
        stream.Frame.Visible = activeMode == "Matrix"
    end
    local glyph = activeMode == "Snow" and "❄"
        or activeMode == "Stars" and "✦"
        or activeMode == "Particles" and "·"
        or ""
    for _, record in ipairs(seasonalParticles) do
        record.Object.Text = glyph
    end
end

track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    if guiEffects.NoAnimation or not seasonalLayer.Visible then return end
    local width, height = seasonalLayer.AbsoluteSize.X, seasonalLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
    if width <= 0 or height <= 0 then return end
    for _, record in ipairs(seasonalParticles) do
        record.Y += record.Speed * deltaTime
        record.X += record.Drift * deltaTime
        if record.Y > height + 18 then
            record.Y = -18
            record.X = seasonalRandom:NextNumber(0, width)
        end
        if record.X < -18 then record.X = width + 18 end
        if record.X > width + 18 then record.X = -18 end
        record.Object.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(record.X, record.Y)
    end
end))

setVisualTheme(visualThemeMode)

connections.refreshMainMatrix = function()
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	for _, stream in ipairs(matrixColumns) do
		for digitIndex, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
			digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
			digit.TextTransparency = math.min(0.9, 0.18 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.085)
		end
	end
end

track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	if not mainFrame.Visible then return end

	local layerHeight = matrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
	for _, stream in ipairs(matrixColumns) do
		stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
		stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
		stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)

		if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.16 then
			stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
			stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = randomMatrixDigit()
			stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % matrixCharacterCount + 1
		end

		if stream.Y > layerHeight then
			stream.Y = -matrixCharacterCount * matrixLineHeight
			stream.Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(38, 82)
			for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
				digit.Text = randomMatrixDigit()
			end
		end
	end
end))

	{ Rotation = 42 }

local commandPaletteButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

commandPaletteButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
commandPaletteButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8)
commandPaletteButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.background
commandPaletteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
commandPaletteButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
commandPaletteButton.ClipsDescendants = true
commandPaletteButton.AutoButtonColor = false
commandPaletteButton.Text = ""

applyGlass(commandPaletteButton, 0.1)

local commandButtonState = {
	Scale = Instance.new("UIScale"),
	Hidden = getgenv().AaHideCommandButton == true,
	PaletteOpen = false,
	Random = Random.new(),
	MatrixStreams = {},
	VisibilityAnimation = { Token = 0, Tween = nil, TransparencyTween = nil },
}
commandButtonState.Scale.Scale = 1
commandButtonState.Scale.Parent = commandPaletteButton

function commandButtonState.SetHidden(hidden)
	commandButtonState.Hidden = hidden
	getgenv().AaHideCommandButton = hidden
	commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Token += 1
	local token = commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Token
	if commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Tween then
		commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Tween:Cancel()
	end
	if commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.TransparencyTween then
		commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.TransparencyTween:Cancel()
	end

	if commandButtonState.PaletteOpen then
		return
	end

	if hidden and not commandPaletteButton.Visible then
		commandButtonState.Scale.Scale = 0.01
		commandPaletteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		return
	end

	commandPaletteButton.Visible = true
	if not hidden then
		commandButtonState.Scale.Scale = 0.01
		commandPaletteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end
	commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandButtonState.Scale,
		TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, hidden and Enum.EasingDirection.In or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{ Scale = hidden and 0.01 or 1 }
	)
	commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.TransparencyTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandPaletteButton,
		TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, hidden and Enum.EasingDirection.In or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{ BackgroundTransparency = hidden and 1 or 0.1 }
	)
	track(commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
		if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed and token == commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Token then
			commandPaletteButton.Visible = not hidden
		end
	end))
	commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.Tween:Play()
	commandButtonState.VisibilityAnimation.TransparencyTween:Play()
end

if commandButtonState.Hidden then
	commandButtonState.SetHidden(true)
end

for columnIndex = 1, 10 do
	local stream = Instance.new("Frame")
	stream.Name = "ButtonMatrixStream"
	stream.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 36)
	stream.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4 + (columnIndex - 1) * 19, 0)
	stream.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	stream.BorderSizePixel = 0
	stream.ZIndex = 201
	stream.Parent = commandPaletteButton
	local digits = {}
	for digitIndex = 1, 3 do
		local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		digit.Name = "Digit"
		digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
		digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * 12)
		digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
		digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		digit.Text = commandButtonState.Random:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
		digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		digit.TextTransparency = 0.35 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.2
		digit.TextSize = 10
		digit.ZIndex = 201
		digit.Parent = stream
		digits[digitIndex] = digit
	end
	table.insert(commandButtonState.MatrixStreams, {

		Digits = digits,

		Speed = commandButtonState.Random:NextNumber(12, 24),
	})
end

track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	for _, stream in ipairs(commandButtonState.MatrixStreams) do
		stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
		if stream.Y >= commandPaletteButton.AbsoluteSize.Y then
			stream.Y = commandButtonState.Random:NextNumber(-36, -12)
			for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
				digit.Text = commandButtonState.Random:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
			end

	end
end))

commandButtonState.Text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
commandButtonState.Text.Name = "RunningText"
commandButtonState.Text.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 28)
commandButtonState.Text.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-180, 0)
commandButtonState.Text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
commandButtonState.Text.Text = ".;< )$28acze (k Cjs46"
commandButtonState.Text.TextColor3 = colors.text
commandButtonState.Text.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
commandButtonState.Text.TextSize = 10
commandButtonState.Text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
commandButtonState.Text.ZIndex = 202
commandButtonState.Text.Parent = commandPaletteButton
task.spawn(function()
	local originalText = "</> Commands By Drzzy"
	local randomCharacters = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789!@#$%^&*<>?/;:+"

	local function makeRandomText()
		local characters = {}
		for index = 1, #originalText do
			local originalCharacter = originalText:sub(index, index)
			if originalCharacter:match("%s") then
				characters[index] = originalCharacter
			else
				local randomIndex = commandButtonState.Random:NextInteger(1, #randomCharacters)
				characters[index] = randomCharacters:sub(randomIndex, randomIndex)
			end
		end
		return table.concat(characters)
	end

	local function moveTextTo(position, duration, randomizeDuringMove)
		local tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
			commandButtonState.Text,
			TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
			{ Position = position }
		)
		tween:Play()
		if randomizeDuringMove then
			while tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing
				and commandPaletteButton.Parent do
				commandButtonState.Text.Text = makeRandomText()
				task.wait(0.08)
			end
		else
			tween.Completed:Wait()
		end
		return tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed
	end

	while commandPaletteButton.Parent do
		if guiEffects.NoAnimation then
			commandButtonState.Text.Text = originalText
			commandButtonState.Text.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 0)
			task.wait(0.25)
		else
			commandButtonState.Text.Text = makeRandomText()
			commandButtonState.Text.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-180, 0)
			if not moveTextTo(UDim2.fromOffset(5, 0), 5, true) then break end
			local centerStartedAt = os.clock()
			local decodedCharacters = {}
			for index = 1, #originalText do
				decodedCharacters[index] = commandButtonState.Text.Text:sub(index, index)
			end
			for index = 1, #originalText do
				if not commandPaletteButton.Parent then break end
				decodedCharacters[index] = originalText:sub(index, index)
				commandButtonState.Text.Text = table.concat(decodedCharacters)
				task.wait(0.08)
			end

			local timeUntilScramble = 9 - (os.clock() - centerStartedAt)
			if timeUntilScramble > 0 then
				task.wait(timeUntilScramble)
			end
			if not commandPaletteButton.Parent then break end

			local scrambleInterval = 1 / #originalText
			for index = 1, #originalText do
				if not commandPaletteButton.Parent then break end
				if not decodedCharacters[index]:match("%s") then
					local randomIndex = commandButtonState.Random:NextInteger(1, #randomCharacters)
					decodedCharacters[index] = randomCharacters:sub(randomIndex, randomIndex)
				end
				commandButtonState.Text.Text = table.concat(decodedCharacters)
				task.wait(scrambleInterval)
			end

			if not commandPaletteButton.Parent then break end
			commandButtonState.Text.Text = table.concat(decodedCharacters)
			if not moveTextTo(UDim2.fromOffset(190, 0), 5) then break end
		end
	end
end)

track(commandPaletteButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if connections.openCommandPalette then
		connections.openCommandPalette()
	end
end))

local activePage = "Tools"
local sidebarHidden = false
local validTabNames = {
	Tools = true,
	Radar = true,
	Troll = true,
	Combat = true,
	Players = true,
	Explorer = true,
	Settings = true,
}
local favoriteTabNames = {}
local savedFavoriteTabNames = getgenv().AaFavoriteTabs
if type(savedFavoriteTabNames) == "table" then
	for tabName, isFavorite in pairs(savedFavoriteTabNames) do
		if validTabNames[tabName] and isFavorite == true then
			favoriteTabNames[tabName] = true
		end
	end
end
getgenv().AaFavoriteTabs = favoriteTabNames
local tabRecords = {}

local function updateTabOrder()
	local orderedTabs = table.clone(tabRecords)
	table.sort(orderedTabs, function(left, right)
		local leftFavorite = favoriteTabNames[left.Name] == true
		local rightFavorite = favoriteTabNames[right.Name] == true
		if leftFavorite ~= rightFavorite then
			return leftFavorite
		end
		return left.Index < right.Index
	end)

	for position, record in ipairs(orderedTabs) do
		local yOffset = 34 + ((position - 1) * 38)
		record.Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, yOffset)
		record.Button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 34)
		record.Icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sidebarHidden and 3 or 7, 5)
		record.Icon.Text = string.format("%02d", position)
		record.FavoriteButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, yOffset + 9)
		local isFavorite = favoriteTabNames[record.Name] == true
		record.FavoriteButton.Text = isFavorite and "★" or "☆"
		record.FavoriteButton.TextColor3 = isFavorite and colors.text or colors.muted
		record.FavoriteButton.Name = isFavorite and "Remove" .. record.Name .. "Favorite" or "Add" .. record.Name .. "Favorite"
	end
end

local function makeTab(text, index, glyph)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Name = text .. "Navigation"
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 34)
	button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 34 + ((index - 1) * 38))
	button.BackgroundColor3 = colors.sidebar
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Text = ""
	button.TextColor3 = colors.text
	button.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	button.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
	button.TextSize = 11
	button.Parent = sidebar
	round(button, 7)
	applyGlass(button, 0.38)

	local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	icon.Name = "Icon"
	icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 24)
	icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 5)
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.Text = glyph
	icon.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	icon.TextSize = 12
	icon.Parent = button

	local buttonLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	buttonLabel.Name = "Label"
	buttonLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
	buttonLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0)
	buttonLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	buttonLabel.Text = text
	buttonLabel.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	buttonLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	buttonLabel.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
	buttonLabel.TextSize = 11
	buttonLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	buttonLabel.Parent = button

	local favoriteButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	favoriteButton.Name = favoriteTabNames[text] and "Remove" .. text .. "Favorite" or "Add" .. text .. "Favorite"
	favoriteButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 16)
	favoriteButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34 + ((index - 1) * 38) + 9)
	favoriteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	favoriteButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	favoriteButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	favoriteButton.Text = favoriteTabNames[text] and "★" or "☆"
	favoriteButton.TextColor3 = favoriteTabNames[text] and colors.text or colors.muted
	favoriteButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	favoriteButton.TextSize = 14
	favoriteButton.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 1
	favoriteButton.Parent = sidebar

	track(favoriteButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if favoriteTabNames[text] then
			favoriteTabNames[text] = nil
		else
			favoriteTabNames[text] = true
		end
		getgenv().AaFavoriteTabs = favoriteTabNames
		updateTabOrder()
	end))

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if activePage ~= text then
			guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
				BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface,
			}):Play()
		end
	end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if activePage ~= text then
			guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
				BackgroundColor3 = colors.sidebar,
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	table.insert(tabRecords, {
		Name = text,
		Index = index,
		Button = button,
		Icon = icon,
		Label = buttonLabel,
		FavoriteButton = favoriteButton,
	})
	return button
end

local radarTab = makeTab("Radar", 2, "02")

local aimBotTab = makeTab("Combat", 4, "04")
local playersTab = makeTab("Players", 5, "05")
local explorerTab = makeTab("Explorer", 6, "06")
local settingsTab = makeTab("Settings", 7, "07")
updateTabOrder()

local pageFrames = { toolsPage, radarPage, trollPage, aimBotPage, playersPage, explorerPage, settingsPage }
local sidebarTweens = {}
local sidebarTransitionId = 0

local function updateContentLayout(animate)
	sidebarTransitionId += 1
	local transitionId = sidebarTransitionId
	for target, tween in pairs(sidebarTweens) do
		tween:Cancel()
		sidebarTweens[target] = nil
	end

	local sidebarWidth = sidebarHidden and 40 or 102
	local leftOffset = sidebarHidden and 59 or 121
	local widthOffset = sidebarHidden and -63 or -125
	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	local function apply(target, properties)
		if not animate then
			for property, value in pairs(properties) do
				target[property] = value
			end
			return
		end

		local tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(target, tweenInfo, properties)
		sidebarTweens[target] = tween
		tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
			if sidebarTransitionId ~= transitionId or sidebarTweens[target] ~= tween then
				return
			end
			sidebarTweens[target] = nil
			if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
				return
			end
			if target:IsA("GuiObject") and target:GetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween") then
				target.Visible = false
				target:SetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween", nil)
			end
		end)
		tween:Play()
	end

	apply(sidebar, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarWidth, 1, -58) })
	sidebar.Visible = true
	sidebarToggleButton.Text = sidebarHidden and ">" or "<"
	navCaption.Text = sidebarHidden and "M" or "MENU"
	navCaption.TextXAlignment = sidebarHidden and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	apply(navCaption, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sidebarHidden and 0 or 10, 10),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, sidebarHidden and 0 or -20, 0, 20),
	})
	updateTabOrder()
	for _, record in ipairs(tabRecords) do
		if animate then
			if sidebarHidden then
				record.Label.Visible = true
				record.FavoriteButton.Visible = true
				record.Label:SetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween", true)
				record.FavoriteButton:SetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween", true)
				apply(record.Label, { TextTransparency = 1 })
				apply(record.FavoriteButton, { TextTransparency = 1 })
			else
				record.Label.Visible = true
				record.FavoriteButton.Visible = true
				record.Label:SetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween", nil)
				record.FavoriteButton:SetAttribute("HideAfterSidebarTween", nil)
				record.Label.TextTransparency = 1
				record.FavoriteButton.TextTransparency = 1
				apply(record.Label, { TextTransparency = 0 })
				apply(record.FavoriteButton, { TextTransparency = 0 })
			end
		else
			record.Label.Visible = not sidebarHidden
			record.FavoriteButton.Visible = not sidebarHidden
			record.Label.TextTransparency = 0
			record.FavoriteButton.TextTransparency = 0
		end
		apply(record.Icon, {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sidebarHidden and 3 or 7, 5),
		})
	end

	for _, page in ipairs(pageFrames) do
		apply(page, {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(leftOffset, 51),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, widthOffset, 1, -58),
		})
	end
end

track(sidebarToggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	sidebarHidden = not sidebarHidden
	updateContentLayout(true)
end))

local function showPage(name)
	activePage = name
	toolsPage.Visible = name == "Tools"
	radarPage.Visible = name == "Radar"
	trollPage.Visible = name == "Troll"
	aimBotPage.Visible = name == "Combat"
	playersPage.Visible = name == "Players"
	explorerPage.Visible = name == "Explorer"
	settingsPage.Visible = name == "Settings"
	for tabName, tab in pairs({
		Tools = toolsTab,
		Radar = radarTab,
		Troll = trollTab,
		Combat = aimBotTab,
		Players = playersTab,
		Explorer = explorerTab,
		Settings = settingsTab,
	}) do
		local selected = tabName == name
		tab.BackgroundColor3 = selected and colors.surface or colors.sidebar
		tab.Icon.TextColor3 = selected and colors.text or colors.muted
		tab.Label.TextColor3 = selected and colors.text or colors.muted
	end

end

track(toolsTab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	toolsPage.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	showPage("Tools")
end))
track(radarTab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	showPage("Radar")
end))
track(trollTab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	showPage("Troll")
end))
track(aimBotTab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	showPage("Combat")
end))

	showPage("Players")
end))

local radialMenuKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
local radialMenu = Instance.new("Frame")
radialMenu.Name = "RadialFavoritesMenu"
radialMenu.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 240)
radialMenu.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
radialMenu.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
radialMenu.BackgroundColor3 = colors.background
radialMenu.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
radialMenu.BorderSizePixel = 0
radialMenu.Visible = false
radialMenu.ZIndex = 250
radialMenu.Parent = screenGui
round(radialMenu, 999)
local radialButtons = {}

local function getRadialFavorites()
	local result, seen = {}, {}
	for _, record in ipairs(tabRecords) do
		if favoriteTabNames[record.Name] and #result < 6 then
			table.insert(result, record.Name)
			seen[record.Name] = true
		end
	end
	local defaults = { "Tools", "Radar", "Troll", "Combat", "Players", "Settings", "Explorer" }
	for _, name in ipairs(defaults) do
		if #result >= 6 then break end
		if not seen[name] then
			table.insert(result, name)
			seen[name] = true
		end
	end
	return result
end

local function refreshRadialMenu()
	for _, button in ipairs(radialButtons) do button:Destroy() end
	table.clear(radialButtons)
	for index, name in ipairs(getRadialFavorites()) do
		local angle = math.rad(-90 + (index - 1) * 60)
		local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
		button.Name = "Radial_" .. name
		button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(82, 32)
		button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(120 + math.cos(angle) * 78, 120 + math.sin(angle) * 78)
		button.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
		button.BorderSizePixel = 0
		button.AutoButtonColor = true
		button.Text = name
		button.TextColor3 = colors.text
		button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		button.TextSize = 10
		button.ZIndex = 251
		button.Parent = radialMenu
		round(button, 7)
		table.insert(radialButtons, button)
		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			showPage(name)
			setMainFrameOpen(true)
			radialMenu.Visible = false
		end)
	end
end

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if not gameProcessed and input.KeyCode == radialMenuKeyCode then
		refreshRadialMenu()
		radialMenu.Visible = true
	end
end))
track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode == radialMenuKeyCode then
		radialMenu.Visible = false
	end
end))

local function clearExplorerRows()
	for _, row in ipairs(explorerTreeRows) do
		row:Destroy()
	end
	table.clear(explorerTreeRows)
end

local function clearExplorerProperties()
	for _, row in ipairs(explorerPropertyRows) do
		row:Destroy()
	end
	table.clear(explorerPropertyRows)
end

local function getExplorerChildren(instance)
	local success, children = pcall(function()
		return instance:GetChildren()
	end)
	if not success then return {} end
	table.sort(children, function(left, right)
		if left.Name == right.Name then
			return left.ClassName < right.ClassName
		end
		return string.lower(left.Name) < string.lower(right.Name)
	end)
	return children
end

local function formatExplorerValue(value)
	if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
		local ok, fullName = pcall(value.GetFullName, value)
		return ok and fullName or value.Name
	end
	return tostring(value)
end

local function addExplorerProperty(instance, name, value, order)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Name = "Property_" .. name
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 34)
	row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3 + (order - 1) * 36)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = explorerProperties
	round(row, 5)
	applyGlass(row, 0.1)

	local propertyName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	propertyName.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 13)
	propertyName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 2)
	propertyName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	propertyName.Text = name
	propertyName.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	propertyName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
	propertyName.TextSize = 8
	propertyName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	propertyName.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	propertyName.Parent = row

	local propertyValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	propertyValue.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 16)
	propertyValue.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 15)
	propertyValue.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	propertyValue.Text = value
	propertyValue.TextColor3 = colors.text
	propertyValue.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	propertyValue.TextSize = 8
	propertyValue.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	propertyValue.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	propertyValue.Parent = row
	table.insert(explorerPropertyRows, row)
end

inspectExplorerInstance = function(instance)
	clearExplorerProperties()
	if not instance then
		explorerProperties.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		updateExplorerSelectionLayout(false, false)
		return
	end
	local isScript = instance:IsA("Script") or instance:IsA("LocalScript") or instance:IsA("ModuleScript")
	updateExplorerSelectionLayout(true, isScript)

	local properties = { "Name", "ClassName", "Parent", "Archivable" }
	if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
		table.insert(properties, "Position")
		table.insert(properties, "Size")
		table.insert(properties, "Anchored")
		table.insert(properties, "CanCollide")
		table.insert(properties, "Transparency")
	elseif instance:IsA("Humanoid") then
		table.insert(properties, "Health")
		table.insert(properties, "MaxHealth")
		table.insert(properties, "WalkSpeed")
	elseif instance:IsA("Player") then
		table.insert(properties, "DisplayName")
		table.insert(properties, "UserId")
		table.insert(properties, "AccountAge")
		table.insert(properties, "Team")
	elseif instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton") or instance:IsA("TextBox") then
		table.insert(properties, "Text")
		table.insert(properties, "Visible")
	elseif instance:IsA("ValueBase") then
		table.insert(properties, "Value")
	end

	for index, propertyName in ipairs(properties) do
		local success, value = pcall(function()
			return instance[propertyName]
		end)
		if success then
			addExplorerProperty(instance, propertyName, formatExplorerValue(value), index)
		end
	end
	explorerProperties.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #explorerPropertyRows * 36 + 6)
end

local function selectExplorerInstance(instance)
	explorerSelected = instance
	inspectExplorerInstance(instance)
end

local function isExplorerScript(instance)
	return instance:IsA("Script") or instance:IsA("LocalScript") or instance:IsA("ModuleScript")
end

showExplorerSource = function(instance)
	connections.invalidateExplorerSource()
	local token = explorerSourceToken
	if explorerSourceGui then
		explorerSourceGui:Destroy()
	end
	if explorerSourceInputConnection then
		explorerSourceInputConnection:Disconnect()
		explorerSourceInputConnection = nil
	end
	if explorerSourceMatrixConnection then
		explorerSourceMatrixConnection:Disconnect()
		explorerSourceMatrixConnection = nil
	end

	local sourceGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	sourceGui.Name = "ExplorerSourceViewer"
	sourceGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	sourceGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
	sourceGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483647
	sourceGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	sourceGui.Parent = playerGui
	explorerSourceGui = sourceGui

	local viewportSize = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(900, 700)
	local windowWidth = math.min(420, math.max(300, viewportSize.X - 24))
	local windowHeight = math.min(360, math.max(340, viewportSize.Y - 24))
	local window = Instance.new("Frame")
	window.Name = "Window"
	window.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(windowWidth, windowHeight)
	window.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -windowWidth / 2, 0.5, 0)
	window.BackgroundColor3 = colors.background
	window.BorderSizePixel = 0
	window.ClipsDescendants = true
	window.Active = true
	window.ZIndex = 100
	window.Visible = false
	window.Parent = sourceGui
	local setSourceOpen, sourceAnimation = createHorizontalOpenController(window)
	local sourceSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UISizeConstraint")
	sourceSizeConstraint.MinSize = Vector2.new(0, 340)
	sourceSizeConstraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(420, 360)
	sourceSizeConstraint.Parent = window
	round(window, 8)
	local windowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

	windowStroke.Parent = window

	local matrixLayer = Instance.new("Frame")
	matrixLayer.Name = "MatrixBackground"
	matrixLayer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	matrixLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	matrixLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
	matrixLayer.ClipsDescendants = true
	matrixLayer.ZIndex = 101
	matrixLayer.Parent = window
	local matrixRandom = Random.new()
	local matrixStreams = {}
	for columnIndex = 1, math.ceil(windowWidth / 18) do
		local column = Instance.new("Frame")
		column.Name = "MatrixStream"
		column.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 60)
		column.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((columnIndex - 1) * 18, 0)
		column.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		column.BorderSizePixel = 0
		column.ZIndex = 101
		column.Parent = matrixLayer
		local digits = {}
		for digitIndex = 1, 5 do
			local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
			digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * 12)
			digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
			digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			digit.Text = matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
			digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 205, 190)
			digit.TextTransparency = 0.62 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.045
			digit.TextSize = 9
			digit.ZIndex = 101
			digit.Parent = column
			digits[digitIndex] = digit
		end
		matrixStreams[#matrixStreams + 1] = {
			Frame = column,
			Digits = digits,
			Y = matrixRandom:NextNumber(-60, windowHeight),
			Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(18, 34),
			RefreshElapsed = 0,
			RefreshIndex = matrixRandom:NextInteger(1, #digits),
		}
	end
	explorerSourceMatrixConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
		if explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui or not sourceGui.Parent then
			return
		end
		local layerHeight = matrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
		for _, stream in ipairs(matrixStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.18 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > layerHeight then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			end
		end
	end)

	local header = Instance.new("Frame")
	header.Name = "Header"
	header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
	header.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface

	round(header, 8)

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -195, 0, 20)
	title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = instance.Name .. " [" .. instance.ClassName .. "]"
	title.TextColor3 = colors.text
	title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	title.TextSize = 11
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	title.Active = true
	title.ZIndex = 102
	title.Parent = header
	local sourceCredits = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	sourceCredits.Name = "Credits"
	sourceCredits.Size = UDim2.new(1, -195, 0, 12)
	sourceCredits.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 20)
	sourceCredits.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sourceCredits.Text = "By Drzzy, CryzeX"
	sourceCredits.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	sourceCredits.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	sourceCredits.TextSize = 8
	sourceCredits.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	sourceCredits.ZIndex = 102
	sourceCredits.Parent = header

	local copyButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	copyButton.Name = "CopySource"
	copyButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 24)
	copyButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -88, 0, 5)
	copyButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	copyButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	copyButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	copyButton.Text = "COPY"
	copyButton.TextColor3 = colors.text
	copyButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	copyButton.TextSize = 9
	copyButton.ZIndex = 103
	copyButton.Parent = header
	round(copyButton, 5)

	local syntaxButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	syntaxButton.Name = "SyntaxHighlightToggle"
	syntaxButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 24)
	syntaxButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -157, 0, 5)
	syntaxButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	syntaxButton.BorderSizePixel = 0

	syntaxButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	syntaxButton.TextSize = 8
	syntaxButton.ZIndex = 103
	syntaxButton.Parent = header
	round(syntaxButton, 5)

	local closeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	closeButton.Name = "Close"
	closeButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
	closeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -29, 0, 5)
	closeButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	closeButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	closeButton.Text = "X"
	closeButton.TextColor3 = colors.text
	closeButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	closeButton.TextSize = 10
	closeButton.ZIndex = 103
	closeButton.Parent = header
	round(closeButton, 5)
	closeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui then return end
		explorerSourceToken += 1
		setSourceOpen(false)
		local closeTween = sourceAnimation.Tween
		if closeTween then
			closeTween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
				if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed or explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui then return end
				if explorerSourceInputConnection then
					explorerSourceInputConnection:Disconnect()
					explorerSourceInputConnection = nil
				end
				if explorerSourceMatrixConnection then
					explorerSourceMatrixConnection:Disconnect()
					explorerSourceMatrixConnection = nil
				end
				explorerSourceGui = nil
				sourceGui:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end)

	local sourceStatus = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	sourceStatus.Name = "Status"
	sourceStatus.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 15)
	sourceStatus.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -19)
	sourceStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sourceStatus.Text = "Decompiling source..."
	sourceStatus.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	sourceStatus.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	sourceStatus.TextSize = 9
	sourceStatus.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	sourceStatus.Active = true
	sourceStatus.ZIndex = 102
	sourceStatus.Parent = window

	local sourceScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	sourceScroll.Name = "SourceScroll"
	sourceScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -68)
	sourceScroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 50)
	sourceScroll.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	sourceScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
	sourceScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
	sourceScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
	sourceScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 115)
	sourceScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.XY
	sourceScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	sourceScroll.Active = true
	sourceScroll.ClipsDescendants = true
	sourceScroll.ZIndex = 102
	sourceScroll.Parent = window
	round(sourceScroll, 5)

	local codeContent = Instance.new("Frame")
	codeContent.Name = "CodeContent"
	codeContent.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
	codeContent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	codeContent.ZIndex = 103
	codeContent.Parent = sourceScroll

	local currentSource = ""
	local function escapeCodeText(text)
		return text:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;")
	end

	local syntaxEnabled = true
	local sourceLineHeight = 16
	local palette = {
		keyword = "#C792EA",
		string = "#C3E88D",
		comment = "#697386",
		number = "#F78C6C",
		global = "#82AAFF",
	}
	local keywords = {
			["and"] = true, ["break"] = true, ["continue"] = true, ["do"] = true,
			["else"] = true, ["elseif"] = true, ["end"] = true, ["false"] = true,
			["for"] = true, ["function"] = true, ["if"] = true, ["in"] = true,
			["local"] = true, ["nil"] = true, ["not"] = true, ["or"] = true,
			["repeat"] = true, ["return"] = true, ["then"] = true, ["true"] = true,
			["until"] = true, ["while"] = true,
	}
	local globals = {
			game = true, workspace = true, script = true, Enum = true, Instance = true,
			Vector2 = true, Vector3 = true, CFrame = true, Color3 = true, UDim2 = true,
			Players = true, task = true, math = true, string = true, table = true,
	}
	local function formatHighlightedSource(source)
		local output = {}
		local function appendToken(token, color)
			local escaped = escapeCodeText(token)
			if color then
				output[#output + 1] = '<font color="' .. color .. '">' .. escaped .. "</font>"
			else
				output[#output + 1] = escaped
			end
		end

		local index = 1
		while index <= #source do
			local current = source:sub(index, index)
			local nextTwo = source:sub(index, index + 1)
			if nextTwo == "--" then
				local newline = source:find("\n", index, true)
				local finish = newline and newline - 1 or #source
				appendToken(source:sub(index, finish), palette.comment)
				index = finish + 1
			elseif current == '"' or current == "'" then
				local finish = index + 1
				while finish <= #source do
					local char = source:sub(finish, finish)
					if char == "\\" then
						finish = finish + 2
					elseif char == current then
						finish = finish + 1
						break
					else
						finish = finish + 1
					end
				end
				finish = math.min(finish - 1, #source)
				appendToken(source:sub(index, finish), palette.string)
				index = finish + 1
			elseif current:match("%s") then
				local finish = index + 1
				while finish <= #source and source:sub(finish, finish):match("%s") do
					finish = finish + 1
				end
				appendToken(source:sub(index, finish - 1))
				index = finish
			elseif current:match("[%a_]") then
				local tokenText = source:sub(index):match("^[%a_][%w_]*")
				local color = keywords[tokenText] and palette.keyword or globals[tokenText] and palette.global
				appendToken(tokenText, color)
				index = index + #tokenText
			elseif current:match("%d") then
				local tokenText = source:sub(index):match("^%d[%w_%.]*")
				appendToken(tokenText, palette.number)
				index = index + #tokenText
			else
				appendToken(current)
				index = index + 1
			end
		end
		return table.concat(output)
	end

	local function sanitizeSourceLinks(source)
		local marker = "sitetest1.lua.expert"
		while true do
			local markerPosition = source:lower():find(marker, 1, true)
			if not markerPosition then
				break
			end

			local startPosition = markerPosition
			local prefix = source:sub(math.max(1, markerPosition - 8), markerPosition - 1):lower()
			if prefix:sub(-8) == "https://" then
				startPosition = markerPosition - 8
			elseif prefix:sub(-7) == "http://" then
				startPosition = markerPosition - 7
			end
			if startPosition > 4 and source:sub(startPosition - 4, startPosition - 1):lower() == "www." then
				startPosition -= 4
			end

			local endPosition = markerPosition + #marker - 1
			while endPosition < #source do
				local nextCharacter = source:sub(endPosition + 1, endPosition + 1)
				if nextCharacter:match("[%w%._~:/?#%%&=+-]") then
					endPosition += 1
				else
					break
				end
			end
			source = source:sub(1, startPosition - 1) .. source:sub(endPosition + 1)
		end
		return source
	end

	local function renderSource(source)
		if token ~= explorerSourceToken or explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui or not sourceGui.Parent then
			return
		end
		source = sanitizeSourceLinks(source)
		local decompilerHeader = "-- Decompiler Script By CryZeX"
		while source:sub(1, #decompilerHeader) == decompilerHeader do
			source = source:sub(#decompilerHeader + 1)
			if source:sub(1, 2) == "\r\n" then
				source = source:sub(3)
			elseif source:sub(1, 1) == "\n" then
				source = source:sub(2)
			end
		end
		source = decompilerHeader .. "\n" .. source
		currentSource = source
		local lines = string.split(source, "\n")
		if #lines == 0 then lines = { "" } end
		local lineNumbers = table.create(#lines)
		local longestLine = 1
		for index, line in ipairs(lines) do
			lineNumbers[index] = tostring(index)
			longestLine = math.max(longestLine, #line)
		end
		local contentWidth = math.max(windowWidth - 32, sourceScroll.AbsoluteSize.X, 58 + longestLine * 8)
		local contentHeight = math.max(windowHeight - 80, sourceScroll.AbsoluteSize.Y, #lines * sourceLineHeight + 12)
		codeContent.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(contentWidth, contentHeight)
		for _, child in ipairs(codeContent:GetChildren()) do
			child:Destroy()
		end

		local lineNumberLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		lineNumberLabel.Name = "LineNumbers"
		lineNumberLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
		lineNumberLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, contentHeight - 8)
		lineNumberLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lineNumberLabel.Text = table.concat(lineNumbers, "\n")
		lineNumberLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(135, 140, 150)
		lineNumberLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		lineNumberLabel.TextSize = 11
		lineNumberLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		lineNumberLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		lineNumberLabel.TextWrapped = false
		lineNumberLabel.ZIndex = 105
		lineNumberLabel.Parent = codeContent

		local sourceDisplay = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		sourceDisplay.Name = "SourceText"
		sourceDisplay.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 4)
		sourceDisplay.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(contentWidth - 58, contentHeight - 8)
		sourceDisplay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sourceDisplay.RichText = true
		sourceDisplay.Text = syntaxEnabled and formatHighlightedSource(source) or escapeCodeText(source)
		sourceDisplay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
		sourceDisplay.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		sourceDisplay.TextSize = 11
		sourceDisplay.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		sourceDisplay.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		sourceDisplay.TextWrapped = false
		sourceDisplay.TextTransparency = 0
		sourceDisplay.ZIndex = 105
		sourceDisplay.Parent = codeContent
		sourceScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(contentWidth, contentHeight)
		sourceScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
	end

	local function updateSyntaxButton()
		syntaxButton.Text = syntaxEnabled and "SYNTAX ON" or "SYNTAX OFF"
		syntaxButton.TextColor3 = syntaxEnabled and colors.accent or colors.muted
	end
	updateSyntaxButton()
	syntaxButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		syntaxEnabled = not syntaxEnabled
		updateSyntaxButton()
		if currentSource ~= "" then
			renderSource(currentSource)
		end
	end)

	copyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if currentSource == "" then
			sourceStatus.Text = "Source is not ready to copy."
			return
		end
		local clipboard = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard
		if type(clipboard) ~= "function" then
			sourceStatus.Text = "Clipboard is not available in this executor."
			return
		end
		local success, copyError = pcall(clipboard, currentSource)
		sourceStatus.Text = success and "Source copied to clipboard." or ("Copy failed: " .. tostring(copyError))
	end)

	local sourceDragStart
	local windowDragStart
	local draggingSourceWindow = false
	local function beginSourceWindowDrag(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			draggingSourceWindow = true
			sourceDragStart = input.Position
			windowDragStart = window.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					draggingSourceWindow = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	window.InputBegan:Connect(beginSourceWindowDrag)
	sourceStatus.InputBegan:Connect(beginSourceWindowDrag)
	explorerSourceInputConnection = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not draggingSourceWindow or explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui then
			return
		end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			local delta = input.Position - sourceDragStart
			window.Position = UDim2.new(
				windowDragStart.X.Scale, windowDragStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
				windowDragStart.Y.Scale, windowDragStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
			)
		end
	end)

	setSourceOpen(true)
	local decompileFunction = decompile or (getgenv and getgenv().decompile)
		or decompile_func
	if type(decompileFunction) ~= "function" then
		sourceStatus.Text = "Decompiler is not available in this executor."
		renderSource("Decompiler is not available in this executor.")
		return
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local success, source = pcall(decompileFunction, instance)
		if token ~= explorerSourceToken or explorerSourceGui ~= sourceGui or not sourceGui.Parent then
			return
		end
		if success and type(source) == "string" and source ~= "" then
			local cleanedSource = sanitizeSourceLinks(source)
			sourceStatus.Text = string.format("%d lines · Luau source", #string.split(cleanedSource, "\n") + 1)
			renderSource(cleanedSource)
		else
			local errorText = "Could not decompile source:\n" .. tostring(source)
			sourceStatus.Text = "Decompilation failed."
			renderSource(errorText)
		end
	end)
end

explorerViewSourceButton.TextSize = 8

updateExplorerSelectionLayout = function(hasSelection, isScript)
	explorerSelectionLayoutToken += 1
	local token = explorerSelectionLayoutToken
	for _, tween in ipairs(explorerLayoutTweens) do
		tween:Cancel()
	end
	table.clear(explorerLayoutTweens)

	local info = TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	local treeGoal = hasSelection
		and { Size = UDim2.new(0.52, -4, 1, -52) }
		or { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -52) }
	local panelGoal = hasSelection
		and {
			Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 2, 0, isScript and 79 or 49),
			Size = UDim2.new(0.48, -4, 1, isScript and -82 or -52),
		}
		or {
			Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 2, 0, 49),
			Size = UDim2.new(0.48, -4, 0, 0),
		}
	table.insert(explorerLayoutTweens, guiEffects.CreateTween(explorerTree, info, treeGoal))
	table.insert(explorerLayoutTweens, guiEffects.CreateTween(explorerProperties, info, panelGoal))
	for _, tween in ipairs(explorerLayoutTweens) do
		tween:Play()
	end

	if hasSelection then
		explorerProperties.Visible = true
	end
	if explorerPropertyToolbar then
		if isScript then
			explorerPropertyToolbar.Visible = true
			explorerViewSourceButton.Visible = true
			explorerViewSourceButton.Position = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 0)
			local buttonTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
				explorerViewSourceButton,
				info,
				{ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }
			)
			table.insert(explorerLayoutTweens, buttonTween)
			buttonTween:Play()
		elseif explorerPropertyToolbar.Visible then
			local buttonTween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
				explorerViewSourceButton,
				info,
				{ Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }
			)
			table.insert(explorerLayoutTweens, buttonTween)
			buttonTween.Completed:Connect(function()
				if token ~= explorerSelectionLayoutToken then return end
				explorerPropertyToolbar.Visible = false
				explorerViewSourceButton.Visible = false
			end)
			buttonTween:Play()
		end
	end

	if not hasSelection then
		local panelTween = explorerLayoutTweens[2]
		panelTween.Completed:Connect(function()
			if token == explorerSelectionLayoutToken then
				explorerProperties.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end
end

explorerViewSourceButton.Position = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 0)
explorerViewSourceButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if explorerSelected and isExplorerScript(explorerSelected) then
		showExplorerSource(explorerSelected)
	end
end)

local function makeExplorerTreeRow(instance, depth, order, isRoot)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Name = "ExplorerTreeRow"
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, explorerRowHeight)
	row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, (order - 1) * explorerRowHeight)
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = explorerTree
	table.insert(explorerTreeRows, row)

	local children = getExplorerChildren(instance)
	local hasChildren = #children > 0
	local isExpanded = explorerExpanded[instance] == true
	local indent = math.min(depth * 11, 80)

	local expandButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	expandButton.Name = "Expand"
	expandButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, explorerRowHeight)
	expandButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(indent, 0)
	expandButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	expandButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	expandButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	local isSearching = explorerSearchBox.Text ~= ""
	expandButton.Text = hasChildren and not isSearching and (isExpanded and "-" or "+") or ""
	expandButton.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	expandButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	expandButton.TextSize = 10
	expandButton.Parent = row
	expandButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if hasChildren and not isSearching then
			explorerExpanded[instance] = not isExpanded
			renderExplorerTree()
		end
	end)

	local itemButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	itemButton.Name = "Select"
	itemButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(indent + 17), 1, 0)
	itemButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(indent + 15, 0)
	itemButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
	itemButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
	itemButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	itemButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	itemButton.Text = instance.Name .. "  [" .. instance.ClassName .. "]"
	itemButton.TextColor3 = isRoot and colors.accent or colors.text
	itemButton.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	itemButton.TextSize = 8
	itemButton.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	itemButton.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	itemButton.Parent = row
	round(itemButton, 3)
	itemButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		selectExplorerInstance(instance)
		local now = os.clock()
		if explorerLastClickTarget == instance and now - explorerLastClickTime <= 0.35 then
			explorerLastClickTarget = nil
			if isExplorerScript(instance) then
				showExplorerSource(instance)
			end
		else
			explorerLastClickTarget = instance
			explorerLastClickTime = now
		end
	end)
end

renderExplorerTree = function()
	clearExplorerRows()
	local searchQuery = string.lower(explorerSearchBox.Text)
	if searchQuery ~= "" then
		local results = explorerSearchResults or {}
		for index, instance in ipairs(results) do
			if index > explorerTreeLimit then break end
			makeExplorerTreeRow(instance, 0, index, false)
		end
		explorerTree.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.min(#results, explorerTreeLimit) * explorerRowHeight)
		return
	end

	local rowCount = 0
	local function append(instance, depth, isRoot)
		if rowCount >= explorerTreeLimit then return end
		rowCount += 1
		makeExplorerTreeRow(instance, depth, rowCount, isRoot)
		if explorerExpanded[instance] then
			for _, child in ipairs(getExplorerChildren(instance)) do
				append(child, depth + 1, false)
				if rowCount >= explorerTreeLimit then break end
			end
		end
	end
	for _, service in ipairs(explorerRoots) do
		append(service, 0, true)
		if rowCount >= explorerTreeLimit then break end
	end
	explorerTree.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, rowCount * explorerRowHeight)
end

searchExplorer = function(query)
	explorerSearchToken += 1
	local token = explorerSearchToken
	query = string.lower(query)
	if query == "" then
		explorerSearchResults = nil
		explorerStatus.Text = "Client-visible hierarchy - select an item to inspect"
		renderExplorerTree()
		return
	end

	explorerStatus.Text = "Searching client-visible instances..."
	task.spawn(function()
		local results = {}
		local stack = table.clone(explorerRoots)
		local visited = 0
		while #stack > 0 and visited < 12000 and #results < explorerTreeLimit do
			if token ~= explorerSearchToken or not explorerPage.Parent then return end
			local instance = table.remove(stack)
			visited += 1
			local matches = string.find(string.lower(instance.Name), query, 1, true)
				or string.find(string.lower(instance.ClassName), query, 1, true)
			if matches then
				table.insert(results, instance)
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(getExplorerChildren(instance)) do
				if #stack >= 12000 then break end
				table.insert(stack, child)
			end
			if visited % 150 == 0 then task.wait() end
		end
		if token ~= explorerSearchToken then return end
		explorerSearchResults = results
		explorerStatus.Text = string.format("Showing %d matches (limit %d)", #results, explorerTreeLimit)
		renderExplorerTree()
	end)
end

local function refreshExplorer()
	explorerSearchToken += 1
	explorerSearchResults = nil
	explorerSelected = nil
	explorerExpanded = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	inspectExplorerInstance(nil)
	searchExplorer(explorerSearchBox.Text)
end

track(explorerSearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	searchExplorer(explorerSearchBox.Text)
end))
track(explorerRefreshButton.Activated:Connect(refreshExplorer))
track(screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
	if explorerSourceInputConnection then
		explorerSourceInputConnection:Disconnect()
		explorerSourceInputConnection = nil
	end
	if explorerSourceMatrixConnection then
		explorerSourceMatrixConnection:Disconnect()
		explorerSourceMatrixConnection = nil
	end
	if explorerSourceGui then
		explorerSourceGui:Destroy()
		explorerSourceGui = nil
	end
end))
renderExplorerTree()
end

local playerCards = {}
connections.viewedPlayer = nil
connections.previousCameraSubject = nil
connections.viewToken = 0

connections.restoreViewedPlayer = function()
	if not connections.viewedPlayer then return end

	connections.viewToken += 1
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local localCharacter = player.Character
	local localHumanoid = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local fallbackSubject = localHumanoid or connections.previousCameraSubject
	if camera and fallbackSubject and fallbackSubject.Parent then
		camera.CameraSubject = fallbackSubject
	end
	connections.viewedPlayer = nil
	connections.previousCameraSubject = nil
end

connections.applyViewedCamera = function(targetPlayer, character)
	if connections.viewedPlayer ~= targetPlayer or targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players then return false end
	local currentCharacter = character or targetPlayer.Character
	local humanoid = currentCharacter and currentCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not humanoid or not camera then return false end
	camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	return true
end

connections.viewPlayer = function(targetPlayer)
	if targetPlayer == player or targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players then return end

	local character = targetPlayer.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local targetRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not character or not camera then
		warn("Players tab VIEW could not find the target character or camera.")
		return
	end

	if not connections.viewedPlayer then
		connections.previousCameraSubject = camera.CameraSubject
	end
	connections.viewToken += 1
	local token = connections.viewToken
	connections.viewedPlayer = targetPlayer

	if humanoid and connections.applyViewedCamera(targetPlayer, character) then
		return
	end

	local requestPosition
	if targetRoot then
		requestPosition = targetRoot.Position
	else
		local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
			return character:GetPivot()
		end)
		if ok then requestPosition = pivot.Position end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		if requestPosition then
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(requestPosition, 5)
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("Players tab could not request target streaming: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end

		local deadline = os.clock() + 8
		while token == connections.viewToken and connections.viewedPlayer == targetPlayer
			and targetPlayer.Parent == Players and os.clock() < deadline do
			if connections.applyViewedCamera(targetPlayer) then return end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		if token == connections.viewToken and connections.viewedPlayer == targetPlayer then
			warn("Players tab VIEW timed out waiting for the target character to stream.")
			connections.restoreViewedPlayer()
		end
	end)
end

track(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	local targetPlayer = connections.viewedPlayer
	if targetPlayer then
		task.defer(function()
			connections.applyViewedCamera(targetPlayer)
		end)
	end
end))

track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	task.defer(function()
		local targetPlayer = connections.viewedPlayer
		if targetPlayer then
			connections.applyViewedCamera(targetPlayer)
		end
	end)
end))

local function getPlayerDistance(targetPlayer)
    local localRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot or not targetRoot then return math.huge end
    return (localRoot.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
end

local function getPlayerTeamOptions()
    local options = { "All", "No Team" }
    local found, names = {}, {}
    for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if targetPlayer.Team then found[targetPlayer.Team.Name] = true end
    end
    for teamName in pairs(found) do table.insert(names, teamName) end
    table.sort(names)
    for _, teamName in ipairs(names) do table.insert(options, teamName) end
    return options
end

track(playerSortButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local index = table.find(playerSortModes, playerSortMode) or 1
    playerSortMode = playerSortModes[index % #playerSortModes + 1]
    playerSortButton.Text = "SORT: " .. string.upper(playerSortMode)
    if refreshPlayerCards then refreshPlayerCards() end
end))

track(playerTeamButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local options = getPlayerTeamOptions()
    local index = table.find(options, playerTeamFilter) or 1
    playerTeamFilter = options[index % #options + 1]
    playerTeamButton.Text = "TEAM: " .. string.upper(playerTeamFilter)
    if refreshPlayerCards then refreshPlayerCards() end
end))

local function makePlayerContextAction(label, callback)
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)

    button.ZIndex = 301
    button.Parent = playerContextMenu
    round(button, 5)
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        local target = contextTargetPlayer
        closePlayerContextMenu()
        if target and target.Parent == Players then callback(target) end
    end)
end

makePlayerContextAction("Copy username", function(target)
    if type(setclipboard) == "function" then
        pcall(setclipboard, target.Name)
    else
        warn("Clipboard API is unavailable in this environment.")
    end
end)
makePlayerContextAction("Copy UserId", function(target)
    if type(setclipboard) == "function" then
        pcall(setclipboard, tostring(target.UserId))
    else
        warn("Clipboard API is unavailable in this environment.")
    end
end)
makePlayerContextAction("Open profile", function(target)
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("GuiService"):InspectPlayerFromUserId(target.UserId)
    end)
end)

local function teleportToPlayer(targetPlayer)
	if targetPlayer == player or targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players then
		return false, "PLAYER LEFT"
	end

	local targetCharacter = targetPlayer.Character
	local targetRoot = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local localCharacter = player.Character
	local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local localHumanoid = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not targetRoot then return false, "TARGET NOT LOADED" end
	if not localRoot or not localHumanoid or localHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false, "CHARACTER NOT READY"
	end

	localRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
	return true, "TELEPORTED"
end

local function updatePlayerSearch()
	local query = string.lower(playerSearchBox.Text)
	local totalCount = 0
	local visibleCount = 0
	for targetPlayer, record in pairs(playerCards) do
		if targetPlayer.Parent == Players then
			totalCount += 1
			local matchesSearch = query == ""
				or string.find(string.lower(targetPlayer.Name), query, 1, true) ~= nil
				or string.find(string.lower(targetPlayer.DisplayName), query, 1, true) ~= nil
			local matchesTeam = playerTeamFilter == "All"
				or (playerTeamFilter == "No Team" and targetPlayer.Team == nil)
				or (targetPlayer.Team ~= nil and targetPlayer.Team.Name == playerTeamFilter)
			local matches = matchesSearch and matchesTeam
			record.Card.Visible = matches
			if matches then visibleCount += 1 end
		end
	end
	playersCountLabel.Text = string.format("Players: %d  |  Showing: %d", totalCount, visibleCount)
end

local function removePlayerCard(targetPlayer)
	local record = playerCards[targetPlayer]
	if not record then return end

	for _, connection in ipairs(record.Connections) do
		connection:Disconnect()
	end
	record.Card:Destroy()
	playerCards[targetPlayer] = nil
	if connections.viewedPlayer == targetPlayer then
		connections.restoreViewedPlayer()
	end
end

	if existingRecord then
		existingRecord.Card.LayoutOrder = order
		existingRecord.DisplayNameLabel.Text = targetPlayer.DisplayName
		existingRecord.UsernameLabel.Text = "@" .. targetPlayer.Name
		existingRecord.AccountAgeLabel.Text = string.format("Account age: %d days", targetPlayer.AccountAge)

			existingRecord.PinButton.Text = pinnedPlayers[targetPlayer.UserId] and "★" or "☆"
		end
