	local unviewButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	unviewButton.Name = "UnviewButton"
	unviewButton.Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -8, 0, 26)
	unviewButton.Position = UDim2.new(2 / 3, -4, 1, -35)
	unviewButton.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	unviewButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	unviewButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	unviewButton.Text = "UNVIEW"
	unviewButton.TextColor3 = colors.text
	unviewButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	unviewButton.TextSize = 10
	unviewButton.Parent = card
	round(unviewButton, 6)
	applyGlass(unviewButton, 0.08)

	local record = {
		Card = card,
		DisplayNameLabel = displayLabel,
		UsernameLabel = usernameLabel,
		AccountAgeLabel = accountAgeLabel,
		PinButton = pinButton,
		Connections = {},
	}
	playerCards[targetPlayer] = record
	table.insert(record.Connections, pinButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		pinnedPlayers[targetPlayer.UserId] = not pinnedPlayers[targetPlayer.UserId] or nil
		pinButton.Text = pinnedPlayers[targetPlayer.UserId] and "★" or "☆"
		if refreshPlayerCards then refreshPlayerCards() end
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, card.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
			contextTargetPlayer = targetPlayer
			local viewport = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
			local x = math.clamp(input.Position.X, 4, math.max(4, viewport.X - 158))
			local y = math.clamp(input.Position.Y, 4, math.max(4, viewport.Y - 106))
			playerContextMenu.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
			playerContextMenu.Visible = true
		end
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, viewButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		connections.viewPlayer(targetPlayer)
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, gotoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local succeeded, status = teleportToPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if not succeeded then
			warn("Players tab GOTO failed: " .. status)
		end
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, unviewButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(connections.restoreViewedPlayer))
	table.insert(record.Connections, targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		if connections.viewedPlayer ~= targetPlayer then return end
		local token = connections.viewToken
		task.spawn(function()
			local deadline = os.clock() + 8
			while token == connections.viewToken and connections.viewedPlayer == targetPlayer
				and targetPlayer.Character == character and os.clock() < deadline do
				if connections.applyViewedCamera(targetPlayer, character) then return end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
	end))
end

refreshPlayerCards = function()
	local currentPlayers = Players:GetPlayers()
	local present = {}
	table.sort(currentPlayers, function(left, right)
		local leftPinned = pinnedPlayers[left.UserId] == true
		local rightPinned = pinnedPlayers[right.UserId] == true
		if leftPinned ~= rightPinned then return leftPinned end
		if playerSortMode == "Account age" and left.AccountAge ~= right.AccountAge then
			return left.AccountAge > right.AccountAge
		elseif playerSortMode == "Distance" then
			local leftDistance = getPlayerDistance(left)
			local rightDistance = getPlayerDistance(right)
			if leftDistance ~= rightDistance then return leftDistance < rightDistance end
		end
		local leftName = string.lower(left.DisplayName)
		local rightName = string.lower(right.DisplayName)
		if leftName == rightName then
			return string.lower(left.Name) < string.lower(right.Name)
		end
		return leftName < rightName
	end)

	for index, targetPlayer in ipairs(currentPlayers) do
		present[targetPlayer] = true
		addPlayerCard(targetPlayer, index + 4)
	end
	for targetPlayer in pairs(playerCards) do
		if not present[targetPlayer] then
			removePlayerCard(targetPlayer)
		end
	end
	updatePlayerSearch()
end

track(playerSearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(updatePlayerSearch))
track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and playerContextMenu.Visible then
		local point = input.Position
		local pos = playerContextMenu.AbsolutePosition
		local size = playerContextMenu.AbsoluteSize
		local inside = point.X >= pos.X and point.X <= pos.X + size.X
			and point.Y >= pos.Y and point.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y
		if not inside then closePlayerContextMenu() end
	end
end))
track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(refreshPlayerCards))
track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(targetPlayer)
	removePlayerCard(targetPlayer)
	updatePlayerSearch()
end))
refreshPlayerCards()

task.spawn(function()
	while screenGui.Parent do
		task.wait(30)
		if not screenGui.Parent then break end
		refreshPlayerCards()
	end
end)

local dragStart
local frameStart
local dragging = false

track(titleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		dragging = true
		dragStart = input.Position
		frameStart = mainFrame.Position
		track(input.Changed:Connect(function()
			if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
				dragging = false
			end
		end))
	end
end))

track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
			frameStart.X.Scale, frameStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
			frameStart.Y.Scale, frameStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
		)
	end
end))

local function makeRow(text, order, action, parentPage)
	local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
	row.Name = text:gsub("%W", "") .. "Row"
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.AutoButtonColor = false
	row.Text = ""
	row.LayoutOrder = order
	row.Parent = parentPage or toolsPage
	round(row, 8)

	row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		guiEffects.CreateTween(row, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
			BackgroundColor3 = colors.surfaceHover,
		}):Play()
	end)
	row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		guiEffects.CreateTween(row, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
			BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface,
		}):Play()
	end)

	applyGlass(row, 0.22)

	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1, action and -66 or -62, 1, 0)
	label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 0)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Text = text
	label.TextColor3 = colors.text
	label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	label.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
	label.TextSize = 13
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = row

	if action then
		local actionLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		actionLabel.Name = "Action"
		actionLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 22)
		actionLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0.5, -11)
		actionLabel.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
		actionLabel.Text = "RUN"
		actionLabel.TextColor3 = colors.accent
		actionLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		actionLabel.TextSize = 9
		actionLabel.Parent = row
		round(actionLabel, 6)
		applyGlass(actionLabel, 0.16)
		return row, nil, label
	end

	local indicator = Instance.new("Frame")
	indicator.Name = "Indicator"
	indicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 16)
	indicator.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -8)
	indicator.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	indicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
	indicator.Parent = row
	round(indicator, 99)

	local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
	knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
	knob.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
	knob.BackgroundColor3 = colors.muted
	knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
	knob.Parent = indicator
	round(knob, 99)

	return row, knob, label
end

local function setToggleVisual(knob, on)
	if not knob then return end
	guiEffects.CreateTween(knob, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
		Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 2) or UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2),
		BackgroundColor3 = on and colors.success or colors.muted,
	}):Play()
end

function guiEffects.SetNoAnimation(enabled)
	guiEffects.NoAnimation = enabled
	getgenv().AaNoAnimation = enabled
	if enabled then
		guiEffects.HideMatrix()
		seasonalLayer.Visible = false
	else
		guiEffects.RestoreMatrix()
		setVisualTheme(visualThemeMode)
	end
end

local themePalettes = {
	Default = {
		background = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 14),
		header = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 18),
		sidebar = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 16),
		surface = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 25),
		surfaceHover = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 35),
		track = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 13),
		border = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 50),
		text = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
		muted = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 153),
		accent = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 237),
		success = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230),
	},
}
local currentTheme = "Default"
local cleanupGui
local resetAllSettings

local function applyTheme(themeName)
	local nextPalette = themePalettes[themeName]
	if not nextPalette then return end

	local previousPalette = themePalettes[currentTheme]
	for key, nextColor in pairs(nextPalette) do
		local previousColor = previousPalette[key]
		for _, instance in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
			if instance:IsA("GuiObject") and instance.BackgroundColor3 == previousColor then
				instance.BackgroundColor3 = nextColor
			end
			if (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton") or instance:IsA("TextBox"))
				and instance.TextColor3 == previousColor then
				instance.TextColor3 = nextColor
			end
			if (instance:IsA("ImageLabel") or instance:IsA("ImageButton"))
				and not instance:GetAttribute("PreserveImageColor")
				and instance.ImageColor3 == previousColor then
				instance.ImageColor3 = nextColor
			end
			if instance:IsA("ScrollingFrame") and instance.ScrollBarImageColor3 == previousColor then
				instance.ScrollBarImageColor3 = nextColor
			end
			if instance:IsA("UIStroke") and instance.Color == previousColor then
				instance.Color = nextColor
			end
		end
		colors[key] = nextColor
	end

	backgroundGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 13)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(21, 21, 22)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 13)),
	})
	if connections.terminalGradient then
		connections.terminalGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, colors.background),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, colors.surface),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colors.background),
		})
	end
	currentTheme = themeName
	if connections.refreshMainMatrix then
		connections.refreshMainMatrix()
	end
	if connections.refreshTerminalMatrix then
		connections.refreshTerminalMatrix()
	end
	if connections.refreshTerminalSyntax then
		connections.refreshTerminalSyntax()
	end
	showPage(activePage)
end

local function formatUptime(seconds)
	seconds = math.max(0, math.floor(seconds))
	local hours = math.floor(seconds / 3600)
	local minutes = math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)
	local remainingSeconds = seconds % 60
	return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", hours, minutes, remainingSeconds)
end

connections.settingsRefreshElapsed = 0
track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	connections.settingsRefreshElapsed += deltaTime
	if connections.settingsRefreshElapsed < 1 then return end
	connections.settingsRefreshElapsed = 0
	playerCountValue.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
	uptimeValue.Text = formatUptime(Workspace.DistributedGameTime)
end))

	playerCountValue.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
end))

	task.defer(function()
		if playerCountValue.Parent then
			playerCountValue.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
		end
	end)
end))

	end
end)

local customMessageScriptSource = [=[
local customMessage = "Custom Message ex.feet"
local lplr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

while true do
    local char = lplr.Character
    local animator = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animator", true)

    if animator then
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = ("http=507770677" .. utf8.char(Random.new():NextInteger(1, 65535)) .. customMessage):sub(1, 256)

        local loaded = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
        loaded:Play(0, 0.0001, 0)
    end

    task.wait()
end
]=]

track(customMessageRun.Activated:Connect(function()
	local message = customMessageInput.Text
	if message:match("^%s*$") then
		customMessageStatus.Text = "Enter a message before running the source."
		return
	end
	if #message > 100 then
		customMessageStatus.Text = "Message is too long; limit is 100 characters."
		return
	end

	customMessageStatus.Text = "Running custom message source..."
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local scriptText = customMessageScriptSource:gsub("Custom Message ex.feet", message)
		local compiled = assert(loadstring(scriptText))
		compiled()
	end)
	if not ok then
		customMessageStatus.Text = "Custom message source failed to run."
		warn("Custom message source failed:", err)
	end
end))

local function sendFlatNotification(title, text, color, force)
    if not notificationsEnabled and not force then
        return
    end

    local nFrame = Instance.new("Frame", notifContainer)
    getgenv().AaNotificationOrder = (getgenv().AaNotificationOrder or 0) + 1
    nFrame.Name = "MatrixNotification"
    nFrame.LayoutOrder = getgenv().AaNotificationOrder
    nFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 58)
    nFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 9)
    nFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    nFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    nFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    nFrame.ZIndex = 10
    Instance.new("UICorner", nFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local pendingContainer = getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationContainer
    if pendingContainer and pendingContainer.Parent and pendingContainer ~= notifContainer then
        for _, pendingNotification in ipairs(pendingContainer:GetChildren()) do
            if pendingNotification:IsA("GuiObject") then
                pendingNotification.Parent = notifContainer
            end
        end
        local pendingGui = getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationGui
        if pendingGui then
            pendingGui:Destroy()
        end
        getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationContainer = nil
        getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationGui = nil
    end

    local matrixLayer = Instance.new("Frame", nFrame)
    matrixLayer.Name = "MatrixBackground"
    matrixLayer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    matrixLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    matrixLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    matrixLayer.ClipsDescendants = true
    matrixLayer.ZIndex = 10

    local matrixRandom = Random.new()
    local matrixStreams = {}
    for columnIndex = 1, 18 do
        local column = Instance.new("Frame", matrixLayer)
        column.Name = "MatrixStream"
        column.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 36)
        column.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((columnIndex - 1) * 13, 0)
        column.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        column.BorderSizePixel = 0
        column.ZIndex = 10

        local digits = {}
        for digitIndex = 1, 4 do
            local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel", column)
            digit.Name = "Digit"
            digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 9)
            digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * 9)
            digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
            digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            digit.Text = matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
            digit.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 215)
            digit.TextTransparency = 0.78 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.04
            digit.TextSize = 8
            digit.ZIndex = 10
            digits[digitIndex] = digit
        end

        table.insert(matrixStreams, {
            Frame = column,
            Digits = digits,
            Y = matrixRandom:NextNumber(-36, 58),
            Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(14, 24),
            RefreshElapsed = 0,
            RefreshIndex = matrixRandom:NextInteger(1, #digits),
        })
    end

    local topbar = Instance.new("Frame", nFrame)
    topbar.Name = "Topbar"
    topbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    topbar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 18)
    topbar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    topbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topbar.ZIndex = 11
    Instance.new("UICorner", topbar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local topbarBottomFill = Instance.new("Frame", topbar)
    topbarBottomFill.Name = "BottomFill"
    topbarBottomFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
    topbarBottomFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -10)
    topbarBottomFill.BackgroundColor3 = topbar.BackgroundColor3
    topbarBottomFill.BackgroundTransparency = topbar.BackgroundTransparency
    topbarBottomFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topbarBottomFill.ZIndex = 11

    local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", topbar)
    titleLabel.Name = "Title"
    titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0)
    titleLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 0)
    titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLabel.Text = title:upper()
    titleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
    titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    titleLabel.TextSize = 9
    titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    titleLabel.ZIndex = 12

    for index = 1, 3 do
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", topbar)
        dot.Name = "WindowDot"
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -9 - ((index - 1) * 9), 0.5, -2.5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(75, 75, 77)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 12
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    end

    local messageLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", nFrame)
    messageLabel.Name = "Information"
    messageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -22)
    messageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 20)
    messageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    messageLabel.Text = text
    messageLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
    messageLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    messageLabel.TextSize = 11
    messageLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    messageLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    messageLabel.TextWrapped = true
    messageLabel.ZIndex = 12

    local matrixConnection = trackOrbitConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        if getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true then
            return
        end
        if not nFrame.Parent then
            return
        end
        local layerHeight = matrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
        for _, stream in ipairs(matrixStreams) do
            stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
            stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
            stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
            if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.2 then
                stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
                stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
                    matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
                stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
            end
            if stream.Y > layerHeight then
                stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
            end
        end
    end))

    TweenService:Create(nFrame, TweenInfo.new(getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true and 0 or 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 58),
    }):Play()
    task.delay(2.5, function()
        if not nFrame.Parent then
            return
        end
        local tween = TweenService:Create(nFrame, TweenInfo.new(getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true and 0 or 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 58),
        })
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Wait()
        matrixConnection:Disconnect()
        nFrame:Destroy()
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while getgenv().OrbitToolsy do
        task.wait(3)
        if #addedT > 0 or #removedT > 0 then
            local txt = ""
            if #addedT > 0 then
                txt = txt .. "+ " .. table.concat(addedT, ", ") .. " "
            end
            if #removedT > 0 then
                txt = txt .. "- " .. table.concat(removedT, ", ")
            end
            sendFlatNotification("Tool Update", txt, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
            table.clear(addedT)
            table.clear(removedT)
        end
    end
end)

local cmds = {"offset", "speed", "rot", "mode", "target", "unorbit", "equip", "unequip", "stop", "lerp", "notify"}

local cmdDocs = {
    offset = "offset [num] - Sets radius distance",
    speed = "speed [num] - Sets orbit speed",
    rot = "rot [num] - Sets tool spinning speed",
    mode = "mode [num] - Changes orbit pattern (1-20+)",
    target = "target [name] - Orbits a specific player",
    unorbit = "unorbit - Returns orbit to yourself",
    equip = "equip - Equips all tools from backpack",
    unequip = "unequip - Stores tools in backpack",
    stop = "stop - Resets your character; the GUI stays active",
    lerp = "lerp [num] - Adjusts transition smoothness",
    notify = "notify [on/off] - Enables or disables notifications"
}

local function getPartialMatch(input)
    if not input or input == "" then return nil end
    for _, c in ipairs(cmds) do
        if c:sub(1, #input) == input then return c end
    end
    return nil
end

local helpFrame 
local helpEntries = {}

local function processCommandString(input)
    local args = input:split(" ")
    if #args == 0 then return end

    local c = args[1]:lower()
    local val = args[2]

    if not table.find(cmds, c) then return end

    if c == "notify" then
        local setting = val and val:lower()
        if setting == "off" then
            sendFlatNotification("Notifications", "Notifications disabled", Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
            notificationsEnabled = false
        elseif setting == "on" then
            notificationsEnabled = true
            sendFlatNotification("Notifications", "Notifications enabled", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        else
            warn("Usage: notify [on/off]")
        end
    elseif c == "offset" then
        offset = tonumber(val) or offset
        sendFlatNotification("Settings", "Distance: " .. offset, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "speed" then
        local s = tonumber(val) or speed
        speed = (s < 0.5 and s > -0.5) and ((s >= 0) and 0.5 or -0.5) or s
        sendFlatNotification("Settings", "Orbit Speed: " .. speed, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "rot" then
        toolRotSpeed = tonumber(val) or toolRotSpeed
        sendFlatNotification("Settings", "Tool Spin: " .. toolRotSpeed, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "mode" then
        mode = tonumber(val) or mode
        sendFlatNotification("Settings", "Pattern Mode: " .. mode, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "target" then
        if not val then return end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Name:lower():find(val:lower(), 1, true) or (p.DisplayName and p.DisplayName:lower():find(val:lower(), 1, true)) then
                targetHRP = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or targetHRP
                lastTargetChange = os.clock()
                tweenEnabled = false
                sendFlatNotification("Targeting", "Locked: " .. p.DisplayName, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
                break
            end
        end
    elseif c == "unorbit" then
        targetHRP = hrp
        sendFlatNotification("Targeting", "Returned to self", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    elseif c == "equip" then
        for _, tool in ipairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") then tool.Parent = chr end
        end
        sendFlatNotification("Command", "Equipped All", Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "unequip" then
        for _, tool in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") then tool.Parent = plr.Backpack end
        end
        sendFlatNotification("Command", "Unequipped All", Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "lerp" then
        lerpSpeed = tonumber(val) or lerpSpeed
        sendFlatNotification("Settings", "Tween Speed: " .. lerpSpeed, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    elseif c == "stop" then
        if chr:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then chr.Humanoid.Health = 0 end
        sendFlatNotification("System", "Character reset; GUI remains active", Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100))
    end
end

function cleanupTool(h)
    local i = table.find(handles, h)
    if i then
        table.insert(removedT, toolNames[i])
        lastPos[h] = nil
        lastMove[h] = nil
        if orbitParts[i] then orbitParts[i]:Destroy() end
        if movers[h] then
            if movers[h].align then movers[h].align:Destroy() end
            if movers[h].angular then movers[h].angular:Destroy() end
            movers[h] = nil
        end
        if connections[h] then
            connections[h]:Disconnect()
            connections[h] = nil
        end
        table.remove(orbitParts, i)
        table.remove(handles, i)
        table.remove(toolNames, i)
    end
end

function setupTool(v)
    if not v or not v:IsA("Tool") then return end
    local h = v:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not h or table.find(handles, h) then return end

    v.Parent = bp
    v.Parent = chr
    v.Parent = bp
    v.Parent = workspace
    v.Parent = chr

    local ra = chr:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or chr:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
    if ra then
        local grip = ra:FindFirstChild("RightGrip")
        if grip then
            grip:Destroy()
            v.Parent = bp
            v.Parent = chr
            v.Parent = bp
            v.Parent = workspace
            v.Parent = chr
        end
    end

    connections[h] = v.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if parent ~= chr then cleanupTool(h) end
    end)
    table.insert(handles, h)
    table.insert(toolNames, v.Name)
    table.insert(addedT, v.Name)
    local i = #handles

    lastPos[h] = h.Position
    lastMove[h] = os.clock()

    local p = Instance.new("Part", workspace)
    p.Name = "OrbitReference_" .. v.Name
    p.Anchored, p.CanCollide, p.Transparency = true, false, 1
    p.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    p.Position = hrp.Position
    orbitParts[i] = p

    local av = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity", h)
    av.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    av.P = 1250

    local ap = Instance.new("AlignPosition", h)
    ap.MaxForce, ap.MaxVelocity, ap.Responsiveness = math.huge, math.huge, 200
    ap.Attachment0 = Instance.new("Attachment", h)
    ap.Attachment1 = Instance.new("Attachment", p)

    movers[h] = {align = ap, angular = av}
end

local function createUI()
    if getgenv and getgenv().OrbitUI then getgenv().OrbitUI:Destroy() end

    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "FeOrbitUI"
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screenGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483644
    screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    screenGui.Parent = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    if getgenv then getgenv().OrbitUI = screenGui end

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
    frame.Name = "MainFrame"
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 360)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0.5, 0)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 14)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    frame.Active = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 50)
    stroke.Transparency = 0.15
    stroke.Thickness = 1

    local sizeConstraint = Instance.new("UISizeConstraint", frame)
    sizeConstraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(420, 360)
    sizeConstraint.MinSize = Vector2.new(0, 360)

    closeOrbitUI = function()
        if not frame.Parent then
            return
        end

        if getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true then
            if getgenv().OrbitUI == screenGui then
                getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
            end
            screenGui:Destroy()
            return
        end

        local closingTween = TweenService:Create(
            frame,
            TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
            { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 360) }
        )
        closingTween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
            if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
                return
            end
            if getgenv().OrbitUI == screenGui then
                getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
            end
            screenGui:Destroy()
        end)
        closingTween:Play()
    end

    local matrixLayer = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    matrixLayer.Name = "MatrixBackground"
    matrixLayer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    matrixLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    matrixLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    matrixLayer.ClipsDescendants = true
    matrixLayer.ZIndex = 1

    local matrixRandom = Random.new()
    local matrixStreams = {}
    local matrixCharacterCount = 12
    local matrixLineHeight = 16
    local function randomMatrixDigit()
        return matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
    end

    for columnIndex = 1, 22 do
        local column = Instance.new("Frame", matrixLayer)
        column.Name = "MatrixStream"
        column.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, matrixCharacterCount * matrixLineHeight)
        column.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5 + (columnIndex - 1) * 20, 0)
        column.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        column.BorderSizePixel = 0
        column.ZIndex = 1

        local digits = {}
        for digitIndex = 1, matrixCharacterCount do
            local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel", column)
            digit.Name = "Digit"
            digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, matrixLineHeight)
            digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * matrixLineHeight)
            digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
            digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            digit.Text = randomMatrixDigit()
            digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
            digit.TextSize = 13
            digit.TextTransparency = math.min(0.92, 0.22 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.06)
            digit.ZIndex = 1
            digits[digitIndex] = digit
        end

        table.insert(matrixStreams, {
            Frame = column,
            Digits = digits,
            Y = matrixRandom:NextNumber(-matrixCharacterCount * matrixLineHeight, 360),
            Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(32, 70),
            RefreshElapsed = 0,
            RefreshIndex = matrixRandom:NextInteger(1, matrixCharacterCount),
        })
        if columnIndex % 4 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end

    local topbar = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    topbar.Name = "Topbar"
    topbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
    topbar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 18)
    topbar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    topbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topbar.ZIndex = 2
    Instance.new("UICorner", topbar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local topbarBottomFill = Instance.new("Frame", topbar)
    topbarBottomFill.Name = "BottomFill"
    topbarBottomFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
    topbarBottomFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -12)
    topbarBottomFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 18)
    topbarBottomFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    topbarBottomFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topbarBottomFill.ZIndex = 2

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
    title.Name = "Title"
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 22)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 5)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "Orbit Tools - CMD"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 13
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.ZIndex = 3
    title.Parent = topbar

    local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", topbar)
    subtitle.Name = "Subtitle"
    subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 14)
    subtitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 26)
    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    subtitle.Text = "Your Private Agent Services Panel"
    subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 153)
    subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    subtitle.TextSize = 9
    subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    subtitle.ZIndex = 3

    for index = 1, 3 do
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", topbar)
        dot.Name = "WindowDot"
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14 - ((index - 1) * 12), 0.5, -3.5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(75, 75, 77)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 3
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    end

    local cmdInput
    local dragStart
    local frameStart
    local dragging = false
    local activeTouch
    local function isPointerOverAaGui(pointer)
        for _, guiObject in ipairs(plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(pointer.X, pointer.Y)) do
            local ancestor = guiObject
            while ancestor and not ancestor:IsA("ScreenGui") do
                ancestor = ancestor.Parent
            end

            if ancestor and ancestor.Enabled
                and (ancestor.Name == "CatlolClientationGui"
                    or ancestor.Name == "CatlolTerminalGui"
                    or ancestor.Name == "CatlolLauncherGui") then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    trackOrbitConnection(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end

        local pointer = input.Position
        local framePosition = frame.AbsolutePosition
        local frameSize = frame.AbsoluteSize
        local insideFrame = pointer.X >= framePosition.X
            and pointer.X <= framePosition.X + frameSize.X
            and pointer.Y >= framePosition.Y
            and pointer.Y <= framePosition.Y + frameSize.Y
        if not insideFrame then
            return
        end

        if isPointerOverAaGui(pointer) then
            return
        end

        local inputPosition = cmdInput.AbsolutePosition
        local inputSize = cmdInput.AbsoluteSize
        local insideCommandInput = pointer.X >= inputPosition.X
            and pointer.X <= inputPosition.X + inputSize.X
            and pointer.Y >= inputPosition.Y
            and pointer.Y <= inputPosition.Y + inputSize.Y
        if insideCommandInput then
            return
        end

        dragging = true
        dragStart = pointer
        frameStart = frame.Position
        activeTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input or nil

        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dragging = false
                activeTouch = nil
            end
        end)
    end))

    trackOrbitConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        local isMouseMove = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        local isActiveTouch = activeTouch ~= nil and input == activeTouch
        if dragging and (isMouseMove or isActiveTouch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                frameStart.X.Scale,
                frameStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
                frameStart.Y.Scale,
                frameStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end))

    local matrixUpdateElapsed = 0
    trackOrbitConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        if getgenv().AaNoAnimation == true then
            return
        end
        if not screenGui.Parent then
            return
        end

        matrixUpdateElapsed += deltaTime
        if matrixUpdateElapsed < 1 / 30 then
            return
        end
        deltaTime = matrixUpdateElapsed
        matrixUpdateElapsed = 0

        local layerHeight = matrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
        for _, stream in ipairs(matrixStreams) do
            stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
            stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
            stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)

            if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.16 then
                stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
                stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = randomMatrixDigit()
                stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % matrixCharacterCount + 1
            end

            if stream.Y > layerHeight then
                stream.Y = -matrixCharacterCount * matrixLineHeight
                stream.Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(32, 70)
                for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
                    digit.Text = randomMatrixDigit()
                end
            end
        end
    end))

    local inputContainer = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    inputContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 35)
    inputContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -35)
    inputContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 16)
    inputContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
    inputContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    inputContainer.ZIndex = 2
    Instance.new("UICorner", inputContainer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local commandBarGlass = Instance.new("UIGradient", inputContainer)
    commandBarGlass.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 35)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 16)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 25)),
    })
    commandBarGlass.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.42),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.12),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.36),
    })
    
    local commandPrompt = Instance.new("TextLabel", inputContainer)
    commandPrompt.Name = "CommandPrompt"
    commandPrompt.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 35)
    commandPrompt.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
    commandPrompt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    commandPrompt.Text = ">"
    commandPrompt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 237)
    commandPrompt.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    commandPrompt.TextSize = 13
    commandPrompt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    commandPrompt.ZIndex = 3

    local suggestionLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", inputContainer)
    suggestionLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0)
    suggestionLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
    suggestionLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    suggestionLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
    suggestionLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    suggestionLabel.TextSize = 13
    suggestionLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    suggestionLabel.Text = ""
    suggestionLabel.ZIndex = 3

    cmdInput = Instance.new("TextBox", inputContainer)
    cmdInput.Name = "CommandInput"
    cmdInput.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0)
    cmdInput.Position = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
    cmdInput.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    cmdInput.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    cmdInput.PlaceholderText = "type a command..."
    cmdInput.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
    cmdInput.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    cmdInput.TextSize = 13
    cmdInput.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    cmdInput.Text = ""
    cmdInput.ZIndex = 4
    cmdInput.ClearTextOnFocus = false

    helpFrame = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    helpFrame.Name = "CommandDirectory"
    helpFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -87)
    helpFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 52)
    helpFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 16)
    helpFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.16
    helpFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    helpFrame.Visible = true
    helpFrame.Active = true
    helpFrame.ZIndex = 2
    Instance.new("UICorner", helpFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    
    local helpTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", helpFrame)
    helpTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 28)
    helpTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4)
    helpTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    helpTitle.Text = "Command List - By Clarencelaleng234"
    helpTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
    helpTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    helpTitle.TextSize = 10
    helpTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    helpTitle.ZIndex = 3

    local scrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", helpFrame)
    scrollFrame.Name = "CommandList"
    scrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -44)
    scrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 36)
    scrollFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 16)
    scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 156)
    scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scrollFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scrollFrame.ZIndex = 3
    Instance.new("UICorner", scrollFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local scrollPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding", scrollFrame)
    scrollPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    scrollPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    scrollPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    scrollPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", scrollFrame)
    listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local selectedCommandArrow
    for c, desc in pairs(cmdDocs) do
        local entry = Instance.new("TextButton", scrollFrame)
        entry.Name = c .. "Command"
        entry.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
        entry.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 38)
        entry.BackgroundTransparency = 0.96
        entry.BorderSizePixel = 0
        entry.AutoButtonColor = false
        entry.Text = ""
        entry.LayoutOrder = table.find(cmds, c) or 0
        entry.ZIndex = 4
        Instance.new("UICorner", entry).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        local commandArrow = Instance.new("TextLabel", entry)
        commandArrow.Name = "SelectedIndicator"
        commandArrow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 32)
        commandArrow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)
        commandArrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        commandArrow.Text = ">"
        commandArrow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
        commandArrow.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        commandArrow.TextSize = 12
        commandArrow.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        commandArrow.TextTransparency = 0.75
        commandArrow.Visible = false
        commandArrow.ZIndex = 5

        local eTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", entry)
        eTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0)
        eTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0)
        eTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        eTitle.Text = desc
        eTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 238)
        eTitle.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        eTitle.TextSize = 12
        eTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        eTitle.ZIndex = 5

        helpEntries[c] = entry
        entry.Activated:Connect(function()
            if selectedCommandArrow then
                selectedCommandArrow.Visible = false
            end
            selectedCommandArrow = commandArrow
            commandArrow.TextTransparency = 0.55
            commandArrow.Visible = true

            local requiresArgument = c == "offset" or c == "speed" or c == "rot"
                or c == "mode" or c == "target" or c == "lerp" or c == "notify"
            cmdInput.Text = c .. (requiresArgument and " " or "")
            cmdInput:CaptureFocus()
            cmdInput.CursorPosition = #cmdInput.Text + 1
        end)

        entry.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            entry.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8
            commandArrow.TextTransparency = selectedCommandArrow == commandArrow and 0.55 or 0.75
            commandArrow.Visible = true
        end)
        entry.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            entry.BackgroundTransparency = 0.96
            local selected = selectedCommandArrow == commandArrow
            commandArrow.TextTransparency = selected and 0.55 or 0.75
            commandArrow.Visible = selected
        end)
    end

    local isUpdating = false
    local currentSuggestionCommand = ""
    
    cmdInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if isUpdating then return end
        local txt = cmdInput.Text
        if txt == "" then suggestionLabel.Text = ""; currentSuggestionCommand = ""; return end
        
        local spaceHit = txt:sub(-1) == " "
        local evalTxt = spaceHit and txt:sub(1, -2) or txt
        local args = evalTxt:split(" ")
        
        local tCmd = args[1]:lower()
        local match = getPartialMatch(tCmd)
        
        currentSuggestionCommand = ""

        if spaceHit and #args == 1 and match then
            isUpdating = true
            cmdInput.Text = match .. " "
            cmdInput.CursorPosition = #cmdInput.Text + 1
            suggestionLabel.Text = match .. " "
            isUpdating = false
            return
        end

        if match then
            if #args == 1 then
                local doc = cmdDocs[match] or match
                suggestionLabel.Text = txt .. doc:sub(#tCmd + 1)
                currentSuggestionCommand = match
            elseif #args == 2 and match == "target" and args[2] ~= "" then
                local sName = args[2]:lower()
                local found = nil
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #sName) == sName then found = p.Name break end
                    if p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #sName) == sName then found = p.DisplayName break end
                end
                
                if found then
                    currentSuggestionCommand = args[1] .. " " .. found
                    suggestionLabel.Text = args[1] .. " " .. args[2] .. found:sub(#sName + 1)
                    
                    if spaceHit then
                        isUpdating = true
                        cmdInput.Text = currentSuggestionCommand .. " "
                        cmdInput.CursorPosition = #cmdInput.Text + 1
                        suggestionLabel.Text = cmdInput.Text
                        isUpdating = false
                    end
                else
                    suggestionLabel.Text = ""
                end
            else
                suggestionLabel.Text = ""
            end
        else
            suggestionLabel.Text = ""
        end
    end)

    cmdInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
        if enter and cmdInput.Text:gsub(" ", "") ~= "" then
            local finalCmd = cmdInput.Text
            
            -- If we have an active autofill ready and what the user typed matches the start of it
            if currentSuggestionCommand and currentSuggestionCommand ~= "" then
                if currentSuggestionCommand:lower():sub(1, #finalCmd) == finalCmd:lower() then
                    finalCmd = currentSuggestionCommand
                end
            end

            processCommandString(finalCmd)
            if selectedCommandArrow then
                selectedCommandArrow.Visible = false
                selectedCommandArrow = nil
            end
            
            isUpdating = true
            cmdInput.Text = ""
            suggestionLabel.Text = ""
            currentSuggestionCommand = ""
            isUpdating = false
        end
    end)
end

createUI()

local characterToolConnection
local function bindCharacterTools(character)
    if characterToolConnection then
        characterToolConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    characterToolConnection = trackOrbitConnection(character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            task.wait()
            setupTool(child)
        end
    end))
end

bindCharacterTools(chr)

for _, v in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do
    if v:IsA("Tool") then setupTool(v) end
end

trackOrbitConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    if not getgenv().OrbitToolsy then return end
    if not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then targetHRP = hrp end

    rot = rot + speed
    local time = os.clock()
    local numTools = #orbitParts

    for i, p in ipairs(orbitParts) do
        if p and p.Parent then
            local angle = math.rad(rot + (360 / numTools) * i)
            local targetCFrame = CFrame.new()

            if mode == 1 then
                targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
            elseif mode == 2 then
                targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * offset, math.sin(time * 2 + i) * 2, math.sin(angle) * offset)
            elseif mode == 3 then
                targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(angle, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
            elseif mode == 4 then
                targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetHRP.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
            elseif mode == 5 then
                targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * offset, math.sin(angle) * offset, math.sin(angle) * offset)
            elseif mode == 6 then
                targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(angle, 0, angle) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
            else
                local subType = mode % 8
                local safeSpeed = time * (1 + (mode % 5) / 10)

                if subType == 0 then
                    local petalCount = (mode % 5) + 3
                    local wave = math.sin(angle * petalCount + safeSpeed)
                    local currentOffset = offset + (wave * (offset / 3))
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(currentOffset, 0, 0)
                elseif subType == 1 then
                    local tiltX = math.rad((mode * 15) % 360) + (time * 0.5)
                    local tiltZ = math.rad((mode * 45) % 360)
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(tiltX, angle, tiltZ) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
                elseif subType == 2 then
                    local height = math.sin(angle + (time * 2)) * (offset * 0.8)
                    local twist = math.cos(safeSpeed + (i/2)) * 2
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset + twist, height, 0)
                elseif subType == 3 then
                    local noiseX = math.sin(angle * ((mode % 3) + 1))
                    local noiseY = math.cos(angle * ((mode % 2) + 1))
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(angle, 0, angle) * CFrame.new(offset * noiseX, offset * noiseY, 0)
                elseif subType == 4 then
                    local fig8X = math.cos(angle) * offset
                    local fig8Z = math.sin(angle * 2) * offset
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(fig8X, 0, fig8Z)
                elseif subType == 5 then
                    local spikeFreq = (mode % 4) + 3
                    local height = math.abs(math.sin(angle * spikeFreq)) * (offset * 0.8)
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset, height - (offset/2), 0)
                elseif subType == 6 then
                    local band = (i % 3)
                    local tiltAngle = math.rad(60 * band)
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(tiltAngle, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset, 0, 0)
                elseif subType == 7 then
                    local pulse = math.sin(safeSpeed * 2) * (offset * 0.4)
                    targetCFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(offset + pulse, 0, 0)
                end

                if mode > 20 then
                    local slowWobble = math.rad(math.sin(time) * 15)
                    targetCFrame = targetCFrame * CFrame.Angles(slowWobble, 0, slowWobble)
                end
            end

            local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-lerpSpeed * deltaTime)
            p.CFrame = p.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha)

            local h = handles[i]
            if h and movers[h] then
                local spinVar = (mode % 30)
                movers[h].angular.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, toolRotSpeed * (10 + spinVar), 0)
            end
        end
    end

    if sethiddenproperty then
        pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(plr, "SimulationRadius", math.huge) end)
    end
end))

local characterGripConnection
local function handleCharacter(character)
    local function destroyGrip()
        for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Name == "RightGrip" then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    destroyGrip()
    if characterGripConnection then
        characterGripConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    characterGripConnection = trackOrbitConnection(character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Name == "RightGrip" then
            obj:Destroy()
        end
    end))
end

if plr.Character then handleCharacter(plr.Character) end

local SETTINGS = {
    VelocityY = 220.290009,
    SimulationRadius = 2147483647
}

local lastHRPPos = hrp.Position
local hrpVel = Vector3.zero

trackOrbitConnection(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
    settings().Physics.AllowSleep = false
    plr.SimulationRadius = SETTINGS.SimulationRadius
end))

trackOrbitConnection(RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
    if not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then return end

    local currentTime = os.clock()
    local currentPos = hrp.Position
    if dt > 0 then hrpVel = (currentPos - lastHRPPos) / dt end
    lastHRPPos = currentPos

    local predictedHRPPos = currentPos + hrpVel * 0.1
    local antiSleep = Vector3.new(0, math.sin(currentTime * 15) * 0.0015, 0)
    local gravityAxis = SETTINGS.VelocityY + math.sin(currentTime)

    for _, h in ipairs(handles) do
        if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then
            local dir = predictedHRPPos - h.Position
            local xz = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
            local velXZ = Vector3.zero

            if xz.Magnitude > 0 then velXZ = xz.Unit * xz.Magnitude * 2 end
            h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velXZ.X, gravityAxis, velXZ.Z)
            h.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, math.huge)
            h.CFrame = h.CFrame + antiSleep
        end
    end
end))

local MAX_ORBIT_DISTANCE = 8

task.spawn(function()
    while getgenv().OrbitToolsy do
        task.wait(0.1)
        for i = #handles, 1, -1 do
            local h = handles[i]
            local orbitPart = orbitParts[i]
            if h and h.Parent and orbitPart then
                local separation = (h.Position - orbitPart.Position).Magnitude
                if separation > MAX_ORBIT_DISTANCE then
                    local tool = h.Parent
                    sendFlatNotification("Orbit Sync", "Resyncing: " .. tool.Name, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
                    if tool.Parent == chr then
                        tool.Parent = bp
                        task.wait()
                        if tool.Parent == bp then tool.Parent = chr end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop = function()
    getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
    getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = false

    for i = #handles, 1, -1 do
        local handle = handles[i]
        local tool = handle and handle.Parent
        if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") and bp.Parent then
            tool.Parent = bp
        end
        cleanupTool(handle)
    end

    for _, connection in ipairs(orbitServiceConnections) do
        connection:Disconnect()
    end
    table.clear(orbitServiceConnections)

    if getgenv().OrbitUI then
        if closeOrbitUI then
            closeOrbitUI()
        else
            getgenv().OrbitUI:Destroy()
            getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
        end
    end
    if getgenv().OrbitNotificationUI then
        getgenv().OrbitNotificationUI:Destroy()
        getgenv().OrbitNotificationUI = nil
    end
    getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop = nil
    getgenv().OrbitToolsyConnections = nil
    getgenv().OrbitNotificationContainer = nil
end

trackOrbitConnection(plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    for i = #handles, 1, -1 do
        cleanupTool(handles[i])
    end

    chr = character
    hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    bp = plr:WaitForChild("Backpack")
    targetHRP = hrp
    lastHRPPos = hrp.Position
    bindCharacterTools(character)
    handleCharacter(character)

    for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            setupTool(child)
        end
    end

    task.delay(0.5, function()
        if getgenv().OrbitToolsy and (not getgenv().OrbitUI or not getgenv().OrbitUI.Parent) then
            createUI()
        end
        getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = getgenv().OrbitToolsy == true
    end)
end))

getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = true
sendFlatNotification("System", "Tool Orbit V3 Initialized", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
]=]

local function makeAimSlider(name, order, minimum, maximum, initialValue, formatValue, callback)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Name = name:gsub("%W", "") .. "Slider"
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 48)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.LayoutOrder = order
	row.Parent = aimBotPage
	round(row, 8)
	applyGlass(row, 0.22)

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Size = UDim2.new(0, 72, 1, 0)
	title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = name
	title.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	title.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	title.TextSize = 11
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.Parent = row

	local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 20)
	valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, -10)
	valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	valueLabel.TextColor3 = colors.text
	valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	valueLabel.TextSize = 11
	valueLabel.Parent = row

	local bar = Instance.new("TextButton")
	bar.Name = "SliderBar"
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -132, 0, 12)
	bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 82, 0.5, -6)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bar.Text = ""
	bar.AutoButtonColor = false
	bar.Parent = row
	round(bar, 6)
	applyGlass(bar, 0.15)

	local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
	fill.BackgroundColor3 = colors.accent
	fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	fill.Parent = bar
	round(fill, 6)

	local value = initialValue
	local function setValue(nextValue)
		value = math.clamp(nextValue, minimum, maximum)
		local ratio = (value - minimum) / (maximum - minimum)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0)
		valueLabel.Text = formatValue(value)
		callback(value)
	end

	local function setFromInput(position)
		local width = bar.AbsoluteSize.X
		if width <= 0 then return end
		local ratio = math.clamp((position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
		setValue(minimum + ratio * (maximum - minimum))
	end

	local isDragging = false
	track(bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			isDragging = true
			setFromInput(input.Position)
		end
	end))
	track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if isDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setFromInput(input.Position)
		end
	end))
	track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			isDragging = false
		end
	end))

	setValue(initialValue)
	return row, setValue
end

local _, setAimFovRadius = makeAimSlider("FOV radius", 11, 30, 300, aimSettings.FOVRadius, function(value)
	return tostring(math.floor(value))
end, function(value)
	aimSettings.FOVRadius = value
	if aimFovCircle then
		aimFovCircle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(value * 2, value * 2)
	end
end)
