local function passesAimTeamCheck(targetPlayer)
	if not aimSettings.TeamCheck or not player.Team then return true end
	return targetPlayer.Team ~= player.Team
end

local function getAimTargetPart(targetPlayer)
	if not targetPlayer
		or not targetPlayer:IsA("Player")
		or targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players
		or targetPlayer == player
		or not passesAimTeamCheck(targetPlayer) then
		return nil
	end

	local character = targetPlayer.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not character or not humanoid or (aimSettings.AliveCheck and humanoid.Health <= 0) then
		return nil
	end

	local part = character:FindFirstChild(aimSettings.LockPart)
		or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
		return part, character
	end
	return nil
end

local function isAimTargetVisible(camera, part, character)
	if not aimSettings.WallCheck then
		return true
	end

	local ignoreList = { character }
	if player.Character then
		table.insert(ignoreList, player.Character)
	end
	return #camera:GetPartsObscuringTarget({ part.Position }, ignoreList) == 0
end

local function findAimTarget()
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then return nil end

	local mousePosition = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
	local nearestPlayer
	local nearestScreenDistance = aimSettings.FOVEnabled and aimSettings.FOVRadius or 2000

	for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local part, character = getAimTargetPart(targetPlayer)
		if part then
			local screenPosition, onScreen = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(part.Position)
			if onScreen then
				local screenDistance = (mousePosition - Vector2.new(screenPosition.X, screenPosition.Y)).Magnitude
				if screenDistance < nearestScreenDistance and isAimTargetVisible(camera, part, character) then
					nearestScreenDistance = screenDistance
					nearestPlayer = targetPlayer
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nearestPlayer
end

local function setAimAssistEnabled(enabled)
	if aimAssistEnabled == enabled then return end
	aimAssistEnabled = enabled
	setToggleVisual(aimEnabledKnob, enabled)
	aimFovCircle.Visible = enabled and aimSettings.FOVEnabled

	if enabled then
		aimAssistConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			local mousePosition = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			aimFovCircle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mousePosition.X, mousePosition.Y)

			if not aimRunning then return end
			local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not camera then return end

			local aimTargetPart, character = getAimTargetPart(aimTargetPlayer)
			if aimTargetPart then
				local screenPosition, onScreen = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(aimTargetPart.Position)
				local screenDistance = (mousePosition - Vector2.new(screenPosition.X, screenPosition.Y)).Magnitude
				local lockRadius = aimSettings.FOVEnabled and aimSettings.FOVRadius or 2000
				local outsideFov = screenDistance > lockRadius
				if not onScreen or outsideFov or not isAimTargetVisible(camera, aimTargetPart, character) then
					aimTargetPlayer = nil
					aimTargetPart = nil
				end
			end

			if not aimTargetPart then
				aimTargetPlayer = findAimTarget()
				aimTargetPart = getAimTargetPart(aimTargetPlayer)
			end

			if aimTargetPart then
				local desiredCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camera.CFrame.Position, aimTargetPart.Position)
				camera.CFrame = desiredCFrame
			end
		end)
	else
		aimRunning = false
		aimTargetPlayer = nil
		aimFovCircle.Visible = false
		if aimAssistConnection then
			aimAssistConnection:Disconnect()
			aimAssistConnection = nil
		end
	end
end

local function setAimOption(optionName, knob, value)
	aimSettings[optionName] = value
	setToggleVisual(knob, value)
	if optionName == "TeamCheck" and connections.setEspTeamCheck then
		connections.setEspTeamCheck(value)
	end
	if optionName == "FOVEnabled" then
		aimFovCircle.Visible = aimAssistEnabled and value
	end
end

track(aimEnabledRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setAimAssistEnabled(not aimAssistEnabled)
end))
track(aimTeamRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setAimOption("TeamCheck", aimTeamKnob, not aimSettings.TeamCheck)
end))
track(aimAliveRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setAimOption("AliveCheck", aimAliveKnob, not aimSettings.AliveCheck)
end))
track(aimWallRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setAimOption("WallCheck", aimWallKnob, not aimSettings.WallCheck)
end))
track(aimFovRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setAimOption("FOVEnabled", aimFovKnob, not aimSettings.FOVEnabled)
end))
local aimLockParts = { "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso" }
track(aimLockPartRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	local currentIndex = table.find(aimLockParts, aimSettings.LockPart) or 1
	local nextIndex = currentIndex % #aimLockParts + 1
	aimSettings.LockPart = aimLockParts[nextIndex]
	aimLockPartLabel.Text = "Lock Part: " .. aimSettings.LockPart
end))

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
		or not aimAssistEnabled
		or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
		return
	end

	aimRunning = true
	aimTargetPlayer = findAimTarget()
end))

track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
		aimRunning = false
		aimTargetPlayer = nil
	end
end))

local function fling()
	local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
	local character, rootPart, velocity, moveAmount = nil, nil, nil, 0.1

	while connections.hiddenFling do
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		character = lp.Character
		rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if rootPart then
			velocity = rootPart.Velocity
			rootPart.Velocity = velocity * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			rootPart.Velocity = velocity
			RunService.Stepped:Wait()
			rootPart.Velocity = velocity + Vector3.new(0, moveAmount, 0)
			moveAmount = -moveAmount
		end
	end
end

local setEspEnabled = (function()
	local espEnabled = false
	local espTeamCheckEnabled = aimSettings.TeamCheck
	local espPlayers = {}
	local espConnections = {}

	local function getEspTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		if targetPlayer.Team then
			return targetPlayer.Team.TeamColor.Color
		end
		return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	end

	local function passesEspTeamCheck(targetPlayer)
		return not espTeamCheckEnabled
			or (player.Team ~= nil and targetPlayer.Team ~= nil and targetPlayer.Team ~= player.Team)
	end

	local function clearEspCharacter(record)
		for _, connection in ipairs(record.characterConnections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		table.clear(record.characterConnections)
		if record.visuals then
			record.visuals:Destroy()
			record.visuals = nil
		end
		if record.billboard then
			record.billboard:Destroy()
			record.billboard = nil
		end
		record.character = nil
		record.nameLabel = nil
		record.humanoid = nil
		record.highlight = nil
	end

	local function updateEspTeamColor(targetPlayer, record)
		if not record.visuals then return end
		local teamColor = getEspTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		record.teamColor = teamColor
		if record.highlight then
			record.highlight.FillColor = teamColor
			record.highlight.OutlineColor = teamColor
		end
		if record.nameLabel then
			record.nameLabel.TextColor3 = teamColor
		end
	end

	local function createEspNameTag(targetPlayer, record, head)
		if record.billboard or not head:IsA("BasePart") then return end
		local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboard.Name = "ESPNameTag"
		billboard.Adornee = head
		billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 30)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
		billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboard.Parent = head

		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel.Name = "TagLabel"
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.TextColor3 = record.teamColor
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		textLabel.TextSize = 14
		textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
		textLabel.Text = targetPlayer.Name
		textLabel.Parent = billboard
		record.billboard = billboard
		record.nameLabel = textLabel
	end

	local function updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, character)
		clearEspCharacter(record)
		if not espEnabled
			or targetPlayer == player
			or not passesEspTeamCheck(targetPlayer)
			or not character
			or not character.Parent then
			return
		end

		record.character = character
		record.teamColor = getEspTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		record.visuals = Instance.new("Folder")
		record.visuals.Name = "LocalPlayerESP"
		record.visuals.Parent = character

		local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
		highlight.Name = "ESPHighlight"
		highlight.Adornee = character
		highlight.FillColor = record.teamColor
		highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
		highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
		highlight.OutlineColor = record.teamColor
		highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		highlight.Parent = record.visuals
		record.highlight = highlight

		record.humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
			createEspNameTag(targetPlayer, record, head)
		end

		table.insert(record.characterConnections, character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if record.character ~= character then return end
			if descendant:IsA("Humanoid") then
				record.humanoid = descendant
			elseif descendant.Name == "Head" and descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				createEspNameTag(targetPlayer, record, descendant)
			end
		end))
	end

	local function removeEspPlayer(targetPlayer)
		local record = espPlayers[targetPlayer]
		if not record then return end
		clearEspCharacter(record)
		for _, connection in ipairs(record.connections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		espPlayers[targetPlayer] = nil
	end

	local function addEspPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if not espEnabled or targetPlayer == player or espPlayers[targetPlayer] then return end

		local record = {
			connections = {},
			characterConnections = {},
			visuals = nil,
		}
		espPlayers[targetPlayer] = record

		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, character)
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character)
			if record.character == character then
				clearEspCharacter(record)
			end
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
			updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TeamColor"):Connect(function()
			record.teamColor = getEspTeamColor(targetPlayer)
			updateEspTeamColor(targetPlayer, record)
		end))

		if targetPlayer.Character then
			updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
		end
	end

	local function updateEspLabels()
		local localCharacter = player.Character
		local localRoot = localCharacter and (
			localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			or localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			or localCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		)
		for targetPlayer, record in pairs(espPlayers) do
			local character = record.character
			local root = character and (
				character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
			)
			local humanoid = record.humanoid
				or (character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
			if record.nameLabel and root and localRoot and humanoid then
				local distance = math.floor((localRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude)
				local health = math.floor(humanoid.Health)
				record.nameLabel.Text = string.format("%s | Health: %d | Studs: %d", targetPlayer.Name, health, distance)
			end
		end
	end

	local function setEnabled(enabled)
		if espEnabled == enabled then return end
		espEnabled = enabled
		connections.combatEspEnabled = enabled
		setToggleVisual(espKnob, enabled)

		if enabled then
			local labelUpdateElapsed = 0
			table.insert(espConnections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
				labelUpdateElapsed += deltaTime
				if labelUpdateElapsed < 0.2 then return end
				labelUpdateElapsed = 0
				updateEspLabels()
			end))
			for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				addEspPlayer(targetPlayer)
			end
			table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(addEspPlayer))
			table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeEspPlayer))
			table.insert(espConnections, player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
				for targetPlayer, record in pairs(espPlayers) do
					updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
				end
			end))
		else
			for _, connection in ipairs(espConnections) do
				connection:Disconnect()
			end
			table.clear(espConnections)
			for targetPlayer in pairs(espPlayers) do
				removeEspPlayer(targetPlayer)
			end
		end
	end

	connections.setEspTeamCheck = function(enabled)
		espTeamCheckEnabled = enabled
		connections.combatEspTeamCheckEnabled = enabled
		if espEnabled then
			for targetPlayer, record in pairs(espPlayers) do
				updateEspCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
			end
		end
		if connections.refreshEspHitboxTeamCheck then
			connections.refreshEspHitboxTeamCheck()
		end
	end

	track(espRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setEnabled(not espEnabled)
	end))
	return setEnabled
end)()

;(function()
	local enabled = false
	local playerRecords = {}
	local serviceConnections = {}
	local boxTransparency = 0.3
	local espHitboxRow, espHitboxKnob = makeRow("ESP Hitbox", 4, false, aimBotPage)

	local function shouldShowPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if targetPlayer == player then return false end
		if connections.combatEspTeamCheckEnabled ~= true then return true end
		return player.Team ~= nil
			and targetPlayer.Team ~= nil
			and targetPlayer.Team ~= player.Team
	end

	local function getTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		return targetPlayer.Team and targetPlayer.Team.TeamColor.Color
			or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	end

	local function clearCharacter(record)
		for _, connection in ipairs(record.characterConnections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		table.clear(record.characterConnections)
		if record.visuals then
			record.visuals:Destroy()
			record.visuals = nil
		end
		table.clear(record.partVisuals)
		record.character = nil
		record.teamColor = nil
	end

	local function addPartVisual(record, part)
		if not record.visuals
			or part.Parent ~= record.character
			or not part:IsA("BasePart")
			or part.Name == "Handle"
			or record.partVisuals[part] then
			return
		end

		local adornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
		adornment.Name = "ESPHitbox"
		adornment.Adornee = part
		adornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
		adornment.ZIndex = 10
		adornment.Size = part.Size
		adornment.Transparency = boxTransparency
		adornment.Color3 = record.teamColor
		adornment.Parent = record.visuals
		record.partVisuals[part] = adornment

		table.insert(record.characterConnections, part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(function()
			if record.partVisuals[part] == adornment and adornment.Parent then
				adornment.Size = part.Size
			end
		end))
	end

	local function updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, character)
		clearCharacter(record)
		if not enabled or not shouldShowPlayer(targetPlayer) or not character or not character.Parent then
			return
		end

		record.character = character
		record.teamColor = getTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		record.partVisuals = {}
		record.visuals = Instance.new("Folder")
		record.visuals.Name = "LocalPlayerESPHitbox"
		record.visuals.Parent = character

		for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			addPartVisual(record, child)
		end
		table.insert(record.characterConnections, character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			addPartVisual(record, child)
		end))
		table.insert(record.characterConnections, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			local adornment = record.partVisuals[child]
			if adornment then
				adornment:Destroy()
				record.partVisuals[child] = nil
			end
		end))
	end

	local function updatePlayerTeamColor(targetPlayer, record)
		record.teamColor = getTeamColor(targetPlayer)
		for _, adornment in pairs(record.partVisuals or {}) do
			if adornment.Parent then
				adornment.Color3 = record.teamColor
			end
		end
	end

	local function removePlayer(targetPlayer)
		local record = playerRecords[targetPlayer]
		if not record then return end
		clearCharacter(record)
		for _, connection in ipairs(record.connections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		playerRecords[targetPlayer] = nil
	end

	local function addPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if not enabled or targetPlayer == player or playerRecords[targetPlayer] then return end
		local record = {
			connections = {},
			characterConnections = {},
			partVisuals = {},
		}
		playerRecords[targetPlayer] = record

		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, character)
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character)
			if record.character == character then
				clearCharacter(record)
			end
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
			updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
		end))
		table.insert(record.connections, targetPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TeamColor"):Connect(function()
			updatePlayerTeamColor(targetPlayer, record)
		end))

		if targetPlayer.Character then
			updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
		end
	end

	local function setEnabled(value)
		if enabled == value then return end
		enabled = value
		connections.combatEspHitboxEnabled = value
		setToggleVisual(espHitboxKnob, value)

		if enabled then
			for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				addPlayer(targetPlayer)
			end
			table.insert(serviceConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(addPlayer))
			table.insert(serviceConnections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removePlayer))
			table.insert(serviceConnections, player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
				for targetPlayer, record in pairs(playerRecords) do
					updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
				end
			end))
		else
			for _, connection in ipairs(serviceConnections) do
				connection:Disconnect()
			end
			table.clear(serviceConnections)
			for targetPlayer in pairs(playerRecords) do
				removePlayer(targetPlayer)
			end
		end
	end

	connections.refreshEspHitboxTeamCheck = function()
		if not enabled then return end
		for targetPlayer, record in pairs(playerRecords) do
			updateCharacter(targetPlayer, record, targetPlayer.Character)
		end
	end
	connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled = setEnabled

	track(espHitboxRow.Activated:Connect(function()
		setEnabled(not enabled)
	end))
end)()

local function initializeCombatEnhancements()
	local hitboxEnabled = false
	local hitboxScale = 3
	local hitboxTargetMode = "Head"
	local hitboxTargetModes = { "Head", "Body", "All" }
	local hitboxOriginals = {}
	local playerConnections = {}
	local playerCollisionRecords = {}
	local playerAddedConnection
	local playerRemovingConnection
	local hitboxHeartbeatConnection
	local localCharacterAddedConnection
	local localCharacterDescendantConnection
	local hitboxRow, hitboxKnob = makeRow("Hitbox Expander", 2, false, aimBotPage)

	local function isCharacterPart(instance)
		return instance:IsA("BasePart")
	end

	local function addPlayerNoCollisionPair(record, localPart, targetPart)
		if not localPart or not targetPart or not localPart.Parent or not targetPart.Parent then
			return
		end
		local targetParts = record.pairs[localPart]
		if not targetParts then
			targetParts = {}
			record.pairs[localPart] = targetParts
		end
		if targetParts[targetPart] then return end

		local constraint = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
		constraint.Name = "PlayerNoCollision"
		constraint.Part0 = localPart
		constraint.Part1 = targetPart
		constraint.Parent = localPart
		targetParts[targetPart] = constraint
		record.constraints[constraint] = true
	end

	local function clearPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
		local record = playerCollisionRecords[targetPlayer]
		if not record then return end
		for _, connection in ipairs(record.connections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		for constraint in pairs(record.constraints) do
			constraint:Destroy()
		end
		playerCollisionRecords[targetPlayer] = nil
	end

	local function setupPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
		clearPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
		if not hitboxEnabled or targetPlayer == player then return end
		local localCharacter = player.Character
		local targetCharacter = targetPlayer.Character
		if not localCharacter or not targetCharacter then return end

		local record = {
			pairs = {},
			constraints = {},
			connections = {},
		}
		playerCollisionRecords[targetPlayer] = record

		local localParts = {}
		for _, descendant in ipairs(localCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if isCharacterPart(descendant) then
				localParts[#localParts + 1] = descendant
			end
		end
		for _, descendant in ipairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if isCharacterPart(descendant) then
				for _, localPart in ipairs(localParts) do
					addPlayerNoCollisionPair(record, localPart, descendant)
				end
			end
		end

		table.insert(record.connections, targetCharacter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if not isCharacterPart(descendant) then return end
			for _, localPart in ipairs(localParts) do
				addPlayerNoCollisionPair(record, localPart, descendant)
			end
		end))
	end

	local function restoreHitboxParts(targetPlayer)
		local originals = hitboxOriginals[targetPlayer]
		if originals then
			for part, properties in pairs(originals) do
				if part.Parent then
					part.Size = properties.Size
					part.Transparency = properties.Transparency
					part.Color = properties.Color
					part.Material = properties.Material
					part.CanCollide = properties.CanCollide
				end
			end
			hitboxOriginals[targetPlayer] = nil
		end
	end

	local function restorePlayer(targetPlayer)
		restoreHitboxParts(targetPlayer)
		clearPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
	end

	local function applyHitbox(targetPlayer)
		if not hitboxEnabled or targetPlayer == player then return end
		local character = targetPlayer.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			restoreHitboxParts(targetPlayer)
			return
		end

		local originals = hitboxOriginals[targetPlayer]
		if not originals then
			originals = {}
			hitboxOriginals[targetPlayer] = originals
		end

		local selectedParts = {}
		for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			local isBodyPart = part:IsA("BasePart")
				and part.Name ~= "Handle"
				and not part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")
				and not part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
			local isSelected = isBodyPart and (
				hitboxTargetMode == "All"
				or (hitboxTargetMode == "Head" and part.Name == "Head")
				or (hitboxTargetMode == "Body" and part.Name ~= "Head" and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart")
			)
			if isSelected then
				selectedParts[part] = true
			end
		end

		for part, properties in pairs(originals) do
			if not selectedParts[part] then
				if part.Parent then
					part.Size = properties.Size
					part.Transparency = properties.Transparency
					part.Color = properties.Color
					part.Material = properties.Material
					part.CanCollide = properties.CanCollide
				end
				originals[part] = nil
			end
		end

		for part in pairs(selectedParts) do
			local properties = originals[part]
			if not properties then
				properties = {
					Size = part.Size,
					Transparency = part.Transparency,
					Color = part.Color,
					Material = part.Material,
					CanCollide = part.CanCollide,
				}
				originals[part] = properties
			end
			part.Size = properties.Size * hitboxScale
			part.Transparency = 0.6
			part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
			part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		end
	end

	local function watchPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if targetPlayer == player or playerConnections[targetPlayer] then return end
		local characterConnections = {}
		playerConnections[targetPlayer] = characterConnections
		table.insert(characterConnections, targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			restorePlayer(targetPlayer)
			task.defer(function()
				setupPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
			end)
		end))
		table.insert(characterConnections, targetPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
			clearPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
			restorePlayer(targetPlayer)
		end))
		if targetPlayer.Character then
			setupPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
		end
	end

	local function setHitboxEnabled(enabled)
		if hitboxEnabled == enabled then return end
		hitboxEnabled = enabled
		connections.combatHitboxEnabled = enabled
		setToggleVisual(hitboxKnob, enabled)
		if enabled then
			for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				watchPlayer(targetPlayer)
				applyHitbox(targetPlayer)
			end
			playerAddedConnection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer)
			playerRemovingConnection = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(targetPlayer)
				restorePlayer(targetPlayer)
				local connectionsForPlayer = playerConnections[targetPlayer]
				if connectionsForPlayer then
					for _, connection in ipairs(connectionsForPlayer) do
						connection:Disconnect()
					end
					playerConnections[targetPlayer] = nil
				end
			end)
			local function watchLocalCharacter(character)
				if localCharacterDescendantConnection then
					localCharacterDescendantConnection:Disconnect()
					localCharacterDescendantConnection = nil
				end
				localCharacterDescendantConnection = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
					if not isCharacterPart(descendant) then return end
					for targetPlayer, record in pairs(playerCollisionRecords) do
						local targetCharacter = targetPlayer.Character
						if targetCharacter then
							for _, targetPart in ipairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
								if isCharacterPart(targetPart) then
									addPlayerNoCollisionPair(record, descendant, targetPart)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if targetPlayer ~= player then
						setupPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
					end
				end
			end
			localCharacterAddedConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchLocalCharacter)
			if player.Character then
				watchLocalCharacter(player.Character)
			end
			hitboxHeartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					applyHitbox(targetPlayer)
					if targetPlayer ~= player and not playerCollisionRecords[targetPlayer] then
						setupPlayerNoCollision(targetPlayer)
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			if playerAddedConnection then
				playerAddedConnection:Disconnect()
				playerAddedConnection = nil
			end
			if playerRemovingConnection then
				playerRemovingConnection:Disconnect()
				playerRemovingConnection = nil
			end
			if hitboxHeartbeatConnection then
				hitboxHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
				hitboxHeartbeatConnection = nil
			end
			if localCharacterAddedConnection then
				localCharacterAddedConnection:Disconnect()
				localCharacterAddedConnection = nil
			end
			if localCharacterDescendantConnection then
				localCharacterDescendantConnection:Disconnect()
				localCharacterDescendantConnection = nil
			end
			for targetPlayer, connectionsForPlayer in pairs(playerConnections) do
				for _, connection in ipairs(connectionsForPlayer) do
					connection:Disconnect()
				end
				playerConnections[targetPlayer] = nil
			end
			for targetPlayer in pairs(hitboxOriginals) do
				restorePlayer(targetPlayer)
			end
		end
	end

	track(hitboxRow.Activated:Connect(function()
		setHitboxEnabled(not hitboxEnabled)
	end))

	local hitboxTargetRow, _, hitboxTargetLabel = makeRow("Hitbox target: Head", 10, true, aimBotPage)
	hitboxTargetRow.Action.Text = "NEXT"
	local function setHitboxTargetMode(mode)
		if not table.find(hitboxTargetModes, mode) then
			return
		end
		hitboxTargetMode = mode
		hitboxTargetLabel.Text = "Hitbox target: " .. mode
	end
	track(hitboxTargetRow.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentIndex = table.find(hitboxTargetModes, hitboxTargetMode) or 1
		local nextIndex = currentIndex % #hitboxTargetModes + 1
		setHitboxTargetMode(hitboxTargetModes[nextIndex])
	end))

	local _, setHitboxScale = makeAimSlider(
		"Hitbox scale",
		12,
		1,
		10,
		hitboxScale,
		function(value)
			return string.format("%.1fx", value)
		end,
		function(value)
			hitboxScale = value
			if hitboxEnabled then
				for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					applyHitbox(targetPlayer)
				end
			end
		end
	)

	local xrayEnabled = false
	local xrayRow, xrayKnob = makeRow("X-Ray Vision", 4, false, aimBotPage)
	local xrayOriginals = {}
	local xrayConnection
	local xrayUpdateElapsed = 0
	local xrayRadius = 150
	local xrayTransparency = 0.8
	local xrayOverlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
	xrayOverlapParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	xrayOverlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
	xrayOverlapParams.MaxParts = 0

	local function restoreXrayParts()
		for part, originalModifier in pairs(xrayOriginals) do
			if part.Parent then
				part.LocalTransparencyModifier = originalModifier
			end
			xrayOriginals[part] = nil
		end
	end

	local function updateXrayParts()
		local character = player.Character
		local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then
			restoreXrayParts()
			return
		end

		local inRange = {}
		for _, part in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(root.Position, xrayRadius, xrayOverlapParams)) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude <= xrayRadius then
				inRange[part] = true
				if xrayOriginals[part] == nil then
					xrayOriginals[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = xrayTransparency
				end
			end
		end

		for part, originalModifier in pairs(xrayOriginals) do
			if not inRange[part] then
				if part.Parent then
					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = originalModifier
				end
				xrayOriginals[part] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	local function setXrayEnabled(enabled)
		if xrayEnabled == enabled then return end
		xrayEnabled = enabled
		connections.combatXrayEnabled = enabled
		setToggleVisual(xrayKnob, enabled)
		if enabled then
			xrayUpdateElapsed = 0
			updateXrayParts()
			xrayConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
				xrayUpdateElapsed += deltaTime
				if xrayUpdateElapsed < 0.2 then return end
				xrayUpdateElapsed = 0
				updateXrayParts()
			end)
		else
			if xrayConnection then
				xrayConnection:Disconnect()
				xrayConnection = nil
			end
			restoreXrayParts()
		end
	end

	track(xrayRow.Activated:Connect(function()
		setXrayEnabled(not xrayEnabled)
	end))

	connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled = setHitboxEnabled
	connections.setCombatHitboxScale = setHitboxScale
	connections.setCombatHitboxTargetMode = setHitboxTargetMode
	connections.setCombatXrayEnabled = setXrayEnabled
end
initializeCombatEnhancements()

for columnIndex = 1, 26 do
	local stream = Instance.new("Frame")
	stream.Name = "MatrixStream"
	stream.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 60)
	stream.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((columnIndex - 1) * 16, 0)
	stream.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	stream.BorderSizePixel = 0
	stream.ZIndex = 50
	stream.Parent = terminalMatrixLayer

	local digits = {}
	for digitIndex = 1, 5 do
		local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		digit.Name = "Digit"
		digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, terminalMatrixLineHeight)
		digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * terminalMatrixLineHeight)
		digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
		digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		digit.Text = terminalMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
		digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
		digit.TextTransparency = math.min(0.9, 0.18 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.085)
		digit.TextSize = 10
		digit.ZIndex = 50
		digit.Parent = stream
		digits[digitIndex] = digit
	end
	table.insert(terminalMatrixStreams, {
		Frame = stream,
		Digits = digits,
		Y = terminalMatrixRandom:NextNumber(-60, 360),
		Speed = terminalMatrixRandom:NextNumber(24, 52),
		RefreshElapsed = 0,
		RefreshIndex = terminalMatrixRandom:NextInteger(1, 5),
	})
end

connections.refreshTerminalMatrix = function()
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	for _, stream in ipairs(terminalMatrixStreams) do
		for digitIndex, instance in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
			instance.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
			instance.TextTransparency = math.min(0.9, 0.18 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.085)
		end
	end
end

track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	if not terminalPanel.Visible then return end
	local layerHeight = terminalMatrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
	for _, stream in ipairs(terminalMatrixStreams) do
		stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
		stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
		stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
		if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.2 then
			stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
			stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = terminalMatrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
			stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
		end
		if stream.Y > layerHeight then
			stream.Y = -60
			stream.Speed = terminalMatrixRandom:NextNumber(24, 52)
		end
	end
end))

local terminalDocuments = {
	{ Name = "Clientation", Text = "", CursorPosition = 1, Protected = true },
}
local terminalTabButtons = {}
local activeTerminalDocument = 1

local function getUniqueTerminalTabName(requestedName, ignoredDocument)
	local name = requestedName:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	if name == "" then
		return nil
	end
	for _, document in ipairs(terminalDocuments) do
		if document ~= ignoredDocument and document.Name:lower() == name:lower() then
			return nil
		end
	end
	return name
end

local function refreshTerminalTabs()
	for index, tabRecord in ipairs(terminalTabButtons) do
		local selected = index == activeTerminalDocument
		tabRecord.Button.TextColor3 = colors.text
		tabRecord.Button.Text = (selected and "> " or "  ") .. tabRecord.Document.Name
		guiEffects.CreateTween(tabRecord.Row, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			BackgroundColor3 = selected and colors.surfaceHover or colors.surface,
			BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.67 or 0.85,
		}):Play()
	end
end

terminalTabMenuStroke.Transparency = 0.12

local terminalTabMenuGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")

	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, colors.header),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, colors.surface),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colors.header),
})

		RefreshElapsed = 0,
		RefreshIndex = 1,
	})
end

local terminalTabMenuTarget
local terminalTabMenuAction
local terminalTabMenuOpen = false
local terminalTabMenuTween
local terminalTabMenuChildTweens = {}
local terminalTabRenameInProgress = false

local function setTerminalTabMenuOpen(open)
	if terminalTabMenuTween then
		terminalTabMenuTween:Cancel()
		terminalTabMenuTween = nil
	end
	for _, tween in ipairs(terminalTabMenuChildTweens) do
		tween:Cancel()
	end
	table.clear(terminalTabMenuChildTweens)

	terminalTabMenuOpen = open
	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(
		open and 0.2 or 0.16,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
		open and Enum.EasingDirection.Out or Enum.EasingDirection.In
	)
	local function playTween(instance, properties)
		local tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(instance, tweenInfo, properties)
		table.insert(terminalTabMenuChildTweens, tween)
		tween:Play()
		return tween
	end

	if open then
		terminalTabMenu.Visible = true
		terminalTabMenuScale.Scale = 0.92
		terminalTabMenu.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		terminalTabMenuStroke.Transparency = 1
		terminalTabMenuTopbar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		terminalTabMenuTopbarFill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		terminalTabMenuTitle.TextTransparency = 1
		playTween(terminalTabMenuScale, { Scale = 1 })
		playTween(terminalTabMenu, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.12 })
		playTween(terminalTabMenuStroke, { Transparency = 0.12 })
		playTween(terminalTabMenuTopbar, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.1 })
		playTween(terminalTabMenuTopbarFill, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.1 })
		playTween(terminalTabMenuTitle, { TextTransparency = 0 })
		for _, actionButton in pairs(terminalTabMenuButtons) do
			local targetTransparency = actionButton.Active and 0.22 or 0.65
			actionButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			actionButton.TextTransparency = 1
			playTween(actionButton, {
				BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency,
				TextTransparency = 0,
			})
		end
		return
	end

	if not terminalTabMenu.Visible then
		return
	end
	local closingTween = playTween(terminalTabMenuScale, { Scale = 0.92 })
	playTween(terminalTabMenu, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	playTween(terminalTabMenuStroke, { Transparency = 1 })
	playTween(terminalTabMenuTopbar, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	playTween(terminalTabMenuTopbarFill, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	playTween(terminalTabMenuTitle, { TextTransparency = 1 })
	for _, actionButton in pairs(terminalTabMenuButtons) do
		playTween(actionButton, {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			TextTransparency = 1,
		})
	end
	terminalTabMenuTween = closingTween
	closingTween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
		if terminalTabMenuTween == closingTween and not terminalTabMenuOpen
			and playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
			terminalTabMenu.Visible = false
			terminalTabMenuTween = nil
		end
	end)
end

local function closeTerminalTabMenu()
	setTerminalTabMenuOpen(false)
	terminalTabMenuTarget = nil
end

local function isPointInsideGui(guiObject, point)
	if not guiObject or not guiObject.Parent or not guiObject.Visible then
		return false
	end
	local position = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
	local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize
	return point.X >= position.X
		and point.X <= position.X + size.X
		and point.Y >= position.Y
		and point.Y <= position.Y + size.Y
end

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if not terminalTabMenuOpen or terminalTabRenameInProgress then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		return
	end

	local pointer = input.Position
	task.defer(function()
		if not terminalTabMenuOpen or terminalTabRenameInProgress
			or isPointInsideGui(terminalTabMenu, pointer)
			or (terminalTabMenuTarget and isPointInsideGui(terminalTabMenuTarget.MenuButton, pointer)) then
			return
		end
		closeTerminalTabMenu()
	end)
end))

track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	if not terminalTabMenu.Visible then
		return
	end
	for _, stream in ipairs(terminalTabMenuStreams) do
		stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
		stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
		stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
		if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.18 then
			stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
			stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text = terminalTabMenuRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
			stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
		end
		if stream.Y > terminalTabMenu.AbsoluteSize.Y then
			stream.Y = -42
			stream.Speed = terminalTabMenuRandom:NextNumber(18, 34)
		end
	end
end))

local function selectTerminalDocument(index)
	if not terminalDocuments[index] then return end
	if terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument] then
		terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument].Text = terminalEditor.Text
		terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument].CursorPosition = terminalEditor.CursorPosition
	end
	activeTerminalDocument = index
	local document = terminalDocuments[index]
	terminalEditor.Text = document.Text
	refreshTerminalTabs()
	terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	terminalLineViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	task.defer(function()
		if terminalEditor.Parent and terminalEditor:IsFocused() then
			terminalEditor.CursorPosition = math.clamp(document.CursorPosition, 1, #document.Text + 1)
		end
	end)
end

local function showTerminalTabMenu(record)
	terminalTabMenuTarget = record
	local protected = record.Document.Protected == true
	terminalTabMenuTitle.Text = "Menu Card : " .. record.Document.Name
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Rename.Text = protected and "Rename  [LOCKED]" or "Rename"
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Delete.Text = protected and "Delete  [LOCKED]" or "Delete"
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Rename.TextColor3 = protected and colors.muted or colors.text
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Delete.TextColor3 = protected and colors.muted or Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 150)
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Rename.Active = not protected
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Rename.Selectable = not protected
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Delete.Active = not protected
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Delete.Selectable = not protected
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Rename.BackgroundTransparency = protected and 0.65 or 0.22
	terminalTabMenuButtons.Delete.BackgroundTransparency = protected and 0.65 or 0.22
	local rowPosition = record.Row.AbsolutePosition - terminalPanel.AbsolutePosition
	local popupX = rowPosition.X + record.Row.AbsoluteSize.X - terminalTabMenu.AbsoluteSize.X
	local popupY = rowPosition.Y + record.Row.AbsoluteSize.Y + 4
	popupX = math.clamp(
		popupX,
		4,
		math.max(4, terminalPanel.AbsoluteSize.X - terminalTabMenu.AbsoluteSize.X - 4)
	)
	if popupY + terminalTabMenu.AbsoluteSize.Y > terminalPanel.AbsoluteSize.Y - 4 then
		popupY = rowPosition.Y - terminalTabMenu.AbsoluteSize.Y - 4
	end
	terminalTabMenu.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(popupX, math.max(4, popupY))
	terminalTabRenameInProgress = false
	setTerminalTabMenuOpen(true)
end

local createTerminalTab
local updateTerminalEditorLayout

local function deleteTerminalDocument(document)
	if document.Protected then
		return
	end
	local deletingIndex = table.find(terminalDocuments, document)
	if not deletingIndex then
		return
	end
	if terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument] then
		terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument].Text = terminalEditor.Text
		terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument].CursorPosition = terminalEditor.CursorPosition
	end

	local deletingActive = activeTerminalDocument == deletingIndex
	table.remove(terminalDocuments, deletingIndex)
	local nextActiveIndex = activeTerminalDocument
	if deletingActive then
		nextActiveIndex = math.min(deletingIndex, #terminalDocuments)
	elseif activeTerminalDocument > deletingIndex then
		nextActiveIndex -= 1
	end

	for _, tabRecord in ipairs(terminalTabButtons) do
		for _, connection in ipairs(tabRecord.Connections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
		tabRecord.Row:Destroy()
	end
	table.clear(terminalTabButtons)
	activeTerminalDocument = math.max(1, nextActiveIndex)
	for index, remainingDocument in ipairs(terminalDocuments) do
		createTerminalTab(remainingDocument, index)
	end
	local nextDocument = terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument]
	if nextDocument then
		terminalEditor.Text = nextDocument.Text
		terminalEditor.CursorPosition = math.clamp(nextDocument.CursorPosition, 1, #nextDocument.Text + 1)
	end
	refreshTerminalTabs()
	updateTerminalEditorLayout()
	closeTerminalTabMenu()
end

createTerminalTab = function(document, index)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Name = "ScriptTabRow" .. index
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 28)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.LayoutOrder = index
	row.ZIndex = 53
	row.Parent = terminalScriptList
	round(row, 6)
	applyGlass(row, 0.18)

	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Name = "Select"
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0)
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Text = document.Name
	button.TextColor3 = colors.text
	button.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	button.TextSize = 9
	button.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	button.ZIndex = 54
	button.Parent = row
	round(button, 6)
	local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
	padding.Parent = button

	local menuButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	menuButton.Name = "Options"
	menuButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	menuButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
	menuButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)
	menuButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	menuButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	menuButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	menuButton.Text = "..."
	menuButton.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	menuButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	menuButton.TextSize = 13
	menuButton.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	menuButton.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	menuButton.ZIndex = 55
	menuButton.Parent = row

	local renameBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
	renameBox.Name = "RenameInput"
	renameBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -4)
	renameBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 2)
	renameBox.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
	renameBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
	renameBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
	renameBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	renameBox.Text = document.Name
	renameBox.TextColor3 = colors.text
	renameBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	renameBox.TextSize = 9
	renameBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	renameBox.Visible = false
	renameBox.ZIndex = 55
	renameBox.Parent = row
	round(renameBox, 4)
	applyGlass(renameBox, 0.05)

	local record = {
		Button = button,
		MenuButton = menuButton,
		Document = document,
		Row = row,
		RenameBox = renameBox,
		Connections = {},
	}
	terminalTabButtons[index] = record
	table.insert(record.Connections, button.Activated:Connect(function()
		selectTerminalDocument(index)
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, menuButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if terminalTabMenu.Visible and terminalTabMenuTarget == record then
			closeTerminalTabMenu()
		else
			showTerminalTabMenu(record)
		end
	end))
	table.insert(record.Connections, renameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		terminalTabRenameInProgress = false
		local name = getUniqueTerminalTabName(renameBox.Text, document)
		if name then
			document.Name = name
			refreshTerminalTabs()
		else
			warn("Tab name must be non-empty and unique.")
			renameBox.Text = document.Name
		end
		if renameBox.Parent then
			renameBox.Visible = false
		end
		closeTerminalTabMenu()
	end))
end

terminalTabMenuAction = function(actionName)
	local record = terminalTabMenuTarget
	if not record then
		return
	end
	local index = table.find(terminalDocuments, record.Document)
	if not index then
		closeTerminalTabMenu()
		return
	end
	if actionName == "Select" then
		closeTerminalTabMenu()
		selectTerminalDocument(index)
	elseif actionName == "Rename" then
		if record.Document.Protected then
			return
		end
		terminalTabRenameInProgress = true
		record.RenameBox.Text = record.Document.Name
		record.RenameBox.Visible = true
		record.RenameBox:CaptureFocus()
		record.RenameBox.CursorPosition = #record.RenameBox.Text + 1
	elseif actionName == "Delete" then
		deleteTerminalDocument(record.Document)
	end
end

for actionName, actionButton in pairs(terminalTabMenuButtons) do
	local currentActionName = actionName
	track(actionButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if terminalTabMenuAction then
			terminalTabMenuAction(currentActionName)
		end
	end))
	track(actionButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		actionButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
	end))
	track(actionButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		actionButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
	end))
end

for index, document in ipairs(terminalDocuments) do
	createTerminalTab(document, index)
end
refreshTerminalTabs()

local function createTerminalDocument()
	local index = #terminalDocuments + 1
	local nameIndex = 1
	local name = "Script " .. nameIndex
	while not getUniqueTerminalTabName(name) do
		nameIndex += 1
		name = "Script " .. nameIndex
	end
	local document = {
		Name = name,
		Text = "",
		CursorPosition = 1,
	}
	table.insert(terminalDocuments, document)
	createTerminalTab(document, index)
	selectTerminalDocument(index)
	terminalScriptList.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
		0,
		math.max(0, terminalScriptList.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - terminalScriptList.AbsoluteSize.Y)
	)
end

local terminalOutputLines = {}
local function escapeRichText(text)
	return text:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;")
end

local function formatHighlightedCode(source)
	if not terminalSyntaxEnabled then
		return escapeRichText(source)
	end
	local palette = {
		keyword = "#C792EA",
		string = "#C3E88D",
		comment = "#697386",
		number = "#F78C6C",
		global = "#82AAFF",
	}
	local keywords = {
		["and"] = true, ["break"] = true, ["continue"] = true, ["do"] = true,
		["else"] = true, ["elseif"] = true, ["end"] = true, ["false"] = true,
		["for"] = true, ["function"] = true, ["if"] = true, ["in"] = true,
		["local"] = true, ["nil"] = true, ["not"] = true, ["or"] = true,
		["repeat"] = true, ["return"] = true, ["then"] = true, ["true"] = true,
		["until"] = true, ["while"] = true,
	}
	local globals = {
		game = true, workspace = true, script = true, Enum = true, Instance = true,
		Vector2 = true, Vector3 = true, CFrame = true, Color3 = true, UDim2 = true,
		Players = true, task = true, math = true, string = true, table = true,
	}
	local output = {}
	local function appendToken(token, color)
		local escaped = escapeRichText(token)
		if color then
			table.insert(output, '<font color="' .. color .. '">' .. escaped .. "</font>")
		else
			table.insert(output, escaped)
		end
	end

	local index = 1
	while index <= #source do
		local current = source:sub(index, index)
		local nextTwo = source:sub(index, index + 1)
		if nextTwo == "--" then
			local newline = source:find("\n", index, true)
			local finish = newline and newline - 1 or #source
			appendToken(source:sub(index, finish), palette.comment)
			index = finish + 1
		elseif current == '"' or current == "'" then
			local finish = index + 1
			while finish <= #source do
				local char = source:sub(finish, finish)
				if char == "\\" then
					finish = finish + 2
				elseif char == current then
					finish = finish + 1
					break
				else
					finish = finish + 1
				end
			end
			finish = math.min(finish - 1, #source)
			appendToken(source:sub(index, finish), palette.string)
			index = finish + 1
		elseif current:match("%s") then
			local finish = index + 1
			while finish <= #source and source:sub(finish, finish):match("%s") do
				finish = finish + 1
			end
			appendToken(source:sub(index, finish - 1))
			index = finish
		elseif current:match("[%a_]") then
			local token = source:sub(index):match("^[%a_][%w_]*")
			local color = keywords[token] and palette.keyword or globals[token] and palette.global
			appendToken(token, color)
			index = index + #token
		elseif current:match("%d") then
			local token = source:sub(index):match("^%d[%w_%.]*")
			appendToken(token, palette.number)
			index = index + #token
		else
			appendToken(current)
			index = index + 1
		end
	end
	return table.concat(output)
end

updateTerminalEditorLayout = function()
	local source = terminalEditor.Text
	local lineCount = 1
	local longestLine = 0
	local currentLineLength = 0
	for index = 1, #source do
		if source:sub(index, index) == "\n" then
			lineCount += 1
			longestLine = math.max(longestLine, currentLineLength)
			currentLineLength = 0
		else
			currentLineLength += 1
		end
	end
	longestLine = math.max(longestLine, currentLineLength)

	local numbers = table.create(lineCount)
	for line = 1, lineCount do
		numbers[line] = tostring(line)
	end
	terminalLineNumbers.Text = table.concat(numbers, "\n")
	if source == "" then
		terminalSyntaxLabel.Text = '<font color="#697386">-- Type Luau code here</font>'
	else
		terminalSyntaxLabel.Text = formatHighlightedCode(source)
	end

	local editorTextHeight = math.max(
		lineCount * 16,
		terminalEditorViewport.AbsoluteSize.Y - 12
	)
	local contentHeight = editorTextHeight + 12
	local contentWidth = math.max(terminalEditorViewport.AbsoluteSize.X, longestLine * 8 + 16)
	terminalEditorViewport.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(contentWidth, contentHeight)
	terminalLineViewport.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(terminalLineViewport.AbsoluteSize.X, contentHeight)
	terminalLineNumbers.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 6)
	terminalLineNumbers.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, editorTextHeight)
	terminalLineDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0)
	terminalSyntaxLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6)
	terminalSyntaxLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(1, contentWidth - 16), 0, editorTextHeight)
	terminalEditor.Position = terminalSyntaxLabel.Position
	terminalEditor.Size = terminalSyntaxLabel.Size
end

local function keepTerminalCursorVisible()
	if not terminalEditor:IsFocused() or terminalEditor.CursorPosition < 1 then return end
	local cursorText = terminalEditor.Text:sub(1, terminalEditor.CursorPosition - 1)
	local line = 1
	local column = 0
	for index = 1, #cursorText do
		if cursorText:sub(index, index) == "\n" then
			line += 1
			column = 0
		else
			column += 1
		end
	end

	local canvasPosition = terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition
	local visibleHeight = terminalEditorViewport.AbsoluteSize.Y
	local visibleWidth = terminalEditorViewport.AbsoluteSize.X
	local cursorY = (line - 1) * 16 + 6
	local cursorX = column * 8 + 8
	local nextY = canvasPosition.Y
	local nextX = canvasPosition.X
	if cursorY < nextY then
		nextY = cursorY
	elseif cursorY + 16 > nextY + visibleHeight then
		nextY = cursorY + 16 - visibleHeight
	end
	if cursorX < nextX then
		nextX = cursorX
	elseif cursorX + 8 > nextX + visibleWidth then
		nextX = cursorX + 8 - visibleWidth
	end
	terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(math.max(0, nextX), math.max(0, nextY))
end
