local terminalCompletionMatches = {}
local terminalCompletionTokenStart = 1
local terminalCompletionTokenEnd = 0
local terminalCompletionSelectedIndex = 1
local terminalCompletionApplying = false
local terminalCompletionActionName = "AaTerminalCompletion_" .. tostring(aaGeneration)

local function updateTerminalCompletionSelection()
	for index, itemButton in ipairs(terminalCompletionButtons) do
		local selected = index == terminalCompletionSelectedIndex
		itemButton.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.15 or 1
		itemButton.TextColor3 = selected and colors.accent or colors.text
	end
end

local function hideTerminalCompletions()
	terminalCompletionPopup.Visible = false
	table.clear(terminalCompletionMatches)
end

local function isTerminalCodePosition(source, cursor)
	local quote
	local escaped = false
	local inComment = false
	local index = 1
	while index < cursor do
		local character = source:sub(index, index)
		local nextCharacter = source:sub(index + 1, index + 1)
		if inComment then
			if character == "\n" then
				inComment = false
			end
		elseif quote then
			if escaped then
				escaped = false
			elseif character == "\\" then
				escaped = true
			elseif character == quote then
				quote = nil
			end
		elseif character == "-" and nextCharacter == "-" then
			inComment = true
			index += 1
		elseif character == '"' or character == "'" or character == "`" then
			quote = character
		end
		index += 1
	end
	return not quote and not inComment
end

local function refreshTerminalCompletions()
	if terminalCompletionApplying or not terminalEditor:IsFocused() or not terminalPanel.Visible then
		hideTerminalCompletions()
		return
	end

	local source = terminalEditor.Text
	local cursor = terminalEditor.CursorPosition
	if cursor < 1 then
		hideTerminalCompletions()
		return
	end
	if not isTerminalCodePosition(source, cursor) then
		hideTerminalCompletions()
		return
	end

	local beforeCursor = source:sub(1, cursor - 1)
	local prefix = beforeCursor:match("([%w_]+)$") or ""
	local tokenStart = cursor - #prefix
	local previousCharacter = source:sub(tokenStart - 1, tokenStart - 1)
	local isMemberCompletion = previousCharacter == "." or previousCharacter == ":"
	if prefix == "" and not isMemberCompletion then
		hideTerminalCompletions()
		return
	end

	local candidates = isMemberCompletion and terminalMemberCompletionItems or terminalCompletionItems
	table.clear(terminalCompletionMatches)
	for _, item in ipairs(candidates) do
		local candidate = item[1]
		if candidate:lower():sub(1, #prefix) == prefix:lower() and candidate ~= prefix then
			table.insert(terminalCompletionMatches, item)
			if #terminalCompletionMatches == #terminalCompletionButtons then
				break
			end
		end
	end

	if #terminalCompletionMatches == 0 then
		hideTerminalCompletions()
		return
	end

	terminalCompletionTokenStart = tokenStart
	terminalCompletionTokenEnd = cursor - 1
	terminalCompletionSelectedIndex = 1
	local popupHeight = #terminalCompletionMatches * 22
	terminalCompletionPopup.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(188, popupHeight)
	for index, itemButton in ipairs(terminalCompletionButtons) do
		local item = terminalCompletionMatches[index]
		itemButton.Visible = item ~= nil
		if item then
			itemButton.LayoutOrder = index
			itemButton.Text = item[1] .. "    " .. item[2]
		end
	end
	updateTerminalCompletionSelection()

	local line = 1
	local column = 0
	for index = 1, cursor - 1 do
		if source:sub(index, index) == "\n" then
			line += 1
			column = 0
		else
			column += 1
		end
	end
	local canvasPosition = terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition
	local popupX = 158 + column * 8 - canvasPosition.X
	local popupY = 50 + (line - 1) * 16 - canvasPosition.Y + 17
	popupX = math.clamp(popupX, 8, terminalPanel.AbsoluteSize.X - 196)
	popupY = math.clamp(popupY, 40, terminalPanel.AbsoluteSize.Y - popupHeight - 8)
	terminalCompletionPopup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(popupX, popupY)
	terminalCompletionPopup.Visible = true
end

acceptTerminalCompletion = function(index)
	local item = terminalCompletionMatches[index]
	if not item then
		return false
	end

	local source = terminalEditor.Text
	local before = source:sub(1, terminalCompletionTokenStart - 1)
	local after = source:sub(terminalCompletionTokenEnd + 1)
	local completedText = before .. item[1] .. after
	local cursorPosition = #before + #item[1] + 1
	hideTerminalCompletions()
	terminalEditor:CaptureFocus()
	terminalCompletionApplying = true
	terminalEditor.Text = completedText
	task.defer(function()
		if terminalEditor.Parent and terminalEditor:IsFocused() then
			terminalEditor.CursorPosition = cursorPosition
		end
		terminalCompletionApplying = false
		refreshTerminalCompletions()
	end)
	return true
end

ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
	terminalCompletionActionName,
	function(_, inputState, inputObject)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin
			or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= terminalEditor
			or not terminalCompletionPopup.Visible then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end

		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Tab or inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return then
			acceptTerminalCompletion(terminalCompletionSelectedIndex)
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
			terminalCompletionSelectedIndex = (terminalCompletionSelectedIndex - 2) % #terminalCompletionMatches + 1
			updateTerminalCompletionSelection()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
			terminalCompletionSelectedIndex = terminalCompletionSelectedIndex % #terminalCompletionMatches + 1
			updateTerminalCompletionSelection()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
			hideTerminalCompletions()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end

		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end,
	false,
	Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 1,
	Enum.KeyCode.Tab,
	Enum.KeyCode.Return,
	Enum.KeyCode.Up,
	Enum.KeyCode.Down,
	Enum.KeyCode.Escape
)

connections.refreshTerminalSyntax = function()
	updateTerminalEditorLayout()
	refreshTerminalTabs()
	setToggleVisual(terminalSyntaxKnob, terminalSyntaxEnabled)
end
track(terminalEditor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	local document = terminalDocuments[activeTerminalDocument]
	if document then
		document.Text = terminalEditor.Text
		document.CursorPosition = terminalEditor.CursorPosition
	end
	updateTerminalEditorLayout()
	task.defer(keepTerminalCursorVisible)
	task.defer(refreshTerminalCompletions)
end))
track(terminalEditor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CursorPosition"):Connect(function()
	keepTerminalCursorVisible()
	task.defer(refreshTerminalCompletions)
end))
track(terminalEditorViewport:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateTerminalEditorLayout))
local terminalScrollSyncing = false
track(terminalEditorViewport:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
	if terminalScrollSyncing then return end
	terminalScrollSyncing = true
	terminalLineViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition.Y)
	terminalScrollSyncing = false
	task.defer(refreshTerminalCompletions)
end))
track(terminalLineViewport:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
	if terminalScrollSyncing then return end
	terminalScrollSyncing = true
	terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
		terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition.X,
		terminalLineViewport.CanvasPosition.Y
	)
	terminalScrollSyncing = false
	task.defer(refreshTerminalCompletions)
end))
track(terminalEditor.Focused:Connect(function()
	terminalSyntaxLabel.TextTransparency = 0
	terminalEditor.TextTransparency = 1
	task.defer(refreshTerminalCompletions)
end))
track(terminalEditor.FocusLost:Connect(function()
	updateTerminalEditorLayout()
	terminalSyntaxLabel.TextTransparency = 0
	terminalEditor.TextTransparency = 1
	task.defer(function()
		if not terminalEditor:IsFocused() then
			local pointer = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			local popupPosition = terminalCompletionPopup.AbsolutePosition
			local popupSize = terminalCompletionPopup.AbsoluteSize
			local pointerOverPopup = terminalCompletionPopup.Visible
				and pointer.X >= popupPosition.X
				and pointer.X <= popupPosition.X + popupSize.X
				and pointer.Y >= popupPosition.Y
				and pointer.Y <= popupPosition.Y + popupSize.Y
			if not pointerOverPopup then
				hideTerminalCompletions()
			end
		end
	end)
end))

local function writeTerminalOutput(text, isError)
	local outputLine = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	outputLine.Name = "OutputLine"
	outputLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0)
	outputLine.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	outputLine.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	outputLine.Text = tostring(text)
	outputLine.TextColor3 = isError and Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 130) or colors.text
	outputLine.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	outputLine.TextSize = 11
	outputLine.TextWrapped = true
	outputLine.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	outputLine.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	outputLine.ZIndex = 52
	outputLine.LayoutOrder = #terminalOutputLines + 1
	outputLine.Parent = terminalOutputFrame
	table.insert(terminalOutputLines, outputLine)
	if #terminalOutputLines > 30 then
		table.remove(terminalOutputLines, 1):Destroy()
		for index, line in ipairs(terminalOutputLines) do
			line.LayoutOrder = index
		end
	end
	task.defer(function()
		if terminalOutputFrame.Parent then
			terminalOutputFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
				0,
				math.max(0, terminalOutputLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y - terminalOutputFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			)
		end
	end)
end

local createOrbitLoadingNotice
local terminalLoading = false
local terminalLoadRequest = 0
connections.cancelPendingTerminalLoad = function()
	terminalLoadRequest += 1
	terminalLoading = false
end
local terminalRunBusy = false
local function runLocalTerminal()
	if terminalRunBusy then
		return
	end

	terminalRunBusy = true
	local source = terminalEditor.Text
	local compiler = loadstring
	if type(compiler) ~= "function" then
		local message = "loadstring is unavailable in this environment."
		warn("Error executing script: " .. message)
		writeTerminalOutput(message, true)
		terminalRunBusy = false
		return
	end

	local compileSucceeded, compiledScript, compileError = pcall(compiler, source)
	if not compileSucceeded or type(compiledScript) ~= "function" then
		local message = tostring(compileSucceeded and compileError or compiledScript)
		warn("Error executing script: " .. message)
		writeTerminalOutput(message, true)
		terminalRunBusy = false
		return
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local success, result = pcall(compiledScript)
		if not success then
			local message = tostring(result)
			warn("Error executing script: " .. message)
			writeTerminalOutput(message, true)
		else
			writeTerminalOutput("Script executed successfully.")
		end
		terminalRunBusy = false
	end)
end

local function openTerminalFromCommand()
	terminalLoadRequest += 1
	terminalLoading = false
	setTerminalOpen(true)
	setToggleVisual(terminalToggleKnob, true)
end

local function closeTerminalFromCommand()
	terminalLoadRequest += 1
	terminalLoading = false
	hideTerminalCompletions()
	closeTerminalTabMenu()
	setTerminalOpen(false)
	setToggleVisual(terminalToggleKnob, false)
end
connections.openTerminalFromCommand = openTerminalFromCommand
connections.closeTerminalFromCommand = closeTerminalFromCommand

track(terminalToggleRow.Activated:Connect(function()
	if terminalAnimation.Open then
		closeTerminalFromCommand()
		return
	end
	if terminalLoading then
		return
	end

	terminalLoading = true
	terminalLoadRequest += 1
	local request = terminalLoadRequest
	local generation = aaGeneration
	task.spawn(function()
		local setLoadingText, destroyLoadingNotice = createOrbitLoadingNotice("Terminal - GUI")
		for remaining = 5, 1, -1 do
			if request ~= terminalLoadRequest or getgenv().AaGeneration ~= generation or not screenGui.Parent then
				destroyLoadingNotice()
				if request == terminalLoadRequest then
					terminalLoading = false
				end
				return
			end
			setLoadingText("Please Wait... " .. remaining)
			task.wait(1)
		end

		if request == terminalLoadRequest and getgenv().AaGeneration == generation and screenGui.Parent then
			setLoadingText("Loading Terminal...")
			setTerminalOpen(true)
			setToggleVisual(terminalToggleKnob, true)
		end

		task.wait(0.8)
		destroyLoadingNotice()
		if request == terminalLoadRequest then
			terminalLoading = false
		end
	end)
end))
createOrbitLoadingNotice = function(titleText, placement)
	local loadingGui
	local container
	if placement == "top" then
		loadingGui = getgenv().AaCommandNotificationGui
		container = getgenv().AaCommandNotificationContainer
		if not loadingGui or not loadingGui.Parent or not container or not container.Parent then
			loadingGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			loadingGui.Name = "AaCommandNotificationGui"
			loadingGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
			loadingGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483647
			loadingGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
			loadingGui.Parent = playerGui

			container = Instance.new("Frame")
			container.Name = "NotificationContainer"
			container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 1, -20)
			container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 10)
			container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			container.ClipsDescendants = false
			container.Parent = loadingGui

			local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
			layout.Name = "NotificationLayout"
			layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
			layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
			layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
			layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
			layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
			layout.Parent = container

			getgenv().AaCommandNotificationGui = loadingGui
			getgenv().AaCommandNotificationContainer = container
		end
	else
		container = getgenv().OrbitNotificationContainer
		if not container or not container.Parent then
			loadingGui = getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationGui
			container = getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationContainer
			if not loadingGui or not loadingGui.Parent or not container or not container.Parent then
				loadingGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
				loadingGui.Name = "OrbitLoadingNotification"
				loadingGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
				loadingGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483643
				loadingGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
				loadingGui.Parent = playerGui

				container = Instance.new("Frame")
				container.Name = "NotificationContainer"
				container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 1, -20)
				container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 10)
				container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				container.ClipsDescendants = false
				container.Parent = loadingGui

				local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
				layout.Name = "NotificationLayout"
				layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
				layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
				layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
				layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
				layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
				layout.Parent = container

				getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationGui = loadingGui
				getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationContainer = container
			end
		end
	end
	local notificationScreenGui = container:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
	local originalDisplayOrder
	if notificationScreenGui then
		originalDisplayOrder = notificationScreenGui:GetAttribute("AaNotificationOriginalDisplayOrder")
		if originalDisplayOrder == nil then
			originalDisplayOrder = notificationScreenGui.DisplayOrder
			notificationScreenGui:SetAttribute("AaNotificationOriginalDisplayOrder", originalDisplayOrder)
		end
		notificationScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483647
	end

	local notice = Instance.new("Frame")
	notice.Name = "MatrixNotification"
	notice.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 58)
	notice.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 9)
	notice.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	notice.BorderSizePixel = 0
	notice.ClipsDescendants = true
	notice.ZIndex = 10
	notice.Parent = container
	getgenv().AaNotificationOrder = (getgenv().AaNotificationOrder or 0) + 1
	notice.LayoutOrder = getgenv().AaNotificationOrder
	round(notice, 8)

	local matrixLayer = Instance.new("Frame")
	matrixLayer.Name = "MatrixBackground"
	matrixLayer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	matrixLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	matrixLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
	matrixLayer.ClipsDescendants = true
	matrixLayer.ZIndex = 10
	matrixLayer.Parent = notice

	local matrixRandom = Random.new()
	local matrixStreams = {}
	for columnIndex = 1, 18 do
		local column = Instance.new("Frame")
		column.Name = "MatrixStream"
		column.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 36)
		column.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((columnIndex - 1) * 13, 0)
		column.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		column.BorderSizePixel = 0
		column.ZIndex = 10
		column.Parent = matrixLayer

		local digits = {}
		for digitIndex = 1, 4 do
			local digit = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			digit.Name = "Digit"
			digit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 9)
			digit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (digitIndex - 1) * 9)
			digit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			digit.BorderSizePixel = 0
			digit.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			digit.Text = matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
			digit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 215)
			digit.TextTransparency = 0.78 + (digitIndex - 1) * 0.04
			digit.TextSize = 8
			digit.ZIndex = 10
			digit.Parent = column
			digits[digitIndex] = digit
		end

		table.insert(matrixStreams, {
			Frame = column,
			Digits = digits,
			Y = matrixRandom:NextNumber(-36, 58),
			Speed = matrixRandom:NextNumber(14, 24),
			RefreshElapsed = 0,
			RefreshIndex = matrixRandom:NextInteger(1, #digits),
		})
	end

	local topbar = Instance.new("Frame")
	topbar.Name = "Topbar"
	topbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	topbar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 18)
	topbar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	topbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	topbar.ZIndex = 11
	topbar.Parent = notice
	round(topbar, 12)

	local topbarFill = Instance.new("Frame")
	topbarFill.Name = "BottomFill"
	topbarFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -10)
	topbarFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
	topbarFill.BackgroundColor3 = topbar.BackgroundColor3
	topbarFill.BackgroundTransparency = topbar.BackgroundTransparency
	topbarFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	topbarFill.ZIndex = 11
	topbarFill.Parent = topbar

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Name = "Title"
	title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0)
	title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 0)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = titleText or "Orbit Tools - Gui"
	title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
	title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	title.TextSize = 9
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	title.ZIndex = 12
	title.Parent = topbar

	for index = 1, 3 do
		local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
		dot.Name = "WindowDot"
		dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5)
		dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -9 - ((index - 1) * 9), 0.5, -2.5)
		dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(75, 75, 77)
		dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
		dot.ZIndex = 12
		dot.Parent = topbar
		round(dot, 999)
	end

	local message = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	message.Name = "Information"
	message.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -22)
	message.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 20)
	message.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	message.Text = "Please Wait..."
	message.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
	message.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	message.TextSize = 11
	message.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	message.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	message.TextWrapped = true
	message.ZIndex = 12
	message.Parent = notice

	local matrixConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
		if not notice.Parent then
			return
		end
		local layerHeight = matrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
		for _, stream in ipairs(matrixStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.2 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
					matrixRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > layerHeight then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			end
		end
	end)

	guiEffects.CreateTween(notice, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 58),
	}):Play()

	return function(text)
		if message.Parent then
			message.Text = text
		end
	end, function()
		local tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(notice, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 58),
		})
		tween:Play()
		tween.Completed:Wait()
		matrixConnection:Disconnect()
		notice:Destroy()
		if notificationScreenGui and notificationScreenGui.Parent then
			local hasNotices = false
			for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "MatrixNotification" then
					hasNotices = true
					break
				end
			end
			if not hasNotices then
				notificationScreenGui.DisplayOrder = originalDisplayOrder
				notificationScreenGui:SetAttribute("AaNotificationOriginalDisplayOrder", nil)
			end
		end
		local hasNotices = false
		for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "MatrixNotification" then
				hasNotices = true
				break
			end
		end
		if loadingGui and loadingGui.Parent and not hasNotices then
			loadingGui:Destroy()
			if placement == "top" then
				getgenv().AaCommandNotificationGui = nil
				getgenv().AaCommandNotificationContainer = nil
			else
				getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationGui = nil
				getgenv().AaLoadingNotificationContainer = nil
			end
		end
	end
end

local function isOrbitGuiLoaded()
	local orbitGui = getgenv().OrbitUI
	return getgenv().OrbitToolsy == true
		and getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady == true
		and orbitGui ~= nil
		and orbitGui.Parent ~= nil
end

local orbitEnabled = isOrbitGuiLoaded()
local orbitLoading = false
local orbitLoadRequest = 0
connections.cancelPendingOrbitLoad = function()
	orbitLoadRequest += 1
	orbitLoading = false
end
setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, orbitEnabled)
local function toggleOrbitPanel(skipCountdown)
	local orbitGuiLoaded = isOrbitGuiLoaded()
	if orbitLoading and not orbitGuiLoaded then
		return false, "Orbit panel is already loading."
	end

	if orbitEnabled or orbitGuiLoaded then
		orbitLoadRequest += 1
		orbitLoading = false
		local stopOrbit = getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop
		if type(stopOrbit) ~= "function" then
			local orbitGui = getgenv().OrbitUI
			if orbitGui and orbitGui.Parent then
				orbitGui:Destroy()
			end
			getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
			getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
			getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = false
			orbitEnabled = false
			setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, false)
			return true
		end

		local success, errorMessage = pcall(stopOrbit)
		if not success then
			warn("Failed to stop Solul Matrix: " .. tostring(errorMessage))
			return false, tostring(errorMessage)
		end
		if guiEffects.NoAnimation then
			local orbitGui = getgenv().OrbitUI
			if orbitGui and orbitGui.Parent then
				orbitGui:Destroy()
			end
			getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
		end
		getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
		getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = false
		orbitEnabled = false
	else
		orbitEnabled = false
		setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, false)
		orbitLoading = true
		orbitLoadRequest += 1
		local request = orbitLoadRequest

		task.spawn(function()
				local setLoadingText, destroyLoadingNotice = createOrbitLoadingNotice("Orbit Tools - Gui")
				local function finishLoading(success, errorMessage)
					if request ~= orbitLoadRequest then
						destroyLoadingNotice()
						return
					end
					if success then
						orbitEnabled = true
						setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, true)
						setLoadingText("Orbit Tools Loaded.")
					else
						orbitEnabled = false
						setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, false)
						setLoadingText("Gagal memuat GUI Orbit.")
						warn("Failed to start Solul Matrix: " .. tostring(errorMessage))
					end
					task.wait(1.2)
					destroyLoadingNotice()
					orbitLoading = false
				end

				if skipCountdown then
					setLoadingText("Opening Orbit panel...")
				else
					for remaining = 5, 1, -1 do
						if request ~= orbitLoadRequest then
							destroyLoadingNotice()
							return
						end
						setLoadingText("Please Wait... " .. remaining)
						task.wait(1)
					end
				end
				if request ~= orbitLoadRequest then
					destroyLoadingNotice()
					return
				end
				setLoadingText("Please Wait..")

				local executionFinished = getgenv().OrbitToolsy == true
				local executionSucceeded = executionFinished
				local executionError
				if executionFinished then
					if type(getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop) ~= "function" then
						finishLoading(false, "The active Orbit instance cannot be controlled.")
						return
					end
				else
					local compiler = loadstring
					if type(compiler) ~= "function" then
						finishLoading(false, "loadstring is unavailable in this environment.")
						return
					end

					local compileSucceeded, compiledScript, compileError = pcall(compiler, orbitScriptSource)
					if not compileSucceeded or type(compiledScript) ~= "function" then
						finishLoading(false, compileSucceeded and compileError or compiledScript)
						return
					end

					executionFinished = false
					task.spawn(function()
						executionSucceeded, executionError = pcall(compiledScript)
						executionFinished = true
					end)
				end

				local deadline = os.clock() + 30
				while os.clock() < deadline do
					if request ~= orbitLoadRequest then
						destroyLoadingNotice()
						return
					end
					if isOrbitGuiLoaded() then
						finishLoading(true)
						return
					end
					if executionFinished and not executionSucceeded then
						local stopOrbit = getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop
						if type(stopOrbit) == "function" then
							pcall(stopOrbit)
						else
							getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
						end
						finishLoading(false, executionError)
						return
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				local stopOrbit = getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop
				if type(stopOrbit) == "function" then
					pcall(stopOrbit)
				else
					getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
				end
				finishLoading(false, "Timed out while creating the Orbit GUI.")
		end)
		return true
	end

	setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, orbitEnabled)
	return true
end
track(orbitToggleRow.Activated:Connect(toggleOrbitPanel))

local function setOrbitPanelFromCommand(enabled)
	if enabled then
		if orbitEnabled then return true end
		if orbitLoading then return false, "Orbit panel is already loading." end
		return toggleOrbitPanel(true)
	end

	orbitLoadRequest += 1
	orbitLoading = false
	local stopOrbit = getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop
	if type(stopOrbit) == "function" then
		local success, errorMessage = pcall(stopOrbit)
		if not success then
			return false, "Failed to close Orbit panel: " .. tostring(errorMessage)
		end
	else
		getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
		getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = false
		local orbitGui = getgenv().OrbitUI
		if orbitGui and orbitGui.Parent then
			orbitGui:Destroy()
		end
		getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
	end
	orbitEnabled = false
	setToggleVisual(orbitToggleKnob, false)
	return true
end
connections.setOrbitPanelFromCommand = setOrbitPanelFromCommand
track(terminalRunButton.Activated:Connect(runLocalTerminal))
track(terminalAddTabButton.Activated:Connect(createTerminalDocument))
track(terminalSyntaxToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
	terminalSyntaxEnabled = not terminalSyntaxEnabled
	updateTerminalEditorLayout()
	setToggleVisual(terminalSyntaxKnob, terminalSyntaxEnabled)
end))
track(terminalClearButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	terminalEditor.Text = ""
	terminalEditorViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	terminalLineViewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	terminalEditor:CaptureFocus()
end))

local function connectTerminalButtonHover(button)
	track(button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
			BackgroundColor3 = colors.surfaceHover,
		}):Play()
	end))
	track(button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		guiEffects.CreateTween(button, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
			BackgroundColor3 = colors.track,
		}):Play()
	end))
end
connectTerminalButtonHover(terminalRunButton)
connectTerminalButtonHover(terminalClearButton)

local terminalDragging = false
local terminalDragStart
local terminalPositionStart
track(terminalHeader.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		terminalDragging = true
		terminalDragStart = input.Position
		terminalPositionStart = terminalPanel.Position
		track(input.Changed:Connect(function()
			if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
				terminalDragging = false
			end
		end))
	end
end))
track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if terminalDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
		local delta = input.Position - terminalDragStart
		terminalPanel.Position = UDim2.new(
			terminalPositionStart.X.Scale,
			terminalPositionStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
			terminalPositionStart.Y.Scale,
			terminalPositionStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
		)
	end
end))

updateTerminalEditorLayout()
writeTerminalOutput("Welcome Back Catlol Agent.")
end

	setFlySpeed(ratio * 200)

local function setWalkSpeedEnabled(enabled)
	local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then return false end
	if connections.walkSpeedEnabled == enabled then return true end

	connections.walkSpeedEnabled = enabled
	if enabled then
		connections.normalWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = connections.walkSpeed
	else
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = connections.normalWalkSpeed
	end
	setToggleVisual(walkSpeedKnob, enabled)
	return true
end

track(walkSpeedToggleRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setWalkSpeedEnabled(not connections.walkSpeedEnabled)
end))

connections.setCollision = function(character, canCollide)
	for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then
			part.CanCollide = canCollide
		end
	end
end

connections.stopFly = function()
	connections.flying = false
	setToggleVisual(flyKnob, false)
	if connections.flyConnection then
		connections.flyConnection:Disconnect()
		connections.flyConnection = nil
	end
	if connections.bodyVelocity then
		connections.bodyVelocity:Destroy()
		connections.bodyVelocity = nil
	end
	if connections.bodyGyro then
		connections.bodyGyro:Destroy()
		connections.bodyGyro = nil
	end
	if connections.currentHumanoid then
		connections.currentHumanoid.PlatformStand = false
		connections.currentHumanoid.AutoRotate = true
		connections.currentHumanoid = nil
	end
end

connections.startFly = function()
	local character = player.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root or not humanoid then return end

	connections.flying = true
	setToggleVisual(flyKnob, true)
	connections.currentHumanoid = humanoid

	connections.bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	connections.bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
	connections.bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	connections.bodyVelocity.Parent = root

	connections.bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	connections.bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
	connections.bodyGyro.P = 3000
	connections.bodyGyro.CFrame = root.CFrame
	connections.bodyGyro.Parent = root

	humanoid.PlatformStand = true
	humanoid.AutoRotate = false

	connections.flyConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not connections.flying or not root.Parent then
			connections.stopFly()
			return
		end

		local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then return end

		local forwardAmount = 0
		if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Up) then
			forwardAmount += 1
		end
		if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Down) then
			forwardAmount -= 1
		end
		local rightAmount = (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 1 or 0)
			- (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 1 or 0)

		local flatCameraForward = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		local flatCameraRight = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.RightVector.Z)
		if flatCameraForward.Magnitude > 0 then flatCameraForward = flatCameraForward.Unit end
		if flatCameraRight.Magnitude > 0 then flatCameraRight = flatCameraRight.Unit end

		if forwardAmount == 0 and rightAmount == 0 and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
			forwardAmount = humanoid.MoveDirection:Dot(flatCameraForward)
			rightAmount = humanoid.MoveDirection:Dot(flatCameraRight)
		end

		local moveVector = camera.CFrame.LookVector * forwardAmount
			+ camera.CFrame.RightVector * rightAmount

		local vertical = 0
		if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then vertical += 1 end
		if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then vertical -= 1 end
		moveVector += Vector3.new(0, vertical, 0)
		if moveVector.Magnitude > 0 then
			moveVector = moveVector.Unit * connections.flySpeed
		end

		connections.bodyVelocity.Velocity = moveVector
		connections.bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, root.Position + camera.CFrame.LookVector)
	end)
end

connections.clearVFlyMovers = function()
	if connections.vFlyVelocity then
		connections.vFlyVelocity:Destroy()
		connections.vFlyVelocity = nil
	end
	if connections.vFlyGyro then
		connections.vFlyGyro:Destroy()
		connections.vFlyGyro = nil
	end
	connections.vFlyTarget = nil
end

connections.stopVFly = function()
	connections.vFlying = false
	setToggleVisual(vFlyKnob, false)
	if connections.vFlyConnection then
		connections.vFlyConnection:Disconnect()
		connections.vFlyConnection = nil
	end
	connections.clearVFlyMovers()
end

connections.startVFly = function()
	local character = player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

	if connections.flying then
		connections.stopFly()
	end
	connections.vFlying = true
	setToggleVisual(vFlyKnob, true)

	connections.vFlyConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local currentCharacter = player.Character
		local currentHumanoid = currentCharacter and currentCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local currentRoot = currentCharacter and currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentHumanoid or not currentRoot or currentHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
			connections.stopVFly()
			return
		end
		if not camera then return end

		local seat = currentHumanoid.SeatPart
		local target = seat and seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") and seat or currentRoot
		if connections.vFlyTarget ~= target or not connections.vFlyVelocity or not connections.vFlyVelocity.Parent then
			connections.clearVFlyMovers()
			connections.vFlyTarget = target

			connections.vFlyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			connections.vFlyVelocity.Name = "ClientationVFlyVelocity"
			connections.vFlyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			connections.vFlyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			connections.vFlyVelocity.Parent = target

			connections.vFlyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
			connections.vFlyGyro.Name = "ClientationVFlyGyro"
			connections.vFlyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			connections.vFlyGyro.P = 3000
			connections.vFlyGyro.CFrame = target.CFrame
			connections.vFlyGyro.Parent = target
		end

		local hasMoveInput = currentHumanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0
			or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W)
			or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A)
			or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S)
			or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D)
		connections.vFlyVelocity.Velocity = hasMoveInput and camera.CFrame.LookVector * connections.vFlySpeed or Vector3.zero

		local cameraForward = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatLook = Vector3.new(cameraForward.X, 0, cameraForward.Z)
		if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.001 then
			flatLook = Vector3.new(target.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, target.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		end
		if flatLook.Magnitude > 0 then
			connections.vFlyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(target.Position, target.Position + flatLook.Unit)
		end
	end)
end

connections.stopNoclip = function()
	connections.noclipping = false
	setToggleVisual(noclipKnob, false)
	if connections.noclipConnection then
		connections.noclipConnection:Disconnect()
		connections.noclipConnection = nil
	end
	local character = player.Character
	if character then
		connections.setCollision(character, true)
	end
end

connections.startNoclip = function()
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	connections.noclipping = true
	setToggleVisual(noclipKnob, true)
	connections.noclipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
		local currentCharacter = player.Character
		if connections.noclipping and currentCharacter then
			connections.setCollision(currentCharacter, false)
		end
	end)
end

connections.setFlingEnabled = function(enabled)
	if connections.hiddenFling == enabled then return end
	connections.hiddenFling = enabled
	setToggleVisual(flingKnob, enabled)

	if enabled then
		connections.flingThread = coroutine.create(fling)
		coroutine.resume(connections.flingThread)
	end
end

track(flingRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	connections.setFlingEnabled(not connections.hiddenFling)
end))

connections.isSitting = function()
	local character = player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return humanoid ~= nil and humanoid.Sit
end

connections.flingCar = function()
	local character = Workspace:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
	local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not head or not humanoid then return false end

	local originalCFrame = head.CFrame
	head.CFrame = CFrame.new(-61.1931343, -300.03904629, -5.78899622, 1, -6.60259411e-06, -0.000170271436, 1.0472202e-07, 0.999269009, -0.0382303186, 0.00017039619, 0.0382303223, 0.999269009)
	task.wait(0.2)
	if connections.cleanupStarted or getgenv().AaGeneration ~= aaGeneration then
		if head.Parent then head.CFrame = originalCFrame end
		return false
	end
	humanoid.Jump = true
	task.wait(0.2)
	if connections.cleanupStarted or getgenv().AaGeneration ~= aaGeneration then
		if head.Parent then head.CFrame = originalCFrame end
		return false
	end
	head.CFrame = CFrame.new(-61.1931343, 5.03904629, -5.78899622, 1, -6.60259411e-06, -0.000170271436, 1.0472202e-07, 0.999269009, -0.0382303186, 0.00017039619, 0.0382303223, 0.999269009)
	return true
end

connections.runFlingCar = function()
	if connections.flingCarBusy then return end
	connections.flingCarBusy = true
	if connections.isSitting() then
		carLabel.Text = "Fling Car..."
		task.spawn(function()
			local succeeded = connections.flingCar()
			carLabel.Text = succeeded and "Fling Car" or "Character unavailable"
			task.wait(1)
			carLabel.Text = "Fling Car"
			connections.flingCarBusy = false
		end)
	else
		carLabel.Text = "Sit down first"
		task.delay(1, function()
			carLabel.Text = "Fling Car"
			connections.flingCarBusy = false
		end)
	end
end
track(carRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(connections.runFlingCar))

track(flyRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if connections.flying then
		connections.stopFly()
	else
		if connections.vFlying then connections.stopVFly() end
		connections.startFly()
	end
end))
track(vFlyRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if connections.vFlying then
		connections.stopVFly()
	else
		connections.startVFly()
	end
end))
track(noclipRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if connections.noclipping then
		connections.stopNoclip()
	else
		connections.startNoclip()
	end
end))

connections.initializeRadar = function()
local MAP_RANGE = 100
local DETECTION_RADIUS = 5000
local markers = {}

local radarHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
radarHolder.Name = "RadarHolder"
radarHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -34)
radarHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
radarHolder.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
radarHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
radarHolder.Parent = radarPage
round(radarHolder, 10)
applyGlass(radarHolder, 0.2)

local radarBackground = Instance.new("Frame")
radarBackground.Name = "RadarBackground"
radarBackground.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12)
radarBackground.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
radarBackground.BackgroundColor3 = colors.track
radarBackground.BorderSizePixel = 0
radarBackground.ClipsDescendants = true
radarBackground.Parent = radarHolder
round(radarBackground, 999)

local radarStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
radarStroke.Color = colors.border
radarStroke.Thickness = 1.2
radarStroke.Transparency = 0.35
radarStroke.Parent = radarBackground

for _, scale in ipairs({ 0.72, 0.48, 0.24 }) do
	local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
	ring.Size = UDim2.new(scale, 0, scale, 0)
	ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	ring.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ring.Parent = radarBackground
	round(ring, 999)
	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Color = colors.muted
	stroke.Thickness = 1
	stroke.Transparency = 0.5
	stroke.Parent = ring
end

local function makeRadarLine(size, position)
	local line = Instance.new("Frame")
	line.Size = size
	line.Position = position
	line.BackgroundColor3 = colors.border
	line.BorderSizePixel = 0
	line.Parent = radarBackground
	round(line, 999)
end

makeRadarLine(UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -0.5))
makeRadarLine(UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, -0.5, 0, 0))

local playerPointer = Instance.new("Frame")
playerPointer.Name = "PlayerPointer"
playerPointer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
playerPointer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
playerPointer.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
playerPointer.BackgroundColor3 = colors.accent
playerPointer.BorderSizePixel = 0
playerPointer.ZIndex = 11
playerPointer.Parent = radarBackground
round(playerPointer, 999)

local coordLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
coordLabel.Name = "Coordinates"
coordLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 25)
coordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, -27)
coordLabel.BackgroundColor3 = colors.surface
coordLabel.Text = "Coordinates: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)"
coordLabel.TextColor3 = colors.text
coordLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
coordLabel.TextSize = 11
coordLabel.Parent = radarPage
round(coordLabel, 8)
applyGlass(coordLabel, 0.2)

local function getHumanoidRoot(character)
	if not character then return nil end
	return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
end

local function getHumanoid(character)
	if not character then return nil end
	return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function getMarkerColor(character)
	local targetHumanoid = getHumanoid(character)
	if not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then return nil end

	local targetPlayer = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	if targetPlayer then
		if player.Team and targetPlayer.Team then
			return targetPlayer.Team == player.Team and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235) or Color3.fromRGB(125, 125, 127)
		end
		return Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 177)
	end
	if targetHumanoid.TeamColor and player.TeamColor then
		return targetHumanoid.TeamColor == player.TeamColor and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235) or Color3.fromRGB(125, 125, 127)
	end
	return Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 177)
end

local function clampToCircle(x, y, radius)
	local distance = math.sqrt(x * x + y * y)
	if distance <= radius or distance == 0 then return x, y end
	local angle = math.atan2(y, x)
	return math.cos(angle) * radius, math.sin(angle) * radius
end

local function createMarker(targetCharacter)
	local marker = Instance.new("TextButton")
	marker.Name = "PlayerMarker"
	marker.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
	marker.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	marker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
	marker.BorderSizePixel = 0
	marker.Text = ""
	marker.AutoButtonColor = false
	marker.ZIndex = 12
	marker.Parent = radarBackground
	round(marker, 999)
	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	stroke.Thickness = 1
	stroke.Transparency = 0.2
	stroke.Parent = marker

	local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	nameLabel.Name = "MarkerName"
	nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	nameLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 12)
	nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 8)
	nameLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
	nameLabel.Text = targetCharacter.Name
	nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	nameLabel.TextSize = 8
	nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	nameLabel.ZIndex = 13
	nameLabel.Parent = marker

	local targetPlayer = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetCharacter)
	if targetPlayer and targetPlayer ~= player then
		marker.Activated:Connect(function()
			local succeeded, status = teleportToPlayer(targetPlayer)
			if not succeeded then
				warn("Radar GOTO failed: " .. status)
			end
		end)
	end
	return marker
end

local function updateMarker(marker, targetCharacter)
	local localRoot = getHumanoidRoot(player.Character)
	local targetRoot = getHumanoidRoot(targetCharacter)
	if not localRoot or not targetRoot then
		marker.Visible = false
		return
	end

	local relative = targetRoot.Position - localRoot.Position
	local x, z = relative.X, relative.Z
	local distance = math.sqrt(x * x + z * z)
	if distance > DETECTION_RADIUS then
		marker.Visible = false
		return
	end

	marker.Visible = true
	local targetPlayer = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetCharacter)
	marker.MarkerName.Text = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Name or targetCharacter.Name
	local radius = math.min(radarBackground.AbsoluteSize.X, radarBackground.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 0.5 - 12
	if radius <= 0 then return end
	if distance > MAP_RANGE then
		local scale = MAP_RANGE / distance
		x, z = x * scale, z * scale
	end
	local screenX, screenY = clampToCircle((x / MAP_RANGE) * radius, -(z / MAP_RANGE) * radius, radius)
	marker.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, screenX, 0.5, screenY)
	marker.BackgroundColor3 = getMarkerColor(targetCharacter) or Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
end

local function refreshMarkers()
	local localRoot = getHumanoidRoot(player.Character)
	if not localRoot then
		for character, marker in pairs(markers) do
			marker:Destroy()
			markers[character] = nil
		end
		return
	end

	local activeTargets = {}
	for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local character = otherPlayer.Character
		local humanoid = getHumanoid(character)
		local root = getHumanoidRoot(character)
		if otherPlayer ~= player and humanoid and root and humanoid.Health > 0
			and (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= DETECTION_RADIUS then
			activeTargets[character] = true
		end
	end

	for character, marker in pairs(markers) do
		if not activeTargets[character] then
			marker:Destroy()
			markers[character] = nil
		end
	end
	for character in pairs(activeTargets) do
		if not markers[character] then
			markers[character] = createMarker(character)
		end
		updateMarker(markers[character], character)
	end
end

local function updateRadar()
	local root = getHumanoidRoot(player.Character)
	if not root then return end
	coordLabel.Text = string.format(
		"Coordinates: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)",
		root.Position.X, root.Position.Y, root.Position.Z
	)
	local forward = root.CFrame.LookVector
	playerPointer.Rotation = -math.deg(math.atan2(forward.X, forward.Z))
	refreshMarkers()
end

local scanDelay = 0
track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if not screenGui.Parent then return end
	scanDelay += deltaTime
	if scanDelay >= 0.15 then
		scanDelay = 0
		updateRadar()
	end
end))
updateRadar()
end

connections.initializeRadar()

resetAllSettings = function()
	setMainGuiTopmost(true)
	guiEffects.SetNoAnimation(false)
	setToggleVisual(guiEffects.NoAnimationKnob, false)
	setToggleVisual(guiEffects.HideCommandKnob, false)
	commandButtonState.SetHidden(false)
	mainGuiToggleKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.Insert
	waitingForMainGuiToggleKey = false
	updateMainGuiToggleKeyButton()
	connections.crHoverEnabled = false
	setToggleVisual(crHoverKnob, false)
	launcherAnimation.SetInfoHoverEnabled(false)
	connections.hiddenFling = false
	setToggleVisual(flingKnob, false)
	setEspEnabled(false)
	if connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled then
		connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled(false)
	end
	setAimAssistEnabled(false)
	if connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled then
		connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled(false)
	end
	if connections.setCombatHitboxScale then
		connections.setCombatHitboxScale(3)
	end
	if connections.setCombatHitboxTargetMode then
		connections.setCombatHitboxTargetMode("Head")
	end
	if connections.setCombatXrayEnabled then
		connections.setCombatXrayEnabled(false)
	end
	aimRunning = false
	aimTargetPlayer = nil
	setAimOption("TeamCheck", aimTeamKnob, false)
	setAimOption("AliveCheck", aimAliveKnob, false)
	setAimOption("WallCheck", aimWallKnob, false)
	setAimOption("FOVEnabled", aimFovKnob, false)
	aimSettings.LockPart = "HumanoidRootPart"
	aimLockPartLabel.Text = "Lock Part: " .. aimSettings.LockPart
	setAimFovRadius(90)
	connections.stopFly()
	connections.stopVFly()
	connections.stopNoclip()

	local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if connections.walkSpeedEnabled and humanoid then
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = connections.normalWalkSpeed
	end
	connections.walkSpeedEnabled = false
	connections.normalWalkSpeed = connections.defaultWalkSpeed
	setToggleVisual(walkSpeedKnob, false)
	setWalkSpeedValue(connections.defaultWalkSpeed)
	setFlySpeed(connections.defaultFlySpeed)
	setVFlySpeed(connections.defaultVFlySpeed)

	applyTheme("Default")
	sidebarHidden = false
	updateContentLayout()
end

local commandPaletteCommands = {
	{ Name = "walkspeed", Aliases = { "ws" }, Description = "Set walk speed: walkspeed/ws [num]", Run = function(value)
		local speed = tonumber(value)
		if not speed then return false, "Usage: walkspeed/ws [num]" end
		setWalkSpeedValue(speed)
		if not setWalkSpeedEnabled(true) then return false, "Character is not ready for walk speed." end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unwalkspeed", Aliases = { "unws" }, Description = "Disable custom walk speed", Run = function()
		if not setWalkSpeedEnabled(false) then return false, "Character is not ready." end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "fly", Description = "Set flight speed: fly [num]", Run = function(value)
		local speed = tonumber(value)
		if not speed then return false, "Usage: fly [num]" end
		setFlySpeed(speed)
		if not connections.flying then
			if connections.vFlying then connections.stopVFly() end
			connections.startFly()
		end
		if not connections.flying then return false, "Character is not ready for flight." end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unfly", Description = "Disable flight", Run = function()
		connections.stopFly()
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "vfly", Description = "Set vehicle flight speed: vfly [num]", Run = function(value)
		local speed = tonumber(value)
		if not speed then return false, "Usage: vfly [num]" end
		setVFlySpeed(speed)
		if not connections.vFlying then connections.startVFly() end
		if not connections.vFlying then return false, "Character is not ready for vehicle flight." end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unvfly", Description = "Disable vehicle flight", Run = function()
		connections.stopVFly()
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "noclip", Description = "Disable character collision", Run = function()
		if not connections.noclipping then connections.startNoclip() end
		if not connections.noclipping then return false, "Character is not ready for noclip." end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "clip", Description = "Restore character collision", Run = function()
		connections.stopNoclip()
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "aimassist", Aliases = { "aim" }, Description = "Enable aim assist", Run = function()
		setAimAssistEnabled(true)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unaim", Description = "Disable aim assist", Run = function()
		setAimAssistEnabled(false)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "esp", Description = "Enable player ESP", Run = function()
		setEspEnabled(true)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unesp", Description = "Disable player ESP", Run = function()
		setEspEnabled(false)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "esphitbox", Aliases = { "esphb" }, Description = "Enable ESP hitbox boxes", Run = function()
		if not connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled then return false, "ESP Hitbox is not ready." end
		connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled(true)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unesphitbox", Aliases = { "unesphb" }, Description = "Disable ESP hitbox boxes", Run = function()
		if not connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled then return false, "ESP Hitbox is not ready." end
		connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled(false)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "fov", Description = "Set and enable FOV: fov [num]", Run = function(value)
		local radius = tonumber(value)
		if not radius then return false, "Usage: fov [num]" end
		setAimFovRadius(radius)
		setAimOption("FOVEnabled", aimFovKnob, true)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unfov", Description = "Disable FOV", Run = function()
		setAimOption("FOVEnabled", aimFovKnob, false)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "hitbox", Description = "Set and enable hitbox: hitbox [num]", Run = function(value)
		local scale = tonumber(value)
		if not scale then return false, "Usage: hitbox [num]" end
		connections.setCombatHitboxScale(scale)
		connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled(true)
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unhitbox", Description = "Disable hitbox", Run = function()
		connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled(false)
		return true
	end },

		setAimOption("TeamCheck", aimTeamKnob, true)

		aimSettings.LockPart = partName
		aimLockPartLabel.Text = "Lock Part: " .. partName

		return true

	{ Name = "view", Description = "View a player: ;view [player]", Run = function(value)
		local query = value and value:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if not query or query == "" then return false, "Usage: ;view [player]" end
		local targetPlayer
		for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if candidate.Name:lower() == query or candidate.DisplayName:lower() == query then
				targetPlayer = candidate
				break
			end
		end
		if not targetPlayer then
			for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if candidate.Name:lower():sub(1, #query) == query
					or candidate.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #query) == query then
					targetPlayer = candidate
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if not targetPlayer then return false, "Player not found: " .. query end
		if targetPlayer == player then return false, "Cannot view your own character." end
		connections.viewPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if connections.viewedPlayer ~= targetPlayer then
			return false, "Could not start viewing " .. targetPlayer.Name .. "."
		end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "unview", Description = "Stop viewing the selected player", Run = function()
		connections.restoreViewedPlayer()
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "goto", Description = "Teleport to a player: ;goto [player]", Run = function(value)
		local query = value and value:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if not query or query == "" then return false, "Usage: ;goto [player]" end
		local targetPlayer
		for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if candidate.Name:lower() == query or candidate.DisplayName:lower() == query then
				targetPlayer = candidate
				break
			end
		end
		if not targetPlayer then
			for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if candidate.Name:lower():sub(1, #query) == query
					or candidate.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #query) == query then
					targetPlayer = candidate
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if not targetPlayer then return false, "Player not found: " .. query end
		local succeeded, message = teleportToPlayer(targetPlayer)
		if not succeeded then return false, message end
		return true
	end },
	{ Name = "killclient", Aliases = { "kill", "deletegui", "killgui" }, Description = "Delete the main GUI and command bar", Run = function()
		if not cleanupGui then return false, "GUI cleanup is not ready." end
		local succeeded, cleanupError = pcall(cleanupGui)
		if screenGui.Parent then screenGui:Destroy() end
		if launcherGui.Parent then launcherGui:Destroy() end
		if not succeeded then
			warn("GUI cleanup failed: " .. tostring(cleanupError))
			return false, "GUI cleanup failed: " .. tostring(cleanupError)
		end
		return true
	end },

		connections.openTerminalFromCommand()
		return true

local commandHintButtons = {}
local commandHintIndicators = {}
local selectedCommandHintName
local function updateCommandHintIndicators(hoveredButton)
	for _, hintButton in ipairs(commandHintButtons) do
		local indicator = commandHintIndicators[hintButton]
		local isSelected = hintButton:GetAttribute("CommandName") == selectedCommandHintName
		local isHovered = hintButton == hoveredButton
		indicator.Visible = isSelected or isHovered
		indicator.TextTransparency = isSelected and 0.53 or (isHovered and 0.75 or 1)
	end
end

local function refreshCommandHints()
	local query = commandPaletteInput.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
	query = query:gsub("^;", "")
	query = query:match("^(%S*)") or ""
	if query == "" then
		commandHints.Visible = false
		return
	end
	local searchQuery = query:match("^(.-)%s+[^%s]*$") or query
	local matches = {}
	for _, command in ipairs(commandPaletteCommands) do
		local matched = command.Name:lower():sub(1, #searchQuery) == searchQuery
		for _, alias in ipairs(command.Aliases or {}) do
			matched = matched or alias:lower():sub(1, #searchQuery) == searchQuery
		end
		if matched then
			table.insert(matches, command)
			if #matches == 5 then break end
		end
	end
	if #matches == 0 then
		commandHints.Visible = false
		return
	end
	for index, hintButton in ipairs(commandHintButtons) do
		local command = matches[index]
		hintButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (index - 1) * 30)
		hintButton.Visible = command ~= nil
		if command then
			hintButton.Text = ";" .. command.Name .. "  -  " .. command.Description
			hintButton:SetAttribute("CommandName", command.Name)
		end
	end
	local contentHeight = #matches * 30 - 2
	commandHintsList.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, contentHeight)
	commandHintsList.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	commandHints.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
		commandPaletteOpenWidth,
		math.min(contentHeight, math.max(120, screenGui.AbsoluteSize.Y * 0.45))
	)
	commandHints.Visible = true
end

local commandPaletteAnimation = { Token = 0, Tween = nil }
local commandListAnimation = { Token = 0, Tween = nil }
local commandPaletteOutsideClickCount = 0

local function openCommandPalette()
	commandPaletteAnimation.Token += 1
	if commandPaletteAnimation.Tween then
		commandPaletteAnimation.Tween:Cancel()
	end
	commandButtonState.PaletteOpen = true
	commandPaletteButton.Visible = false
	commandPalette.Visible = true
	commandPaletteOutsideClickCount = 0
	commandPaletteOpenWidth = math.min(380, math.max(260, screenGui.AbsoluteSize.X - 32))
	commandPaletteSurface.Position = commandPaletteButton.Position
	commandPaletteSurface.Size = commandPaletteButton.Size
	commandPaletteSurface.BackgroundTransparency = commandPaletteButton.BackgroundTransparency
	commandPaletteSurfaceStroke.Transparency = 0.8
	commandHints.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, commandPaletteHeight + 46)
	commandHints.Visible = false
	commandPaletteAnimation.Tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandPaletteSurface,
		TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(commandPaletteOpenWidth, commandPaletteHeight),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 42),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
		}
	)
	guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandPaletteSurfaceStroke,
		TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{ Transparency = 0.12 }
	):Play()
	commandPaletteAnimation.Tween:Play()
end

local function openCommandListWindow()
	commandListAnimation.Token += 1
	if commandListAnimation.Tween then
		commandListAnimation.Tween:Cancel()
	end
	commandListWindow.Visible = true
	commandListWindow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
	commandListAnimation.Tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandListWindow,
		TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{ Size = commandListOpenSize }
	)
	commandListAnimation.Tween:Play()
end

local function closeCommandListWindow()
	if not commandListWindow.Visible then return end
	commandListAnimation.Token += 1
	local token = commandListAnimation.Token
	if commandListAnimation.Tween then
		commandListAnimation.Tween:Cancel()
	end
	commandListAnimation.Tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandListWindow,
		TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
		{ Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }
	)
	track(commandListAnimation.Tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
		if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed
			and token == commandListAnimation.Token then
			commandListWindow.Visible = false
			commandListWindow.Size = commandListOpenSize
		end
	end))
	commandListAnimation.Tween:Play()
end

local function closeCommandPalette()
	if not commandPalette.Visible then return end
	commandPaletteOutsideClickCount = 0
	commandPaletteAnimation.Token += 1
	local token = commandPaletteAnimation.Token
	if commandPaletteAnimation.Tween then
		commandPaletteAnimation.Tween:Cancel()
	end
	commandPaletteButton.Visible = false
	commandPaletteInput:ReleaseFocus()
	commandPaletteInput.Text = ""
	commandHints.Visible = false
	commandPaletteAnimation.Tween = guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandPaletteSurface,
		TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
		{
			Size = commandPaletteButton.Size,
			Position = commandPaletteButton.Position,
			BackgroundTransparency = commandPaletteButton.BackgroundTransparency,
		}
	)
	guiEffects.CreateTween(
		commandPaletteSurfaceStroke,
		TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
		{ Transparency = 0.8 }
	):Play()
	track(commandPaletteAnimation.Tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
		if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed
			and token == commandPaletteAnimation.Token then
			commandButtonState.PaletteOpen = false
			commandPalette.Visible = false
			commandButtonState.SetHidden(commandButtonState.Hidden)
		end
	end))
	commandPaletteAnimation.Tween:Play()
end

connections.openCommandPalette = function()
	if commandPalette.Visible then
		closeCommandPalette()
		return
	end
	openCommandPalette()
	commandPaletteInput:CaptureFocus()
	commandPaletteInput.CursorPosition = #commandPaletteInput.Text + 1
end

track(commandListButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	closeCommandPalette()
	openCommandListWindow()
end))

track(commandPaletteClose.Activated:Connect(closeCommandPalette))

for commandIndex, command in ipairs(commandPaletteCommands) do
	local entryButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	entryButton.Name = command.Name:gsub("%W", "") .. "Command"
	entryButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 42)
	entryButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 14)
	entryButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
	entryButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	entryButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	entryButton.Text = ""
	entryButton.LayoutOrder = commandIndex
	entryButton.ZIndex = 122
	entryButton.Parent = commandPaletteList
	round(entryButton, 6)
	applyGlass(entryButton, 0.86)

	local commandEntryIndicator = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	commandEntryIndicator.Name = "SelectedIndicator"
	commandEntryIndicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 42)
	commandEntryIndicator.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0)
	commandEntryIndicator.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	commandEntryIndicator.Text = ">"
	commandEntryIndicator.TextColor3 = colors.accent
	commandEntryIndicator.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	commandEntryIndicator.TextSize = 13
	commandEntryIndicator.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	commandEntryIndicator.Visible = false
	commandEntryIndicator.ZIndex = 124
	commandEntryIndicator.Parent = entryButton

	local commandNameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	commandNameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.38, -26, 1, 0)
	commandNameLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(25, 0)
	commandNameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	commandNameLabel.Text = command.Name
	if command.Aliases then
		commandNameLabel.Text ..= " / " .. table.concat(command.Aliases, ", ")
	end
	commandNameLabel.TextColor3 = colors.text
	commandNameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	commandNameLabel.TextSize = 11
	commandNameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	commandNameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	commandNameLabel.ZIndex = 123
	commandNameLabel.Parent = entryButton

	local commandDescriptionLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	commandDescriptionLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.62, -8, 1, 0)
	commandDescriptionLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.38, 0, 0, 0)
	commandDescriptionLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	commandDescriptionLabel.Text = command.Description
	commandDescriptionLabel.TextColor3 = colors.muted
	commandDescriptionLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	commandDescriptionLabel.TextSize = 9
	commandDescriptionLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	commandDescriptionLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	commandDescriptionLabel.ZIndex = 123
	commandDescriptionLabel.Parent = entryButton

	local entry = {
		Command = command,
		Button = entryButton,
		Indicator = commandEntryIndicator,
	}
	table.insert(commandPaletteEntries, entry)
	track(entryButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		selectedCommandEntry = entry
		for _, commandEntry in ipairs(commandPaletteEntries) do
			commandEntry.Indicator.Visible = commandEntry == selectedCommandEntry
		end
		commandPaletteInput.Text = ";" .. command.Name .. " "
		closeCommandListWindow()
		openCommandPalette()
		commandPaletteInput:CaptureFocus()
		commandPaletteInput.CursorPosition = #commandPaletteInput.Text + 1
	end))
	track(entryButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		entryButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
		commandEntryIndicator.Visible = true
	end))
	track(entryButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		entryButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
		commandEntryIndicator.Visible = selectedCommandEntry == entry
	end))
end
