end

track(commandPaletteInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	refreshCommandHints()
end))

local function isPointInside(guiObject, point)
	if not guiObject.Visible then return false end
	local position = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
	local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize
	return point.X >= position.X
		and point.X <= position.X + size.X
		and point.Y >= position.Y
		and point.Y <= position.Y + size.Y
end

local commandListDragging = false
local commandListDragInput
local commandListDragStart
local commandListPositionStart
track(commandListTopbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		return
	end
	if isPointInside(commandListClose, input.Position) then return end
	commandListDragging = true
	commandListDragInput = input
	commandListDragStart = input.Position
	commandListPositionStart = commandListWindow.Position
end))
track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	local draggingPointer = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		or (commandListDragInput and input == commandListDragInput)
	if not commandListDragging or not draggingPointer then return end
	local delta = input.Position - commandListDragStart
	commandListWindow.Position = UDim2.new(
		commandListPositionStart.X.Scale,
		commandListPositionStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
		commandListPositionStart.Y.Scale,
		commandListPositionStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
	)
end))
track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		commandListDragging = false
		commandListDragInput = nil
	end
end))
track(commandListClose.Activated:Connect(function()
	closeCommandListWindow()
end))
local commandListMatrixElapsed = 0
track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if guiEffects.NoAnimation then return end
	if commandPalette.Visible then
		local barHeight = commandPaletteSurface.AbsoluteSize.Y
		for _, stream in ipairs(commandBarStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.18 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
					commandBarRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > barHeight then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
				stream.Speed = commandBarRandom:NextNumber(20, 42)
				for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
					digit.Text = commandBarRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if commandListWindow.Visible then
		commandListMatrixElapsed += deltaTime
		local step = commandListMatrixElapsed
		commandListMatrixElapsed = 0
		local layerHeight = commandListMatrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
		for _, stream in ipairs(commandListStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * step
			stream.RefreshElapsed += step
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.2 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
					commandListRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > layerHeight then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
				stream.Speed = commandListRandom:NextNumber(32, 70)
				for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
					digit.Text = commandListRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if commandHints.Visible then
		local hintsHeight = commandHintsMatrixLayer.AbsoluteSize.Y
		for _, stream in ipairs(commandHintsStreams) do
			stream.Y += stream.Speed * deltaTime
			stream.RefreshElapsed += deltaTime
			stream.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(stream.Frame.Position.X.Offset, stream.Y)
			if stream.RefreshElapsed >= 0.18 then
				stream.RefreshElapsed = 0
				stream.Digits[stream.RefreshIndex].Text =
					commandHintsRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				stream.RefreshIndex = stream.RefreshIndex % #stream.Digits + 1
			end
			if stream.Y > hintsHeight then
				stream.Y = -stream.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
				stream.Speed = commandHintsRandom:NextNumber(24, 48)
				for _, digit in ipairs(stream.Digits) do
					digit.Text = commandHintsRandom:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and "0" or "1"
				end
			end
		end
	end
end))

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if not commandPalette.Visible then return end
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		return
	end
	if isPointInside(commandPaletteSurface, input.Position)
		or isPointInside(commandHints, input.Position) then
		commandPaletteOutsideClickCount = 0
		return
	end
	commandPaletteOutsideClickCount += 1
	if commandPaletteOutsideClickCount >= 3 then
		commandPaletteOutsideClickCount = 0
		closeCommandPalette()
	end
end))

local function notifyCommandResult(succeeded, commandName, detail)
	local status = succeeded == nil and "RUNNING" or (succeeded and "SUCCESS" or "FAILED")
	local noticeCreated, setNoticeText, destroyNotice = pcall(
		createOrbitLoadingNotice,
		"COMMAND " .. status,
		"top"
	)
	if not noticeCreated then
		warn("Failed to show command notification: " .. tostring(setNoticeText))
		return function() end
	end
	local function updateNotice(text)
		local updated, updateError = pcall(setNoticeText, text)
		if not updated then
			warn("Failed to update command notification: " .. tostring(updateError))
		end
	end
	updateNotice(status .. " | " .. commandName .. "\n" .. detail)
	local function finish(finalSucceeded, finalDetail)
		local finalStatus = finalSucceeded and "SUCCESS" or "FAILED"
		updateNotice(finalStatus .. " | " .. commandName .. "\n" .. finalDetail)
		task.delay(3.2, function()
			local destroyed, destroyError = pcall(destroyNotice)
			if not destroyed then
				warn("Failed to close command notification: " .. tostring(destroyError))
			end
		end)
	end
	if succeeded ~= nil then
		task.delay(3.2, function()
			local destroyed, destroyError = pcall(destroyNotice)
			if not destroyed then
				warn("Failed to close command notification: " .. tostring(destroyError))
			end
		end)
	end
	return finish
end

local commandExecutionBusy = false
local function executeCommandPaletteInput(submittedText)
	if commandExecutionBusy then return end
	commandExecutionBusy = true
	local inputText = (submittedText or commandPaletteInput.Text):lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
	commandPaletteInput.Text = ""
	selectedCommandHintName = nil
	updateCommandHintIndicators()
	selectedCommandEntry = nil
	for _, commandEntry in ipairs(commandPaletteEntries) do
		commandEntry.Indicator.Visible = false
	end
	if inputText:sub(1, 1) ~= ";" then
		notifyCommandResult(false, "Command", "Commands must start with ';' (example: ;ws 16).")
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end
	inputText = inputText:sub(2):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
	if inputText == "" then
		notifyCommandResult(false, "Command", "Type a command after ';'.")
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end

	local command
	local argument
	local matchedName
	local commandNames = {}
	for _, candidate in ipairs(commandPaletteCommands) do
		table.insert(commandNames, { Name = candidate.Name, Command = candidate })
		for _, alias in ipairs(candidate.Aliases or {}) do
			table.insert(commandNames, { Name = alias, Command = candidate })
		end
	end
	table.sort(commandNames, function(left, right)
		return #left.Name > #right.Name
	end)
	for _, candidate in ipairs(commandNames) do
		local prefix = candidate.Name:lower()
		if inputText == prefix or inputText:sub(1, #prefix + 1) == prefix .. " " then
			command = candidate.Command
			matchedName = candidate.Name
			argument = inputText:sub(#prefix + 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			break
		end
	end
	if not command then
		notifyCommandResult(false, "Command", "Unknown command: " .. inputText)
		warn("Command palette received an unknown command: " .. inputText)
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end

	local finishNotification = notifyCommandResult(nil, matchedName, "Executing command...")
	RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
	local success, result, message = pcall(command.Run, argument)
	if connections.cleanupStarted then
		if not success then
			warn("Command palette cleanup failed for " .. matchedName .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		end
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end
	if not success then
		finishNotification(false, tostring(result))
		warn("Command palette failed for " .. matchedName .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end
	if result == false then
		local failureMessage = message or ("Command could not run: " .. matchedName)
		finishNotification(false, failureMessage)
		commandExecutionBusy = false
		return
	end

	finishNotification(true, "Command ran successfully.")
	commandExecutionBusy = false
	closeCommandPalette()
end

track(commandPaletteInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
	if enterPressed then
		executeCommandPaletteInput(commandPaletteInput.Text)
	end
end))

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Return and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then
		return
	end
	if commandPalette.Visible and commandPaletteInput:IsFocused() then
		local submittedText = commandPaletteInput.Text
		task.defer(function()
			executeCommandPaletteInput(submittedText)
		end)
	end
end))

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if commandPalette.Visible and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
		closeCommandPalette()
		return
	end
	if commandListWindow.Visible and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
		closeCommandListWindow()
		return
	end
end))

local commandPaletteToggleActionName = "AaCommandPaletteToggle"
ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
	commandPaletteToggleActionName,
	function(_, inputState)
		if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end

		openCommandPalette()
		commandPaletteInput.Text = ""
		task.defer(function()
			if commandPalette.Visible then
				commandPaletteInput:CaptureFocus()
				commandPaletteInput.Text = ""
				commandPaletteInput.CursorPosition = 1
			end
		end)
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end,
	false,
	Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 100,
	Enum.KeyCode.Semicolon
)
connections.cleanupCommandPaletteAction = function()
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction(commandPaletteToggleActionName)
end
end)()

connections.cleanupStarted = false
cleanupGui = function()
	if connections.cleanupStarted then return end
	connections.cleanupStarted = true
	local function cleanupStep(description, callback)
		local succeeded, cleanupError = pcall(callback)
		if not succeeded then
			warn(description .. ": " .. tostring(cleanupError))
		end
	end

	getgenv().AaGeneration = (getgenv().AaGeneration or 0) + 1
	if connections.cancelPendingTerminalLoad then
		cleanupStep("Failed to cancel terminal loading", connections.cancelPendingTerminalLoad)
	end
	if connections.cancelPendingOrbitLoad then
		cleanupStep("Failed to cancel Orbit loading", connections.cancelPendingOrbitLoad)
	end
	cleanupStep("Failed to unbind terminal completion", function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction(terminalCompletionActionName)
	end)
	if connections.cleanupCommandPaletteAction then
		cleanupStep("Failed to unbind command palette", connections.cleanupCommandPaletteAction)
		connections.cleanupCommandPaletteAction = nil
	end
	if resetAllSettings then
		cleanupStep("Failed to reset GUI features", resetAllSettings)
	end

	cleanupStep("Failed to disable fling", function()
		if connections.setFlingEnabled then
			connections.setFlingEnabled(false)
		end
		connections.hiddenFling = false
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop aim assist", function()
		setAimAssistEnabled(false)
		aimRunning = false
		aimTargetPlayer = nil
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop ESP", function()
		setEspEnabled(false)
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop ESP Hitbox", function()
		if connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled then
			connections.setCombatEspHitboxEnabled(false)
		end
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to disable hitbox", function()
		if connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled then
			connections.setCombatHitboxEnabled(false)
		end
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to disable X-ray", function()
		if connections.setCombatXrayEnabled then
			connections.setCombatXrayEnabled(false)
		end
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to restore walk speed", function()
		local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if connections.walkSpeedEnabled and humanoid then
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = connections.normalWalkSpeed
		end
		connections.walkSpeedEnabled = false
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop flight", connections.stopFly)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop vehicle flight", connections.stopVFly)
	cleanupStep("Failed to stop noclip", connections.stopNoclip)
	cleanupStep("Failed to close Orbit Tools", function()
		local stopOrbit = getgenv().OrbitToolsyStop
		if type(stopOrbit) == "function" then
			stopOrbit()
		else
			getgenv().OrbitToolsy = false
			getgenv().OrbitToolsyReady = false
			local orbitGui = getgenv().OrbitUI
			if orbitGui and orbitGui.Parent then
				orbitGui:Destroy()
			end
			getgenv().OrbitUI = nil
		end
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to disable terminal GUI", function()
		terminalScreenGui.Enabled = false
		terminalScreenGui:Destroy()
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to restore viewed camera", connections.restoreViewedPlayer)
	cleanupStep("Failed to remove player cards", function()
		for targetPlayer in pairs(playerCards) do
			removePlayerCard(targetPlayer)
		end
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to cancel background tween", function()
		backgroundTween:Cancel()
	end)
	if connections.terminalGradientTween then
		cleanupStep("Failed to cancel terminal gradient tween", function()
			connections.terminalGradientTween:Cancel()
		end)
	end
	cleanupStep("Failed to cancel launcher intro", launcherAnimation.CancelIntro)
	if logoIntroTween then cleanupStep("Failed to cancel logo intro", function() logoIntroTween:Cancel() end) end
	if logoRingTween then cleanupStep("Failed to cancel logo ring tween", function() logoRingTween:Cancel() end) end
	if logoHoverTween then cleanupStep("Failed to cancel logo hover tween", function() logoHoverTween:Cancel() end) end
	connections.hiddenFling = false
	cleanupStep("Failed to invalidate explorer source", connections.invalidateExplorerSource)
	if explorerSourceInputConnection then
		cleanupStep("Failed to disconnect explorer input", function()
			explorerSourceInputConnection:Disconnect()
		end)
		explorerSourceInputConnection = nil
	end
	if explorerSourceMatrixConnection then
		cleanupStep("Failed to disconnect explorer matrix", function()
			explorerSourceMatrixConnection:Disconnect()
		end)
		explorerSourceMatrixConnection = nil
	end
	if explorerSourceGui then
		cleanupStep("Failed to destroy explorer GUI", function()
			explorerSourceGui:Destroy()
		end)
		explorerSourceGui = nil
	end
	if connections.characterConnection then
		cleanupStep("Failed to disconnect character handler", function()
			connections.characterConnection:Disconnect()
		end)
		connections.characterConnection = nil
	end
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
		cleanupStep("Failed to disconnect GUI handler", function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	table.clear(connections)
	connections.cleanupStarted = true
	cleanupStep("Failed to destroy main GUI", function()
		screenGui:Destroy()
	end)
	cleanupStep("Failed to destroy launcher GUI", function()
		launcherGui:Destroy()
	end)
end

getgenv().AaShutdown = function()
	cleanupGui()
end

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessedEvent)
	if waitingForMainGuiToggleKey then
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
			waitingForMainGuiToggleKey = false
			updateMainGuiToggleKeyButton()
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
			mainGuiToggleKeyCode = input.KeyCode
			waitingForMainGuiToggleKey = false
			updateMainGuiToggleKeyButton()
		end
		return
	end

	if not gameProcessedEvent
		and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
		and input.KeyCode == mainGuiToggleKeyCode
		and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
		and not connections.cleanupStarted then
		setMainFrameOpen(not mainFrameAnimation.Open)
	end
end))

connections.characterConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
	connections.stopFly()
	connections.stopVFly()
	connections.stopNoclip()
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid:IsA("Humanoid") then
		connections.normalWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
		if connections.walkSpeedEnabled then
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = connections.walkSpeed
		end
	end
	
end)

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessedEvent)
	if not gameProcessedEvent and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
		cleanupGui()
	end
end))

showPage(activePage)
launcherGui.Enabled = true
logoButton.Visible = true
end
